Chapter 2551: Final Battle in 48 Hours!

For a moment, Jin Yuyan's heart constricted. In a trance, he truly thought that Li Yao was an authentic Immortal Cultivator—and a most brutal and cunning one.

Observing more carefully, he finally discovered that the patterns of broken bones, chopped limbs, and flowing guts on Li Yao's black robe that seemed to be weaved with infinite unrested souls were vaguely wriggling like thousands of living bugs.

Those patterns were alive!

The deformed corpses and the devastated living people congregated into a vortex of horror that attracted Jin Yuyan's soul deep into it. The director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors could almost hear people moaning next to his ears, "We're so miserable… We're so miserable…"

That was too… perverted!

Jin Yuyan couldn't help but shiver hard.

Although he was an Immortal Cultivator, he preferred to be a gentle, peaceful businessman in general, instead of a murderer who had fun in killing and torturing. He was also deeply ashamed of such black sheep among the Immortal Cultivators.

Intuitively, Jin Yuyan stepped back and asked warily, "You…"

"Director Jin—"

Holding his tone, Li Yao continued horrifyingly and unpredictably. Half way through his sentence, he finally realized that his voice was not right. Coughing hard, he resumed his normal tone and waved his hands to turn off the black mist, the fiendish air, and the other messy sound and visual effects, revealing the original look of the clean and sunny lobby. Then, he took off the creepy black robe on his body and changed into a sloppy T-shirt and short pants, before he apologized, "Please do excuse me, I was too devoted to my exercise a moment ago. I hope I did not scare you."

Dumbfounded, Jin Yuyan watched Li Yao transform from a disturbing dark supervillain into a sunny plain young man all of a sudden. He was stunned for a long time before he finally asked, "What's your exercise for?"

"To play the role of 'Black Wind King' better!"

Li Yao said matter-of-factly, "I'll meet the upper class of the Imperium and hang around with the expert Immortal Cultivators as the 'Black Wind King'. Naturally, I need to practice hard and not expose any flaws.

"Therefore, according to my own understanding, I have interpreted the character of 'Black Wind King' from twelve different angles. Based on his experiences and his rise, I think that the character should be ambitious, blatant, unrefined, slightly cunning, and slightly self-deprecating.

"The slight sense of inferiority, in particular, is the core of the character. After all, he has a humble background and only rose miraculously by accident. He does not have many supporters, and he is in a hurry to be famous in the Imperium. So, he will act recklessly and even participate in the most insane adventures. This super creepy black robe is also a symbol of his brutality and dominance. However, it also shows that he is somewhat impatient and easy to be manipulated by other people.

"How about it, Old Jin? Do you think the character I've created is reasonable enough to fool them? Aya, I've never been an Immortal Cultivator before, and I'm just a beginner. Please criticize and enlighten me if you have any insight."

"You are a beginner?"

Looking at Li Yao's sincere and open-minded face, Jin Yuyan couldn't help but feel an overwhelming sense of powerlessness again. He said with a bitter smile, "I'm truly confused about who you are now, Vulture Li Yao. Are you a Cultivator or an Immortal Cultivator, the Black Wind King, or the special representative of the government-in-exile of the Star Ocean Republic? Are you here to renew the Imperium or to destroy it?

"Perhaps Qi Yuanbao's guess was correct. The Black Wind Fleet did fail to destroy Firefly and was swallowed by Firefly instead. While I cannot think of a single accident that could've caused the destruction of the Black Wind Fleet, that is the only reasonable explanation!"

"Maybe, but it is all the same no matter who swallows whom, isn't it?"

Li Yao opened his hands and sat on the sofa. His legs folded, he smiled. "Cultivator, Immortal Cultivator, Black Wind King, special representative of the Star Ocean Republic—are they any different? When I go shopping in your place, will I enjoy discounts if I am an Immortal Cultivator and be charged twice as much if I am a Cultivator?

"All in all, I have proved my unparalleled capabilities. If I really swallowed the Black Wind Fleet, it would only mean that the strength at my disposal was higher than that of the Black Wind Fleet. Also, there are giant markets and enormous business opportunities at the edge of the cosmos. Those things are certainly not lies, are they?

"With that in mind, our cooperation will only bring you greater profits, and it is even more secure than before. Both of us will benefit from it. Why do you need to bother with the insignificant details?"

Jin Yuyan sighed and said, "I thought so too. That's why I've come to talk to you alone. Otherwise, I would've sent a troop to surround this place."

"It would've been the dumbest decision of your life."

Li Yao said casually, "Do you really think that a couple of guys in the Divinity Transformation Stage and thousands of regular soldiers are enough to stop me? Hehe, when I am determined to leave, the capital cannot stop me, and the Seven Seas Grand Market cannot stop me; Blackstar the Great cannot stop me, and the greedy dealers and merchants like you certainly cannot stop me either!"

Sensing the powerful vibe and dominance spurting from Li Yao, Jin Yuyan was somewhat lost again.

But out of his expectation, Li Yao chuckled again and wiped his face. "How about it, Old Jin? My acting skills are not bad, right? Not that I'm bragging, but my face is exactly what I count on while I travel in the sea of stars!"

Jin Yuyan was silent for a long time before he took a deep breath and said, "Enough. I don't care who you are, because we have a mutual enemy at hand. Whatever schemes and conspiracies we may have against each other, let's wait until the battle of Seven Seas is won. Do you agree?"

"I can't agree more!"

Li Yao said, "Let's get down to business. How are your talks with the warlords and the wavering guys within the four families? Do you know the date of the jump of the four families' coalition fleet?"

"I have good news and bad news. The good news is that my negotiations with those people are rather promising. Almost everybody signed a new secret agreement with us to strike a solid alliance."

Jin Yuyan paused for a moment and further explained, "Before, while we had some secret agreements, they did not leave their soul signatures. The details were ambiguous, and their attitudes were unclear too. They might shift their side anytime.

"However, I have pointed it out to the guys who are sitting on the fence that the distribution of interests after the victory, including the development rights of the Sectors at the edge of the cosmos and the market share in the barbaric worlds, will depend on the performance in the battle.

"Also, the arrival of such a powerful ally as the Black Wind Fleet strengthened the confidence of a lot of people in our army. Those who intended to earn more in the game had betted all their stakes on our side.

"This is exactly a snowballing chain reaction. As long as somebody takes the lead, more and more people will follow. By the time more than half of the people are on our side, the remainder of them will have no choice except to follow too.

"All in all, most of the warlords, Sector Masters, and middle class in the four families have left soul signatures and abundant military secrets as a gesture of good faith. There will be severe consequences if the leaders of the four families discover those things. Therefore, it is a sign that they have made up their mind to work for us.

"However, combining the intelligence from everybody, we have the bad news—the four families' coalition fleet, which boasted an unprecedented number of soldiers, has been gathered and will launch a general attack within 48 hours. It is said that a million starships will jump to the Seven Seas Space Zone at the same time. However strong our defense system is, it will be crushed immediately!"

"A million starships?"

Li Yao scorned, "That's bulls*it!"

Li Yao was sort of a battle-tested general himself. Naturally, he knew that the bragging in space battles was no different from the ancient wars tens of thousands of years ago.

In the ancient wars, the dynasties which were still in the feudal phase could launch an expedition with a million soldiers now and then. How was it possible, considering the technological and organizational abilities at that time?

As a matter of fact, the "million soldiers" were at least twice exaggerated, and more than half of the real army was made of peasants and supply teams. It would be remarkable if the capable soldiers could amount to a hundred thousand. Then, even among the capable soldiers, the elite, fearless ones who could fight relentlessly usually only took up ten to twenty percent.

In other words, an army that claimed to have a million soldiers was already remarkable enough if it had thirty to fifty thousand truly capable warriors.

In the space age, although technologies were much more advanced, the battlefield and the supply lines had been much more stretched out too. Considering the lack of unity among the four families, it was impossible for them to mobilize enough resources and soldiers for a great battle of a million warships in such a haste.

A million warships and a million starships were absolutely different.

Li Yao estimated that, counting all the supply ships, the barges, and even the escape capsules, it was possible for them to aggregate to a million starships, but the warships among them couldn't have been more than three hundred thousand.

Of the three hundred thousand warships, most belonged to the warlords and the Sector Masters of the peripheral worlds, who had been flirting with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors at this moment.

Considering the situation today, those people would rather be loyal to the four Kurfürsten if they did not defect. Unless there was an accident, they would not attack the Seven Seas Grand Market for real.

Even in the fleets of the four families themselves, countless captains, advisors, and even commanders were hesitating about whether or not they should overthrow the old men in their houses too.

Therefore, the situation was not as tough as it appeared, and their odds of winning were still rather high.

Jin Yuyan thought the same.

Although the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was not one-tenth as strong as the army of the four families on paper, ninety percent of the army belonged to the warships and the local magnates. As long as they were given enough interests and shown the hope of victory, they could carry out their secret agreement and betray anytime.

The real foundation of the four families' army was the eight fleets that were mobilized from the dominions of the families, which was also known as the Eight Adamantines.

Chapter 2552: Adamantine-Level Super Arsenal Warships!

It had been a while since Li Yao came to the Imperium, and he had a profound understanding of the warships of the Imperium with which the Imperium dominated the universe. He knew that there was a brief period where the giant cannons and enormous warships were revived in the military of the Imperium after three hundred years ago.

Supposedly, when the crystal suits and the Colossi were designed and maintained better and better, the combat abilities of the single soldiers were higher and higher, and the warships were more and more transformed into combinations of firepower output platforms and comprehensive supply platforms, instead of the units leading the charge.

When a hundred experts broke into a hostile starship armed in crystal suits to their teeth, it would be possible for them to destroy a starship that cost billions of dollars to be built. Even if they couldn't, the greatest loss on their side would be the death of the hundred experts, who would've killed a lot of enemies before they were killed.

Considering the cost-efficiency, it was apparently not a bargain to spend military budgets on building larger starships. Devoting the resources to the construction of crystal suit legions and the maintenance and duplication of Colossi was a better option.

However, as the technologies about the crystal suits and the Colossi progressed, so did the firepower output, the spiritual shield, and the unmanned operation of the starships. Giant cannons and enormous warships were always every soldier's dream, so somebody eventually proposed that, when the technologies of the crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus were mostly developed, battle puppets, drones in various forms, and space torpedoes could gather into an overwhelming tide. Plus cannons that were large enough in number, they could destroy all the enemies, including the most powerful crystal suits and Colossi!

"The Colossi are absolutely not invincible. As long as there are enough battle puppets and drones, and the firepower output from the cannons is high enough to establish a continuous, impenetrable fire net, it will be easy to crush the Colossi!

"If you can't, that means the number of the battle puppets and cannons is not large enough, and the fire net is not dense enough. If a hundred thousand cannons are piled on a single starship, what enemy can't they destroy?"

It was exactly the opinion of the people who believed in cannons and warships.

Also, they were not just talking but also conducted decades of theoretical calculations and tests in the most authoritative military lab of the Imperium, proving their point in countless tests on the virtual battlefield. The "super arsenal warships", equipped with hundreds of thousands of spiritual puppets and drones as well as at least ten thousand heavy cannons, were truly capable of causing great trouble to the general Colossi. Even if the specifications of the Colossi were improved to the level of what was used by the Divinity Transformation Stage experts, it was still barely possible for them to break out of the blockage of the "super arsenal warship".

The military of the Imperium couldn't help but feel tempted.

After all, the technologies on the development of the "spiritual residence", which was the central cockpit of the Colossus, hadn't been grasped by the Imperium yet. Even though they could duplicate or transplant the Colossus' limbs or weapon kits, it would be a total loss once the "spiritual residence" was blown up.

There was no telling how long the war between the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance would last. If it was going on for another thousand years, the Colossi on the Imperium's side would be fewer and fewer, whereas the Covenant Alliance, with their abundant primeval heritage, clearly had the technologies of Colossal maintenance and duplication that were more advanced than the Imperium's.

If at the end of the war, the Imperium ran out of the Colossi but the Covenant Alliance still had some left, it would certainly be a very awkward situation.

It was exactly because of such concerns and the "perfect performance" in the virtual wars in the lab that the military of the Imperium activated the plan to build new super arsenal warships known as "Adamantine"!

The Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships were all ten times larger than the biggest arsenal warships that the Imperium had ever built in the past. As a matter of fact, they were more like combinations of arsenal warships, comprehensive supply starships, and even space fortresses.

In the original design, the military stuffed at least thirty thousand cannons of various sorts, a hundred most advanced fire-control crystal processors, and more than five hundred thousand battle puppets and drones into each Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships so that they would become the "perfect and doom-bringing ultimate warships that can end all wars"!

Would the actual combat ability of the Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships be as incredible as the designers and contractors bragged about, and could they defeat the Colossi?

The question would forever remain a mystery.

Perhaps if those Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships had been built according to the highest standard, it was true that they would be blowing up Colossi unstoppably and end all wars.

But before that, what they ended first would be the finance of the Imperium.

The whole construction of the Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships was a sad yet cliché story.

Perhaps the ideas that firepower mattered most and an ocean of drones was invincible were not wrong, but the designers' creations were too avant-garde and magnificent. They overestimated the development speed of the crystal processors in the Imperium and underestimated the technological difficulty to focus hundreds of thousands of cannons on one starship. Or rather, to get the Council of Elders to approve the construction plan of the super warships as soon as possible, from the designers to the few largest military contractors in the Imperium to the persons of interests within the Council of Elders, everybody showed the most intense optimism and turned a blind eye to the "minor difficulties". They proposed a budget plan that was not at all cheap but still bearable if everybody gritted their teeth, as well as a promise that the warships would be ready for battle within twenty years.

The Council of Elders at that time approved the plan. Sixteen "Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships" were built in the dock at the same time, and the construction lasted… a hundred years.

During the construction, not only did many natural and humane accidents take place because of technological challenges, but the advancement of the technologies of crystal processors and battle puppets also forced the designs to be updated again and again. The original plan and budget were soon thrown into the dustbin in the corner, and the new estimated fees of construction were higher and higher until the cost for each Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warship was twenty times more than the original budget!

However "martial" the Imperium of True Human Beings was and however the Council of Elders looked forward to the birth of the super warships, they were collapsing when faced with such budgets and such inestimable time of completion.

"The Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships are truly the most magnificent warships in history, but they are actually on the Covenant Alliance's side. They are about to destroy the Imperium by doing nothing and just lying in the docks!"

Many elders of the Imperium bashed the desk and roared.

However, they couldn't do anything else other than roaring. Astronomical resources had been devoted to the project. It was not like they could sell the sixteen incomplete Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships as garbage, right?

Besides, the astronomical investment of resources would not just vanish. It would only flow back to the persons of interest in the end.

Even the angry elders who were bashing the desk and roaring might have secretly benefited from the project too!

Just like that, the construction plan of the Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships became a ceaseless black hole that exhausted the army of the Imperium. It was hard to say that the Covenant Alliance's march into the Imperium and occupation of the five heavy industrial worlds had nothing to do with the weakening caused by the plan. Furthermore, the Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships even contributed to the serious economic crisis after the war!

After the Covenant Alliance invaded the five worlds including the Black Wind Sector, the Imperium finally couldn't bear the incessant postponement anymore, and the contractors seemed to feel that many people would lose their heads if they kept on dawdling. Then, the sixteen Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships were finally put in service.

However, because of the fierce battles in the frontline and the shortage of budgets, the sixteen Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships were not completed in the end. They were essentially "preborn babies" with congenital deficiencies. The designers and the military contractors claimed that it would not be their fault if the sixteen super warships could not carry out the theoretically maximal combat ability. It was the problem of time and budgets. If they were given several decades with two to three times more in terms of budgets, the Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships would definitely blow up all the Colossi!

Even the designers and builders were so unconfident. It was not hard to imagine the performance of the sixteen gargantuan "premature infants".

On the real battlefield, they completely lost their destructive aura on the virtual battlefield, and they also revealed their weaknesses of clumsiness and vulnerability. Also, because of the expensive cost, the four Kurfürst families couldn't bear to send those super arsenal warships to the most intense battlefields at all. After only two of them were heavily wounded by the Covenant Alliance, the other fourteen super arsenal warships hurried to escape to their mother port in panic.

The Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships became complete jokes. A hundred years later, there were no more believers of grand cannons and giant warships, and no more new super warships were crafted. In the meantime, the daily maintenance cost for the sixteen "preborn babies" was already so astronomical that even the four Kurfürst families could barely handle it. How could they be dragged into the factories to finish the real construction?

Therefore, a hundred years later, they were still the preborn babies that could only carry out 30% of the combat ability. Abandoned by the best fleets of the four Kurfürst families, they were allotted to the secondary fleets, which were then built into "Eight Adamantines" based on those warships and sent to the battlefield to annihilate the reformists.

"I know that the Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships are never praised, but that's a result of multiple factors. As a matter of fact, even though they are uncompleted, preborn babies that can only carry out 30% of the combat ability, they are still enough to devastate any enemy if they are determined to fight."

Jin Yuyan said with a bitter smile, "Their lousy performance on the battlefield against the Covenant Alliance does not mean that they will show any mercy on our garrison fleet. You went through a lot of trouble to deceive the many Immortal Cultivators outside just now, but getting to the bottom of it, the sixteen Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships are the foundation of the four families' coalition army. If we cannot destroy them completely on the battlefield, nobody will betray and join our side easily.

"Therefore, what's your next trick, 'Black Wind King Li Yao'? Where is your so-called 'Black Wind Fleet'?"

Chapter 2553: Dissatisfaction of the Young Lion

"What gives you the illusion that I am nothing more than a talker, Director Jin?"

Li Yao grinned and said, "How do you know that I'm merely bragging and that the Black Wind Fleet will not show up out of nowhere? Even if the Black Wind Fleet really can't come, I'll still have other ways to take care of the sixteen Adamantine-level Super Arsenal Warships. But of course, the best I can do is to wreak havoc upon them, disrupt their battle formation, and temporarily paralyze their fire control and command systems. The real mission of attack will have to be entrusted to your fleet."

With brilliance beaming out of his eyes, Jin Yuyan held back his excitement and said, "It will be enough to temporarily disrupt and paralyze the enemy's central fleet. However, are you really confident?"

"My confidence depends on Director Jin's trust."

Li Yao said, "To maximize the strength of my people requires one prerequisite—Director Jin, you must allocate a space gate to me, which is to be entirely controlled by me!"

Stunned, Jin Yuyan frowned and thought for a long time.

Anybody with the slightest military common sense knew what a space gate meant in such a final battle.

The coordinates and sailing routes of the Seven Seas Space Zone where the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was at were no secrets. It was more than easy for the four families' coalition fleet to jump over.

However, they were not in possession of the space gates of the Seven Seas Space Zone.

If they were to jump over blindly without the navigation of space gates, all the starships would be evenly distributed in the vast vacuum of the Seven Seas Space Zone, which was more than five billion kilometers in diameter.

How much coverage would it be if it had a diameter of five billion kilometers? Even if the four families' coalition fleet had ten times more starships than the "million starships" they claimed, they would still be drops in a bucket when they were randomly distributed in such a space.

When they were just teleported over, the battle formation, grouping, and even command of the four families' coalition fleet would be entirely gone. Every starship would be a lone island. They had to cruise at the edge of the Seven Seas Space Zone for a long time to regroup and find their own team, forming a solid shape before they marched at the central living area where the Seven Seas Grand Market was located by means of a voyage under regular power.

Obviously, attacking the enemy with the well trained and organized troops while the enemy was still unsteady, preventing the enemy from regrouping, dividing and conquering the enemy's isolated warships and supply ships would create the only opportunity of winning for the garrison of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors with its weak defense.

Many space wars in the past, including the battle between the Black Wind Fleet and the Star Glory Federation, counted on that.

If the attackers condensed their battle formation in time and reestablished effective command, the defenders would be dead for sure.

However, if the defenders took the initiative and suppressed the attackers in the loosened state, the scales of victory would be tipped toward the defenders.

Now that Li Yao demanded the control of a space gate, it meant that he would send the most accurate jump coordinates to a certain mysterious fleet outside of the Seven Seas Space Zone randomly, allowing the fleet to jump to the Seven Seas Space Zone in effective command and organization.

If the fleet was hostile, the doom of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors would come even earlier.

No wonder Jin Yuyan was so cautious and hesitant.

"I am truly not suitable to make such important judgments right now."

Jin Yuyan was clearly sitting still, but steam was popping up from his body, as if he were in the middle of a high-intensity physical exercise. His whole body aged at a visible speed as he rubbed his forehead and sighed. "My original body has been wasted. This body belonged to a pure warrior. Her brain is far from enough to accommodate my soul when I need to calculate and deduce for a long time. I'll be mentally deranged sooner or later. It seems that the time has come for me to back off and let someone more appropriate to manage the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors."

Patiently, Li Yao waited for Jin Yuyan's answer in silence.

Jin Yuyan raised his head and looked at him. After a long hesitation, he finally said, "If I were to ask you what other trump cards you still have, you would certainly not speak the truth, would you?"

"No, I would not, at least not for now."

Li Yao said, "Because it would put my people in unnecessary danger."

Jin Yuyan was silent for a long time again.

His body gradually withered, but his head was glowing in an abnormal brilliance, as if his brain had turned into a most ferocious monster that was swallowing the flesh and blood all over his body.

"Fine!"

Meditating for five minutes, Jin Yuyan finally slapped his thigh and made the decision. "You will have a space gate that is enough to navigate the most elite fleet to jump to our heartland. I just hope that your trick will be worth my trust, 'Black Wind King Li Yao'!"

After saying that, Jin Yuyan rose quickly and staggered out of the room.

Li Yao's demand seemed to have exploited all his physical energy and brainpower. He did not even have the time to say goodbye to Li Yao and was in a hurry to go home and rest.

Hardly had Jin Yuyan left the building when Li Jialing walked in from the next door.

"The director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors does not appear very awesome."

Li Jialing said, deep in thought, "I feel that the guy was tricked by Qi Yuanbao and his lot first and then by Brother Yao later. He hasn't demonstrated the performance of an unparalleled expert at all. He's supposed to be better than that. Is it because he is confined inside the shell of a warrior?"

"That his computational ability is hindered because of the inappropriate body is only part of the reason. However, more importantly, it's because he's been faking it. Be careful. This guy is not so simple."

Li Yao said casually, "He is a businessman, not a politician, a commander, or a leader. A politician can be shameless, a commander can be brutal, a leader can be cunning and heartless, but if a businessman leaves a sleazy and untrustworthy impression on everybody, how can he possibly do any business? Naturally, only the people who seem dutiful, obedient, and even slightly clumsy can be the most excellent businessmen."

Li Jialing was stunned. "Is that so?"

"Of course."

Li Yao smiled. "Did you see his bitter, worried look just now? He was frowning and lamenting now and then, as if there were truly nothing he could do when he was played by us. Didn't he look like one of the dealers on the street who sell goods to people at such great discounts that they seem to be suffering great losses?

"However, if everybody believes what they say and buys the goods with the discounted prices, who's losing and who's winning in the end?"

"It does seem to make sense."

Li Yao rubbed the short black hair on his head. "Although the guy appears unassertive and pliable, he has achieved all his purposes without leaving out any!"

"There's more to it. Do you know who he met first before he came here?"

Li Yao said, "Jin Yuyan went to Red Lady, promising to pardon her and let go of Red Lady's assassination against himself."

"Jin Yuyan is so lenient?"

Li Jialing was rather surprised. "Is he trying to subdue Red Lady again? That makes sense. She's an ace assassin in the Divinity Transformation Stage after all. If he can win her over completely in such a way, it will be a rather fair deal."

"Hehe, you are thinking too little of him if he is simply recruiting Red Lady again."

Li Yao said, "Between Jin Yuyan's lines, he was hoping to contact the top leaders of the four Kurfürst families through Red Lady."

"What?!"

Li Jialing said, "That… That's too unbelievable. I'm completely confused. The final battle between the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the four families is about to begin. Half a day ago, Jin Yuyan executed a hundred assassins from the four families right at the gate of the Commodity Market, and he is hoping to reach out to the leaders of the four families now? What… What… What does he want exactly? He's not thinking of betraying everyone, is he? He does not have any reason to do that!"

"That's certainly unlikely. Even if he truly wants to have a direct conversation with the top leaders of the four families through Red Lady, it will only happen after the battle of Seven Seas is won. Perhaps he has his own plans regarding the postwar Imperium. For example, he may be expecting to strike a balance among Blackstar the Great, the four Kurfürsten, and the Black Wind King. Getting to the bottom of it, businessmen will never annihilate their enemies unless it is absolutely necessary to. Killing one more person means one fewer potential customer!"

Li Yao said, "Now, you should understand why I was reluctant to tell Jin Yuyan our real trump cards, right? I told you about his visit to Red Lady because I want to remind you not to underestimate Jin Yuyan. You can cooperate with such people, but you must keep your eyes open all the time. So much as you take a nap, he may have already sold you!"

"Hehe, however cunning Jin Yuyan is, he is not as good as Brother Yao. His dialogue with Red Lady must've been a top secret, wasn't it? But it still did not escape Brother Yao's ears. You've learned about it after such a short amount of time."

Pouting, Li Jialing said rather sourly, "Getting to the bottom of it, it's Brother Yao who is the most awesome one of all!"

"Huh, why does it sound so sarcastic to me? You seem rather dissatisfied about me?"

Looking at Li Jialing, Li Yao realized what it was about. "I got it now. Are you blaming Brother Yao for using you as a decoy to crawl in the ventilation tube and distract everyone's attention, while I went to the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to show off? Aya, about that, it was an opportunity to learn and practice for a young man like you. I was hoping that you could grow faster and become a talent soon!"

"I am not a three-year-old. Who would care about that?"

Li Jialing's anger was written all over his face as he glared at Li Yao sullenly. "I only have one question. Why was I the last person to learn the great secret that you are 'Black Wind King' when I am your brother?"

Chapter 2554: A Honest Gentleman!

Li Yao's smile was immediately frozen.

"Well…"

He scratched his hair, his eyes wandering unnaturally. "It's indeed hard to explain. Give me a minute and let me think."

"It's impossible to explain at all. It's full of questions and contradictions from the beginning to the end, alright?"

Li Jialing jumped to his feet. "What's there to think? You are coming up with a lie of fifty thousand words to trick me, right? I will not fall for it again. My wisdom has dignity!"

"Aya, don't be excited, don't be excited!"

Seeing that Li Jialing was truly turning red in fury, Li Yao felt that the whole thing was rather tricky too. "Brother Yao did not mean to trick you, but the whole thing was truly very twisted, complicated, unusual, and unbelievable. In fact, I've been considering how I should explain it to you, but I haven't come up with the appropriate description yet…"

"Forget it, you don't need to. I understand it well."

Taking a deep breath, Li Jialing calmed himself down and said rather gloomily, "I know that Brother Yao did not mean to keep it a secret from me. It's just that my identity is too special. I may be captured by Blackstar the Great any minute. His soul may invade my shell, control my brain, and swallow my memories.

"Therefore, whatever Brother Yao tells me, it may be learned by the fiend Wuying Qi. It's for that reason that Brother Yao can't tell me anything.

"I understand all the reasonings. I do. I know Brother Yao has your reasons. I just… can't accept it emotionally for the time being. You too know that I don't have any family since childhood. After I finally learned who my mother was, her attitude was rather subtle if not cruel. Since my childhood, Brother Yao has been the only family member who is nice to me without asking for anything in return. Therefore, after I discovered that I was separated from your little society, and everything was kept from me while you were flirting with Boss Bai and Sister Long, I feel rather terrible.

"I'll be fine. Just leave me alone for a while."

While talking, Li Jialing gave Li Yao a rather rigid smile.

"Wait, since you are so frank, I really can't conceal it from you now!"

If Li Yao could still hold it back, he wouldn't have been a Cultivator at all. He said solemnly, "There are two things that we must straighten out. Firstly, I indeed whispered a lot with Boss Bai, but when did I ever 'flirt' with Long Yangjun? You are free to decide what goes into your mouth, but you must watch carefully what comes out of it, especially when it is a matter of life and death! Secondly, fearing that Blackstar the Great may learn it is only a secondary reason why I did not tell you anything. The main reason is what I said just now. It's too complicated and too hard to explain. Since we have a great foe ahead of us, I did not want to add to our trouble.

"However, if the incident is about to affect our brotherhood, Brother Yao has to explain everything to you. When I asked you to crawl into the ventilation tube to distract the enemy's firepower, you went without batting an eye. If I still keep anything from you, how can I be your big brother in the future?"

"Really? Are you really going to tell me everything, Brother Yao?"

Li Jialing was merely complaining to Li Yao in the beginning. Not expecting that Li Yao was really willing to tell everything, he jumped in excitement. However, he pulled a long face after thinking carefully again. "However, what if Blackstar the Great learns it?"

"We will deal with it!"

Li Yao sniffed. "Wuying Qi is not a fool. Now, I have publicized the identity of 'Black Wind King', and the direction of the Black Wind Fleet has been within the knowledge of the Council of Elders in the first place. Even Qi Yuanbao has guessed that it was Firefly that swallowed the Black Wind Fleet. With his wisdom, do you think Wuying Qi cannot infer more things from the traces?

"I assure you that if Wuying Qi truly takes back the Imperium, he will certainly send a new expedition army toward the general direction of the Black Wind Fleet.

"All in all, the time has come for us to show our cards and swords! As long as we win the battle of Seven Seas, even if Wuying Qi learns everything, so what? He won't be able to do anything about 'Vulture Li Yao'!"

Li Yao slapped his thigh, his dominance surging out like tides, making him truly like an unparalleled expert who mastered the sea of stars—that was, ignoring the giant can of bubble juice that he chugged.

Deeply touched by Li Yao's dominance and determination, Li Jialing felt that the blood all over his body was boiling too. The magnificent picture of the universe seemed to be unfolding before his eyes. Holding back his excitement, he said, "Therefore, Brother Yao, 'Black Wind King' is still not your ultimate identity, is it? Although you have fooled the Immortal Cultivators of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors who do not know much about you, I know very well that you have boundaries however shameless and despicable you look on the surface. You are absolutely not an Immortal Cultivator but a real Cultivator!

"What's the truth exactly? Was Qi Yuanbao's guess correct that the Black Wind Fleet was taken down by Firefly? But how was it possible?"

"In the next second, I will explain my identity and the whole thing to you in the most concise way."

Solemnly, Li Yao extended one finger and said, "However, I have one condition, which is that you must believe everything I say. If you still doubt me when I'm being so honest, it will hurt me too much!"

"Of course!"

Li Jialing swore, "Brother Yao trusts me so much that you are willing to tell me the life-and-death secret. Would I still be a human being if I suspect you?"

"Alright, just prick your ears and listen up. I won't repeat my identity."

Li Yao coughed and said, "I have never lied to you since the beginning. I'm truly a Cultivator and a special representative of the government-in-exile of the Star Ocean Republic on Firefly. I did encounter the Black Wind Fleet, fought them hard, and won the allegiance of many experts after it was defeated. However, there were more things that I have left unsaid.

"Firefly alone was naturally no match for the Black Wind Fleet. Another country was involved in the matter, which was a powerful nation of Cultivators named 'Star Glory Federation', my homeland!

"I don't need to tell you the specific coordinates, population, industrial and technological abilities, magical equipment research abilities, relic exploration records, and other secrets to you, right? The history of the country in the past several hundred years can be explained to you after the four Kurfürsten and Blackstar the Great are crushed. You only need to know that Brother Yao is the greatest living hero in the Star Glory Federation who has saved the federation three times, four times, or maybe even more. I killed 'Skeleton Dragon', the strongest demon expert at the early years of the demon dynasties. I once chopped the head of a member of the Pangu Clan and kicked it around like a ball. I also visited a world of ancient Cultivators covered in the dark nebula where time was frozen, talking and dealing with the ancient Cultivators and extraterrestrial devils in the place.

"I crushed the schemes of the Immortal Cultivators countless times, killed a 'star child' from the Imperium, and swallowed 'Blood Stripe Virus', a mysterious alien creature from billions of lightyears away. I made friends with a big shot of demons and made the largest organized force of demons in the entire universe surrender. When I burst into a fury, I even killed the director of my homeland's intelligence agency and the godfather of the Spiritual Nexus!

"It's exactly because of my feats that I became the idol of countless young people of the Star Glory Federation, to the extent that they called me the founder of the New Federation and the 'nation's father'.

"Later, my wife became the Speaker of the federation, my senior brother and my disciple controlled the largest capital group of the federation together, my best friend managed the largest intelligence agency of the federation, and my another disciple led a different special force that ranks second but is even more secretive and brutal… Huh, why do I feel that I'm even more awesome than the four Kurfürsten now that I talk about it!

"Rest assured, I'm naturally not the same kind of people as the four Kurfürsten. Considering that I was simply too great and brilliant, and it was impossible for me to cover my infinite radiance even if I kept a low profile, the federation would've been accelerated into oligarchy if I were to stay in the Star Glory Federation, suppressing the healthy development of other forces. So, a team of close comrades left the federation with me, and we came to the center of the cosmos that is much bigger.

"After I came to the Imperium, I did not forget to continue my training but demanded myself to perform to a much higher standard. I destroyed 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky', helped the reformists to march into the capital, and beat up Blackstar the Great, severely humiliating the thousand-year-old ghost. Right. I also facilitated the birth of a brand-new virtual life by the way, who would likely make the civilization of mankind leap qualitatively in the next millennium and evolve beyond Pangu and Nuwa. In the meantime, there's also the risk that the civilization of mankind will be destroyed, and everybody either dies miserably or ends up as slaves to the crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus.

"However, I feel that the risk is worth it, because other than the mediocre enemies such as Blackstar the Great, the Alliance of the Holy Covenant, or the Kurfürsten, we may be faced with the unfathomable, ultimate destruction that is the Flood! Indeed, the three thousand Sectors we live in are not the only universe but just a tiny pond which I have named 'Pangu universe'. Outside, there may be countless similar ponds that constitute a 'multiverse' together with us. However, a small spindrift from the Flood may be enough to wipe out the fishes, shrimps, and bacteria in several ponds.

"Of course, the existence of 'Flood' and 'multiverse' is not my own deduction. I just helped everybody solve the critical puzzle because I happened to know a thing or two about the language of the 'wizardly universe'. Why do I know the language of the 'wizardly universe'? Because I probably know the greatest secret of the multiverse, although I have accidentally forgotten it.

"Alright. Reviewing my life in the past hundred years in hindsight, I think those are some of the insignificant matters that I have done. I have become a minor celebrity because of luck. In fact, I do not feel that it is a big deal, and I'm certainly not showing off with such trivia. So, why are you looking at me with such a flabbergasted look? I am already being so honest. You wouldn't suspect that I am bragging, would you?"

Chapter 2555: This War of Mine!

Li Jialing looked like a fish that was struggling to surface in its dream. His lips were cramping beyond his control, and he couldn't have seemed more confused, absolutely unable to follow Li Yao.

He rose abruptly, with fury all over his face, as if he were going to accuse Li Yao of bragging again.

However, the moment he stood up, he recalled what he said just now. Besides, even if Li Yao intended to trick him, he wouldn't have made up the unbelievable bulls*it like "national father", or "executing a member of the Pangu Clan", or "Pangu universe and wizardly universe and multiverse", right?

The young man sat down heavily again, dazed while grabbing his hair.

His soul and his mind seemed to have been crumbled by a hundred high-speed crystal trains repeatedly, depriving him of his thinking ability.

"Hey, hey, hey, hey!"

Li Yao glared at the young man, his cheeks puffed out and his lips rising. "You are not really suspicious of me, are you? You promised just now that you would believe every word I say!"

Li Jialing: "Well…"

"See, you are indeed suspicious of me. Every pore on your body is suspecting every word I said just now. I knew it! I knew it! That's exactly why I never told you the truth. For you mere mortals, everything that has happened to me is too shocking and farfetched. If I tell the truth, I would only be regarded as the lousiest conman!"

Li Yao grabbed the juice bottle and chugged it again.

"No, no, I'm not suspicious of you at all, Brother Yao!"

Li Jialing was back to himself, and his face suddenly turned red. His head in a mess, he stammered, "It's just… It's just that I find it hard to imagine or accept for the time being…"

"What is hard to imagine?"

Holding the juice bottle in his mouth, Li Yao glared at Li Jialing.

"I find it hard to imagine that there would be a country in this universe that regards Brother Yao as 'father'."

Li Jialing observed, with a look on his face that suggested he had just swallowed a large piece of deteriorated cake. He grimaced. "What is a country that regards Brother Yao as 'father' possibly like?"

"Hmm…"

Tilting his head, Li Yao thought for a long time. "What's the meaning of that? Are you implying that I am not qualified to be a 'nation's father'?"

"Perhaps I've been in the world of Immortal Cultivators for too long. The leaders I can imagine are all people like Blackstar the Great, the queen, General Lei Chenghu, or the four Kurfürst. I find it hard to understand what is on the mind of the Cultivators. Perhaps in the world of the Cultivators, it is exactly the guys like Brother Yao who are qualified to be the leaders?"

Li Jialing took a deep breath and opened a bottle of bubble juice, too. Drinking it up, he slightly calmed down and said, "Although it is… so weird that it borders on bulls*it, I am still willing to trust you out of intuition. I did not know that the truth would be like this, or that Brother Yao had such important missions and secrets on your shoulders. I-I really understand why Brother Yao couldn't say it before. Crap. Now that I have learned it, wouldn't the Star Glory Federation be in great danger if Blackstar the Great somehow learns it later?"

"Over the past seven hundred years ever since its birth, the Star Glory Federation has been in extreme danger every second. However, we have survived every seemingly unstoppable disaster. I believe it will be the same this time."

Li Yao said solemnly, "Considering the sizes of the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance, their competition is like a battle of two primeval beasts, which is supposedly not to be influenced by a tiny Star Glory Federation. However, drastic changes that have taken place inside the Imperium push the situation to a subtle junction, giving a rarely-seen great opportunity for the Cultivators to change the whole Imperium!

"If we can be the greatest winners of the battle of Seven Seas, we will have a final say about the future development of the entire civilization of mankind. On the contrary, if we continue to hide and preserve our strength, whether the four Kurfürsten defeat the reformists or the reformists' victory is stolen by Blackstar the Great after they defeat the four Kurfürsten, the enemy will be too formidable for us. I have left so many traces at the center of the cosmos that the Immortal Cultivators would notice the anomalies as time goes by. Even if the Star Glory Federation is far away at the edge of the cosmos, how long can it hide?

"This is exactly what I said just now. You have to fight when you need to fight. When the time has come for you to pick up your weapons and fight for survival, you should be bold and do it!

"That's the reason why I confessed my identity as 'Black Wind King' before all the Immortal Cultivators and why I told you the reason of everything just now. It will be inappropriate if Blackstar the Great, my enemy, has guessed everything while my brother is still kept in the dark, right?"

This time, Li Yao's eyes were truly as clear as water and as hard as marrow crystals that contained ultra-intense energy.

Li Jialing was so touched that he did not know what to say. He felt that his whole body was soaked in the ocean named "trust", which surrounded him with warmth.

"About other things…"

He recalled what Li Yao said just now and asked again, "Brother Yao said a lot just now. I managed to understand the parts about Cultivators, demons, and Pangu, but what are 'virtual lives that are entirely different from us', and what are 'Pangu universe, wizardly universe, and multiverse'?"

"It's a very, very long story, but I'm still willing to tell it to you."

Li Yao explained everything in the simplest and most straightforward way.

In the end, he concluded, "Do you understand now? Dongfang Wang, the former prime minister of the Imperium, was quite right about one thing. He said that our enemy was not a specific person or force but the entire universe itself.

"Dongfang Wang truly deserved to be a man who worked as the prime minister for decades. He saw the key keenly. However, he was unaware of 'wizardly civilization'. It was impossible for him to have imagined the vast multiverse beyond the Pangu universe.

"Now, you have learned everything. How do you feel? Faced with an enemy that is probably beyond the imagination of every human being, are you still willing to fight?"

Li Jialing was stunned again.

However, he was back to himself after only three seconds. Taking a long breath in relief, he said with a smile, "I didn't know that there is such a splendid thing as 'multiverse'. So, we are merely a few little frogs in a tiny pond. No, we are not even frogs. Compared to the brilliant ancient civilizations and alien civilizations, we are the immature tadpoles at best, aren't we?"

"Huh?"

Li Yao was somewhat surprised. "You seem very calm to accept such an unbelievable and unimaginable picture as 'multiverse'. You are not suspicious about it at all?"

"There's nothing to be suspicious about."

Li Jialing said calmly, "Since Brother Yao could be a father of a nation, is there anything impossible in this universe? Yes, I am still willing to fight on, because Brother Yao, Sister Long, Boss Bai, Master Fist King, Liu Li, Xiaoming, Wenwen, and even that boy Han Te are all fighting. I can't be worse than Han Te, can I?"

Li Jialing's reaction was a great relief for Li Yao.

The difficulty of the conversation was much lower than he expected. Queen Li Linghai's child and the little monster who had been refined in "Manjusaka, the City in the Sky" for twenty years truly had a strong mind.

"Brother Yao, I've been hesitating whether or not I should do one thing. I'm here partly to discuss it with you too."

Li Jialing said solemnly, "I did not know that you would trust me as much. In such a case, I will have to do that thing!"

Li Yao raised his eyebrows. "What is it?"

Li Jialing told his plan in detail.

Li Yao's eyebrows were furrowed even harder. Staring at Li Jialing for a long time, he said, "Technologically, it is possible, but the risks are extremely high. Are you sure you want to do that?"

"Yes, I'm certain that it is the best way."

Clenching his fists, Li Jialing said, "It is also the only way that I can fight Blackstar the Great to the end!"

"But you could stay away from the battle."

Li Yao said, "Boss Bai, the Fist King, Long Yangjun, and I will work something out to take care of Blackstar the Great. All you need to do is to find a safe place to hide in and stop him from finding you."

"Didn't Brother Yao say just now that hiding and backing off would not help at all?"

With a hoarse voice, Li Jialing said, "I am not as selfless as Brother Yao. Things like people of the world or the situation of the universe are not my business. However, of all people, Blackstar the Great has occupied the body of the queen.

"In any case, the queen is my birth mother. I have to confront her and Blackstar the Great and ask her why she gave birth to me years ago. Did… Did she regard me as a 'container' to accommodate Wuying Qi's soul in the very beginning, or did she have other reasons that she never talked about?

"If what she wants has always been a soulless container since the very beginning, I will be able to cut out all the hilarious thoughts and die an informed death.

"However, if what Brother Yao said before is true and she had other reasons or was even under the coercion from Blackstar the Great, I will naturally have to get her out.

"Li Linghai is my mother. So, this war is mine too. How can I watch you charge in the front while I hide in an 'absolutely safe' corner?"

While talking, Li Jialing's eyes turned red. There was no telling whether he was angry or missing someone.

"Fine, you're quite right. This war is indeed yours too. Besides, there is no corner that is absolutely safe. Speaking of which, I'm afraid that the safest spot for you will be right next to me, Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, the Fist King, Xiaoming, and Wenwen."

Li Yao rubbed his fists zestfully. "Then, let's settle the technical problems on the details!"

While they were talking, their wrist crystal processors buzzed at the same time.

Raising their wrists to check, they both beamed with interest and shouted, "Boss Bai is ready!"

Chapter 2556: Experts of Civil Wars

The building that Li Yao had asked for from Jin Yuyan was one of the communication nodes of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in the first place. The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had a business network all over the center of the cosmos. Naturally, they boasted state-of-the-art communication equipment. Also, after retrieving abundant magical equipment units from the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' warehouses, Li Yao crafted a few enhancement and blockage devices himself, finishing the modification on the super-remote secret communication system.

Both Jin Yuyan and the four families could've monitored their communication. However, the messages that Li Yao sent out detoured in seven Sectors countless times before they were delivered to the flagship of the "Coalition Fleet of the Pyromaniacs". They were also blocked, encrypted, and anti-tracked by Xiaoming and Wenwen. It was absolutely impossible for other people to detect them.

Also, the whole building had been crowded with sound-blocking and other obstruction units thanks to Li Yao's work. The intense interferential waves that they released every second prevented any data from spreading out.

The place was absolutely safe and secure. They were able to talk freely.

Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi!

The two of them reached the communication center inside the building, which had been modified by Li Yao into a remote observation and command room. When the time came, he would be altering the course of the battle of Seven Seas together with Boss Bai in this place.

At this moment, the hundreds of light beams floating in the midair were glowing in white gloomily. Waves were falling like snowflakes in earsplitting noises.

It was the last safety measure.

The communication between Boss Bai and Li Yao adopted special high-frequency encryption methods. If their frequency was not grasped, one would be able to see nothing but the snowflakes and hear nothing but the noises.

Li Yao and Li Jialing looked at each other. The two of them took a deep breath and stimulated their brain cells, boosting their soul.

When it was boosted to a certain frequency, it was kept still while they spread the tides of the rhythmed brain cells to their retinae and acoustic nerves.

As a result, the whole world around became fuzzy, and they were even twisted into bizarre-looking fiends themselves.

The picture on the 3D light beam, however, gradually became clear. They could also hear Boss Bai's laughter too.

Because the signals zigzagged in space, traveling between the Seven Seas Grand Market and the flagship of the "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs" with the communication bases in dozens of different Sectors as platforms, there were significant interferences and delays.

Therefore, Boss Bai had no time to wait for Li Yao and Li Jialing's reaction but simply began his introduction, hoping to transmit as much information to Li Yao as possible.

After more than half a month of fighting, Boss Bai had become a minor celebrity in the coalition fleet of the four families. Boss Bai today looked absolutely unlike what he used to be in the Blue Sky Market. He was wearing the fanciful combat suit of the Imperium's generals, and the pads on his shoulders almost made it taller than his head. He was also carrying medals, sashes, command sabers captured by him, and the likes all over his body, which made him look like a colorful rooster when he introduced his achievements to Li Yao and Li Jialing in excitement.

In short, the field battles in the previous half month helped the "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs" to better hone their combat ability. Xiaoming and Wenwen, as two information lives, learned the pace of war. The Fist King was adapted to the recently-upgraded warships too.

Besides, because the "National Protection Army", the skin on the "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs", appeared both loyal and capable of fighting among the sloppy, hesitative miscellaneous troops, almost like the single light in a dark night, it was greatly appreciated by the command center of the four families' coalition fleet, which transferred the commandership of several miscellaneous troops to them. Even Li Wuji's position in the command center had risen. He took the opportunity to retrieve abundant assets and equipment for Boss Bai's army. He also brought up the expectation of the commanders, who hoped that they could carry on in the final battle in the next phase and defend the honor of the Imperium well.

In the meantime, Boss Bao had also got the most confidential intelligence through Li Wuji, including the time of the final battle, the grouping information of the fleets, and the strategies on the four families' side.

Such a campaign where a million starships were involved was too massive to be kept a secret. Various people had already sent the approximate time of battle to the Seven Seas Grand Market, and Boss Bai merely narrowed it down to the hour. At this moment, the terrifying forces that were claimed to be from a hundred Sectors had been gathered and prepared. It was estimated that they would launch a space jump in sixteen hours to reach the Seven Seas Space Zone, surrounding the enemy without leaving any gap!

Despite their intimidating size, the morale of the attackers was absolutely not high.

Both the "million starships" and the "coalition fleet of a hundred Sectors" were mostly made of the local armed forces from the edge of the Imperium.

On one hand, the warlords had the natural instinct to preserve their strength. On the other hand, they had been associated with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors for a long time. Chances were that their spokespersons just had a meeting in the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and listened to the roar of "Black Wind King Li Yao". They were thinking about which side they should take right now. How was it possible for them to fight the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors relentlessly without caring about anything?

The four families knew the situation very well, but there was little they could do. They had to boost the morale with both carrots and sticks.

But there were many more sticks than carrots. What they offered were simply promises that would be fulfilled after the Seven Seas Grand Market was conquered.

It must be noted that the warlords and local magnates from the peripheral worlds of the Imperium had been trapped once in the Imperium's counterattack. At that time, countless experts and young men from the peripheral worlds believed in the four families' promise and joined the expedition army, fighting the army of the Covenant Alliance in places far from their homes. After dozens of Sectors were recovered after their hard work, however, the four families were conspiring to kick away the contributors who had shed blood, sweat, and tears and to carve up the newly-recovered areas for themselves.

The incident was exactly the fuse that caused the rise of the reformists.

With such a lesson in mind, even though most of the warlords were not openly on the reformists' side, they simply regarded the many promises that the four Kurfürsten made as bulls*it.

In short, the four Kurfürst families became the victims of their own scheme. Their credit was already gone, and it was difficult for them to lead the team.

Within the four families, on the other hand, there were experts who were adept at civil wars. There were vaguely irrecoverable chasms among them.

Although they knew that the morale on their side was certainly not high in any aspect, due to the huge gap in the size of the army, almost all the leaders of the four families believed that it would be a quick battle without much suspense.

"We are time-honored noble families with a group of experts and tough generals. Our overwhelming starships can swallow the entire Seven Seas Space Zone. Can we possibly be defeated by the small dealers of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors?"

It was the opinion of most of the leaders in the four families.

"The only minor trouble that we may come across in the battle is to gather the messy starships from the hundred Sectors after the jump and make them obey the command center. As long as the gathering is accomplished—no, as long as 30% of our fleet is gathered in the Seven Seas Space Zone with their command restored, the dealers in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors will immediately surrender, right?"

Even the most cautious leaders also thought so.

Since the victory was suspenseless, the distribution of the accomplishments and how to earn the greatest benefits from the "glorious triumph" became the critical problem that was better resolved in advance.

"The four families seem united on the surface, but they actually have a lot of problems among them."

Boss Bai said, "Don't forget that only half a year ago, the Li family, the Song family, and the Yun family joined their hands and declared war on the Dongfang family that was still the strongest back then, significantly weakening the Dongfang family! How could such hatred be forgotten easily?"

Boss Bai told Li Yao that among the four families, the Dongfang family had just suffered serious setbacks. The leaders had been completely cleaned up, and many of the middle and bottom classes joined the reformists in the capital. Its capability had dropped to the bottom of the four families although it was at the top previously.

The Li family wasn't any better. At first, the collapse of "Manjusaka, the City in the Sky" and the failure to annihilate the fleet of the Cultivators made them a joke among all the Immortal Cultivators. Then, Li Lingfeng, the "future Kurfürst", was killed abruptly, and Li Linghai revealed her real appearance as a reformist, stabbing the family's back brutally.

What was most unbearable of all was that "Silver Fox" Li Jiande, the soul of the family, perished in the battle in the royal palace. The backbone of the family was lost.

The series of heavy blows significantly undermined the Li family, whose comprehensive capability was not any better than the Dongfang family's. They had to work with each other in order to check the Yun and Song families.

The Yun and Song families did not suffer major losses in the competitions in the previous phase. Naturally, they were fighting each other to be the leader of the four families.

Which of them would become the leader of the four families, on the other hand, depended on by whom the new emperor was endorsed.

After the demise of Emperor Shenwu, the supreme throne of the Imperium had been vacant. The Yun family and the Song family both proposed their favorite members of the royal family, and they were caught in an impasse. That was why it had been postponed to this day.

The result of the battle of Seven Seas would probably be an important factor that decided whose puppet would become the emperor.

"Yun Xuefeng, the commander of the four families' coalition fleet, is a general who had rather distinguished performances in the Imperium's counterattack."

Boss Bai continued the introduction, "At that time, the fleets of the four families were mostly staying in the rear comfortably, preparing themselves to claim the fruits later. Yun Xuefeng's fleet was the only troop that fought with other miscellaneous troops and had a few head-on clashes with the army of the Covenant Alliance, showing the might of the four families. Some people even believe that he is on par with General Lei Chenghu in the expedition army. Haha. That's naturally a joke. However, the guy is certainly not a random worthless general, and he does want to achieve something for himself."

Chapter 2557: Boss Bai's Scheme!

Boss Bai told Li Yao that, based on his knowledge in the past and his recent contact with Yun Xuefeng, and especially after analyzing the guy's battle cases in the Imperium's counterattack, he realized that while the guy was not a useless general, he was hardly an eminent general either. At best, he was a qualified commander with enough experience.

The problem was that the consecutive victories in the Imperium's counterattack filled Yun Xuefeng's head with illusions and arrogance. He was truly under the impression that he was a general on par with Lei Chenghu and had been competing with him whenever possible. When Lei Chenghu openly joined the reformists, he even declared that he would meet Lei Chenghu in a space battlefield someday so that it could be decided which one of them was better.

"On the surface, Yun Xuefeng and Lei Chenghu both secured glorious accomplishments in the Imperium's counterattack, but what Yun Xuefeng commanded was one of the most elite fleets of the Imperium, which had the incessant support of resources from the four families. On the other hand, what Lei Chenghu commanded was a miscellaneous troop that was short of ammunitions and resources. The two commanders' capabilities are absolutely not on the same level."

Boss Bai said, "Yun Xuefeng also knows that it is more of an adulation that he can compare himself to Lei Chenghu, and that his accomplishments do not really deserve it. It only makes him even angrier and more determined to prove that he is better than Lei Chenghu.

"When the commander has such a state of mind, it will be easy for them to be reckless in the battle. If the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors sets up a few traps taking advantage of his eagerness for victory, he will certainly fall for them."

Yun Xuefeng was the commander of the four families' coalition fleet. He and the Yun family behind him naturally hoped to finish the battle as soon and unstoppably as possible.

However, the Song family, whose overall capability was on par with the Yun family, thought otherwise.

Yun Xuefeng was the commander of the battle. If Yun Xuefeng did distinguish himself on the battlefield, winning a great triumph thunderously, the Yun family's influence would immediately expand tenfold. Countless forces would obey the Yun family, and the new emperor would likely be the Yun family's puppet.

It was the last thing that the Song family would like to see.

Therefore, while they had to win the battle, the "Adamantine fleets" that belonged to the Song family would never work too hard but would possibly watch Yun Xuefeng commanding the Yun family's fleets to fight the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors while doing nothing. They would even slightly hamstring him so that the Yun family and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors would both suffer heavy losses.

For the Song family, the best result of the battle was that Yun Xuefeng's fleet suffered a notable setback on the battlefield when it pressed the charge recklessly, seriously humiliating Yun Xuefeng, before the Song family's fleets appeared and saved the day, securing the glorious triumph.

In such a way, the Yun family would take all the blame, and all the credit would go to the Song family. Naturally, the future emperor would be the Song family's favorite member of the royal family too.

Because of such subtle considerations, while the Yun family's fleets and the Song family's fleets were allies in name, and the Song family's fleets were theoretically under Yun Xuefeng's command, who knew what would happen after they did come to the battlefield!

As for the Li family and the Dongfang family, they were too weakened to compete with the Yun and Song families for now and could only listen to commands obediently.

However, the "Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships" were so valuable that every family only had four of them. Also, the production had stopped a long time ago. Even the docks and the relevant assembly lines had been dismantled or repurposed.

Every Adamantine Super Arsenal Warship was unique. One being blown up would mean one fewer.

The Li family and the Dongfang family had suffered tremendous losses. If they were to lose a few Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships in a war that was meant to annihilate nothing more than a bunch of dealers, wouldn't it be killing them?

Therefore, the Li family and the Dongfang family reiterated their attitude however Yun Xuefeng lulled or coerced them. They had been seriously worn out in the previous phase, and the Yun family and the Song family would certainly be the main attackers in the mission. It would be better for them to cheer for those leading the charge and provide remote fire support! Whoever gave connivance for them to preserve their strength would be supported by them. If Yun Xuefeng insisted that they should lead the charge and be killed, the two families would simply stand on the Song family's side and support the emperor elected by the Song family.

Not just Song Xuefeng, who was just a commander of the army, but even the Kurfürst of the Yun family could barely do anything about the Li family or the Dongfang family.

"All in all, those are the problems within the four families' coalition fleet."

Boss Bai concluded, "Led by their commander Yun Xuefeng, the Yun family truly wants to settle the battle as quickly as possible. However, their allies all have other plans. The Song family hopes that the Yun family will suffer a heavy loss so that they can take over the leadership. The Li family and the Dongfang family want to preserve their strength or even make up for their losses in the previous phase without paying anything. As for the other troops of the warlords and local forces, they are all traitors who sit on the fence. If the free coins in their hands become twice as valuable, they will sell any master without batting an eye!

"It may be too farfetched to say that the so-called 'army of a million starships', which has so many internal contradictions despite the massive size, is doomed to failure, but it is possible to defeat it!"

After hearing Boss Bai's introduction, Li Yao was stunned for a long time. He observed rather in amusement, "With a great foe ahead of them, the four Kurfürst families are still conspiring against each other in internal conflicts? I really don't know how I should comment on them!"

"When a country is about to die, monsters will be showing up. Such is the case at the end of any regime."

Boss Bai smiled and said, "Also, you reserved the cause and the consequence. It was exactly because the four families conspire against each other that they have lost solidarity, the reformists have risen, and they are faced with a great foe!

"One can never change one's nature. Do you think that the leaders of the four Kurfürst families would just wake up and respect each other in unity after the capital was lost? How is it possible?"

"It does make sense."

Li Yao was convinced by Boss Bai and thought that Boss Bai did have a point. Where there were humans, there were conflicts of interests, and when there were conflicts of interests, there were plots and conspiracies. Even the Star Glory Federation, a small country that only controlled seven Sectors, had the contradiction between the "central worlds" and the "peripheral worlds" when faced with the assault of the Black Wind Fleet. For that, Ding Lingdang and Jin Xinyue had been at loggerheads against each other. If Li Yao hadn't returned in time, nobody could predict what the result of their competition would've been.

The scale of the Imperium of True Human Beings and the intensity of the conflicts of interests were ten times greater than in the Star Glory Federation. It would be a miracle if the Immortal Cultivators who did everything for themselves could stop fighting and plotting against each other.

Li Yao's eyes flashed as he continued asking, "Since the coalition fleet of the four families has such deep contradictions, what are we going to do next?"

"In order to deal with the situation where all the branch fleets are disorganized and randomly distributed all over the area after a 'blind jump' to the Seven Seas Space Zone, the four families' coalition fleet has activated a state-of-the-art rapid response command chain system named 'Flying Lightning Command Chain'."

Boss Bai said, "The super command chain based on ultra-powerful Spiritual Nexus, signal enhancement, and brand-new algorithms is 30% more powerful than the old command chain system. It can help the lone starships reach out to their allies and flagship quickly. If the flagship is too far away to be contacted, it will automatically connect the starship to the command system of the highest level around. According to the best ratio of interferential warships, arsenal warships, shield warships, comprehensive supply warships, and other models, temporary battle units will be gathered and concentrated.

"Under my instruction, Li Wuji has been very close to Yun Xuefeng and even secretly pledged loyalty to him. For Yun Xuefeng at this moment, the allegiance of a marquess of the Li family is certainly a great help. Also, our 'National Protection Army' has distinguished performances recently and demonstrated enormous strength. Yun Xuefeng has to pay enough respect to us.

"Therefore, Yun Xuefeng trusts Li Wuji very much right now and has given Li Wuji, or our 'National Protection Army', a very high clearance in the 'Flying Lightning Command Chain' system.

"Specifically, it means that when the million starships of the four families' coalition fleet jump to the Seven Seas Space Zone at the same time, and all the branch fleets have to be regathered and regrouped, my flagship 'Alloy Virus' will be a temporary command center. All the starships within several million kilometers will receive my command temporarily.

"If you can control a space gate, our fleet will be able to jump over while staying organized to keep the highest combat ability. In such a way, the lone starships scattered in space will be subdued by us and forced to join our battle formation easily when they move close to us. Hehe, once those starships accept my leadership and open their mainframe crystal processors to us, it will be impossible for them to get away!

"Just like that, we will gather the lone starships and snowball all the way while we search for the four Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships that belong to Yun Xuefeng. Once we locate them, we will immediately send the coordinates to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and let them attack Yun Xuefeng's fleet vehemently. Trust me, the commanders of the Song family, the Li family, and the Dongfang family will only burst into laughter when they hear that Yun Xuefeng is being attacked instead of reinforcing him immediately.

"If the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is unable to take down the four incomplete Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships with all the forces it has, we can declare the battle lost and escape immediately, but as long as the four Adamantine warships are destroyed, the situation will be entirely different. Yun Xuefeng's flagship is one of the four Adamantine warships, and the mainframe crystal processors and the highest command system of 'Flying Lightning Command Chain' are deployed on it too. As long as the flagship is captured, giving Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King a chance to study them well, hehe, the results will certainly be marvelous!"

Chapter 2558: Ten Hours Until the Battle!

Li Yao's eyes suddenly glittered. He couldn't help but smile, "So, it's the sixteen Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships that you're after. To be honest, I was very tempted when I heard them for the first time too. The super arsenal warships that are highly automatic and can even collect debris during the battle to create new spiritual puppets and drones are really the best carriers for Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King. If all the sixteen Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships belong to us, we will be invincible!"

"To be honest, I am not a fan of those big, stupid, expensive things that are also hard to maintain. The resources to build the sixteen Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships are enough to build a couple of strong rapid response fleets whose range of application and battle performance will be multiple times greater than the former!"

Boss Bai sniffed and remarked in disdain, "Getting to the bottom of it, the sixteen Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships are just a project that certain people in the four families proposed to trick funding, demonstrate their strength, and intimidate the ordinary people and the bottom-level Immortal Cultivators. They're more politically significant than they are militarily. With the development level of the crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus that the Imperium has today, it is not ready to embrace such automatic 'future warships'.

"However, you're right about one thing.
