Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King have computation logics that are entirely different from regular human beings, which allow them to exploit the potentials of the crystal processors and fully utilize the Spiritual Nexus. If they are the controllers of the sixteen Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships, they will be much more powerful than in the hands of the idiots of the four families!"

"Then—"

Holding back his excitement, Li Yao asked, "Is it possible for Xiaoming and Wenwen to hack the 'Flying Lightning Command Chain' and infiltrate the mainframe crystal processors of the sixteen warships to take them over?"

"We can't for now. It is too difficult, Dad!"

Xiaoming and Wenwen's voices came from outside of the main picture. Then, two minor light beams were opened up, displaying the cute faces of the two children. Xiaoming pouted his lips and said, "Although we are information lives and we can travel freely on the Spiritual Nexus, our interferences with the virtual world have limits too, and we have to abide by the law of balance and conservation of energy! Other than everything else, the mainframe crystal processors of the sixteen Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships are as big as skyscrapers. How can we expect to compete with them with our tiny heads?"

"That's right. Furthermore, the four families have deployed different safety measures for their Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships, which means that we have to conquer four different firewalls in order to control the sixteen warships. That's… That's really difficult!"

Wenwen also said, "It is probably as difficult as blowing up a planet in one punch for you, Dad. Dad is a material life, and planets are objects in the material world. However, without enough energy and ways to use it, it is still impossible for you to alter materials freely, right?"

Li Yao immediately understood it after hearing their explanation.

"Therefore, we need somebody else's help to create the best opportunity for us."

Xiaoming and Wenwen said at the same time, "For example, if Yun Xuefeng's flagship that has the highest command clearances is captured, we will be able to completely understand the ranks and the transmission of orders in the 'Flying Lightning Command Chain' system. Then, our computational ability can be enhanced through the mainframe crystal processors on Yun Xuefeng's flagship, allowing us to be connected to the mainframe crystal processors of even more starships on the battlefield. When the computational ability of enough mainframe crystal processors is combined, the odds of success will be much higher if we invade the other Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships with such an 'army'!"

"According to the morale and the internal conflicts in the four families' coalition army that I observed, as long as the four Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships controlled by Yun Xuefeng are blown up, or even if Yun Xuefeng is captured himself, the seemingly overwhelming and magnificent 'army of a million starships' will immediately collapse."

Boss Bai said, "Should we blow up or capture seven to eight Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships, both the troops of the warlords and the underappreciated Immortal Cultivators within the four families will defect without any hesitation!

"I believe that the battlefield will definitely be extremely chaotic by then. All the starships will be running amok like headless flies. Whoever can keep organized and expand their command chain, including more and more starships into their command system, will be the greatest winner!"

Li Yao said with a smile, "It will certainly be us!"

"I hope so."

Boss Bai said, "Up until now, everything has been going on very smoothly. I don't see anything that may stop us."

Li Yao was slightly stunned by that. Tilting his head and thinking for a long time, he asked, "Have you received any updates about the capital from the four families? In the recent months, we've been active at the edge of the Imperium, but Blackstar the Great seems to be awfully quiet."

"Not exactly. Wuying Qi must be busy digesting the resources of the capital. It was the heart of the Imperium in the old days after all. The complicated forces and the astronomical resources can't be combined without a couple of months."

Boss Bai said, "Besides, even if Wuying Qi wants to do something, he lacks strength and space. The 'Eight Adamantines' are hardly the best fleets of the four families, but why have they been sent for the battle of Seven Seas? Because the best fleets are all watching the capital closely!

"However, the Astounding Thunder Fleet, the best troop on the reformists' side, has always been deeply pinned into the four families' heartland. The expedition army in the newly-recovered area also has an ambiguous attitude, even showing signs that they are inclined to Lei Chenghu. The recklessness of any party may lead to unpredictable, drastic changes. That's why the best fleets of the four families dare not move easily.

"All in all, Wuying Qi has been completely stalled. Besides, he needs the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to win the battle of Seven Seas. So, even if he knows everything that's happening here, how is he going to interfere?"

"For some reason, I still feel disturbed."

Tapping his knee, Li Yao said thoughtfully, "All in all, Xiaoming and Wenwen should spare part of your computational ability and disguise the space gate that is about to be opened to you in the Seven Seas Space Zone. It will be best if you can pretend that you hack it remotely or even prevent anybody else from realizing that you are jumping over through the space gate!"

Boss Bai's eyes glittered. "Are you saying that you do not want to expose the relationship between us?"

"Exactly. After I confessed my identity as 'Black Wind King', many things about me are on the table. Blackstar the Great will learn them sooner or later."

Li Yao said, "You are my last trump cards. He can't know that the 'National Protection Army' is 'Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs' yet, or the relationship between the fleet and me.

"The later the cards are shown, the better. It will be perfect if the four families still think that you are the loyal 'National Protection Army' when the battle comes to the end."

"You are giving me a puzzle."

Boss Bai said, "However, it is very interesting and worth challenging!"

"Then, let's begin. I'll send the coordinates and navigation parameters of the space gate to your flagship later and turn off the defense system on the space gate's side so that Xiaoming and Wenwen can take over the space gate remotely in case Jin Yuyan's men play any trick."

Li Yao laughed too. "I can't wait to see your 'splendid performance' again, Boss Bai!"

The two of them ended the conversation in evil, creepy laughter.

Li Yao turned around, only to discover that Li Jialing was looking at him in confusion.

"Do the Cultivators all laugh so scarily and wickedly?"

Li Jialing asked carefully.

"Forget it. It suddenly appears to me that although the Cultivators like Brother Yao laugh rather unpleasantly…"

Li Jialing smiled too. "… I still hope that there are as many people in the universe like you as possible!"

…

There were ten hours to go until the final battle.

The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had learned the accurate time of the battle through various sources, knowing that the Seven Seas Space Zone would turn into an ocean of destruction that was more scorching than magma half a day later.

However, it did not extinguish the fighting will of the soldiers and the reformists of the Business Alliance. Instead, their morale was higher than ever.

Billions of space torpedoes had been sprayed out overwhelmingly, filling every sailing route and even every inch of space in the Seven Seas Space Zone.

Those space torpedoes of the latest model boasted the features of searching for the enemy automatically and attaching themselves to the enemy. They had particularly sharp senses on space ripples.

It meant that when the million starships of the four families jumped out, they would likely be hit by countless space torpedoes while they were still in the twisted, four-dimensional state.

With any luck, dozens of relatively cheap space torpedoes would be enough to lay waste to the most expensive main-force warship and raise an overwhelming, destructive space tsunami that trapped all the starships nearby which had jumped over just now in the gap between the three-dimension and the four-dimension forever.

Besides the space torpedoes, the garrison fleets, the mercenary groups, and the fleets of the space pirates in the past were cruising at the edge of the Seven Seas Space Zone too. Their mission was to find the enemy's lone warships after the jump and destroy them while the enemy was still unsteady and hadn't regained effective command!

When the fleets from a hundred Sectors jumped at the same time, some were inevitably faster than the others, like the beach landing of an army of a million soldiers. The whole jump would probably take one to three days. In such a way, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors will have a chance to outnumber the enemy in specific regions and annihilate the enemy!

The mercenaries, space pirates, and assassins naturally did not have the slightest loyalty. However, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had generously offered countless free coins, bonds, and shares, building the loyalty out of nothing.

Compared to the empty promises of the four families, the free coin was much more credible. For the sake of money, everybody was willing to fight hard!

At the edge of the Seven Seas Space Zone, a sealed space gate that had been closed for a long time blossomed like a bud, sending complicated streams of information to somewhere far away in the distant space.

The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was all set for the war!

Chapter 2559: Yun Xuefeng and Li Wuji

Six hours before the battle, inside the "Purple Cloud Sector", one of the worlds closest to the edge of the Imperium that belonged to the Yun family…

During the past two months, more than ten main fleets of the Yun family and its vassal families had been gathered here to strengthen their armor and firepower, install the latest "Flying Lightning Command Chain" system, and practice the tactics required in the joint battle.

The "Solar Slingshots", made of special materials that could absorb the energy of stellar storms and transform it into the impetus for space jumps, were shipped to a Sector that boasted a gargantuan star.

The Solar Slingshots were hexagonal metal panels that were slightly arced. After all the hexagons were connected like beehives, they turned into a magnificent metal pot. The metal pot, which was in silver at the beginning, turned crimson and looked like blood after absorbing tremendous furious solar energy.

The Solar Slingshots had been stretched to the maximum. As long as they were released, they would unleash the magnificent force that was enough to rip the space.

The dozen main fleets were all prepared to go. Organized like silver chess pieces symmetrically, they were formed into delicate and glamorous cubes in the boundless space.

Despite the conflicts among the four Kurfürst families and the undercurrents of the peripheral warlords' troops, those main fleets of the Yun family were at least determined to win the battle and annihilate the "rebels" as soon as possible.

Their determination transmitted among the starships quickly in flashes and waves, adding thousands of colors to the silver cubes.

The four Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships, as centers of the mixed fleet, finally arrived at the jump point.

Whether or not the combat units inside had been unfortunately castrated due to the lack of funding, the four super warships truly deserved to be called "Adamantine" judging from their magnificent appearance. They were like four space fortresses that were moving slowly. The dense turrets, cannons, and launchers of drones and battle puppets on the surface made everybody feel dizzy when they looked. Their heartbeats were either accelerated to the fastest or reduced to none.

The four Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships were even more shocking than the flagships of the best fleets of the four families in terms of appearance. "Doom", "destruction", or "suffocation" were not enough to describe one-thousandth of their majesty and dominance.

To this moment, countless maintenance ships were still conducting the last strengthening and supplying around the four Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships, delivering all kinds of resources up above. Those warships were like black holes that had swallowed immeasurable ammunitions, fuel, battle puppets, and drones, but their stomachs were still not fed.

All the soldiers were deeply awed by the arrival of the Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships, particularly those from the vassal families as they had never seen such warships before.

At this moment, when they saw the four planets crushing at them overwhelmingly, they finally understood that "the largest super warships of the Imperium" was certainly not an exaggeration. The overwhelming oppression from them couldn't be found from any other starships.

Standing on "Landslide", one of the four Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships and his flagship, and inspecting his troops through the panoramic virtual light beams, Yun Xuefeng couldn't help but feel more ambitious than ever. He was even about to draw his sword and dance in excitement.

As a fleet commander, his life was certainly worth it after having such a moment!

Yun Xuefeng was almost a perfect noble from every aspect.

He was almost two meters tall, strong and handsome. He was the best among his peers since childhood. Nobody ever stayed on top of him in any fight or competition.

In the Imperium's counterattack, most of the nobles were hesitating and dawdling in the rear, waiting to steal the fruits of victory. Yun Xuefeng, on the other hand, considered it a shame to be like them. He commanded his fleet to charge forward and was henceforth known as "Scourging Storm".

Somebody compared him to Lei Chenghu and called them the two pearls of the expedition army. Some even claimed that Lei Chenghu had become an old and toothless tiger, and that the Imperium in the next hundred years would be his.

Yun Xuefeng was not an idiot. He naturally knew that those adulations were not entirely true, but he had always been craving to tower over Lei Chenghu.

Although there was no chance right now, he would certainly be able to collide with Lei Chenghu's Astounding Thunder Fleet after he razed the Seven Seas Grand Market, right?

By then, everybody would know which of them was better, "Yun" or "Lei".

Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep!

When the four Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships, divided into two groups, sailed into the pivot of his fleet respectively, Yun Xuefeng received a communication request from a distant world. The name of the person made him raise his lips in delight.

"Reporting to Commander Yun, my fleet and the 'National Protection Army' are ready. We can launch the first wave of jumps anytime!"

Li Wuji, Marquess Yongchun, appeared on the light beam. With a determined face, he said solemnly, "After the jump, my fleet and the National Protection Army will definitely gather as soon as possible and stall the enemy to buy time for the gathering of the 'Adamantine Fleet'!"

"Good."

Yun Xuefeng sighed and said, "I did not know that there were still men of loyalty and integrity like you in the four families, Marquess Yongchun. Had there been a few more loyal contributors like you, the situation wouldn't have gotten as worse as today at all! Just rest assured and fight without concerns. As long as the battle is won, I will double compensate for your losses!"

"Understood. I will certainly do my best to serve my country and to return Commander Yun's trust and appreciation!"

Li Wuji said in gratitude, "I did not expect that Commander Yun would give me such a high permission in the 'Flying Lightning Command Chain' and that more than 90% of the starships are commanded by me. That's really…"

"Hehe. More than 90% of the starships in our coalition fleet belong to the vacillating warlords and local magnates, who will not move forward at all unless a gun is stuck to the back of their heads."

Yun Xuefeng said gloomily, "Since you are so loyal to me and have such an excellent fleet, I certainly need to release part of the commandership to you. After we jump to the Seven Seas Space Zone, gather those loose cannons well and force them to fight hard. Whoever slacks off and tries to preserve their strength, don't show any mercy on them but punish them according to the martial law!"

"Yes, yes, now that Commander Yun has put it that way, I will not hesitate even if I have to force all the warlords from the peripheral worlds to fight!"

Li Wuji rubbed his hands and declared determinedly. Then, he suddenly hesitated and changed the topic. "However, it's easy to deal with the troops of the regular warlords. I am confident to control and manipulate them. But what about the starships of the Li family, the Song family, and even the Dongfang family? Commander Yun, you should know that they do not consider a minor 'marquess' to be a big deal at all. Even though I have relatively high permission in the command chain, they will not necessarily obey my orders."

"Huh?"

Slightly frowning, Yun Xuefeng said in dissatisfaction, "Everybody knows that you work for me now. Who dares to disobey your orders? Your fleet has such a high combat ability. Are your starships all vegetarians?"

"That being said, if those people really disobey my orders, it's not like I can set up the cannons and bombard them directly, right?"

Li Wuji said with a bitter smile, "I, for one, am determined to annihilate the rebels whatever the losses will be. However, many people from the Li family, the Song family, or the Dongfang family will want to preserve their strength. Just think about it, Commander Yun, will they follow my orders?"

Yun Xuefeng coughed rather embarrassedly. "Then, what's your plan?"

"I'm not suggesting anything else. Even if I have to force those wavering guys to follow my orders with violence, I will not hesitate to do it."

Li Wuji said, "However, my place in my family is very awkward when I am openly siding with Commander Yun without caring about the opinions of my family. If there is any conflict or misunderstanding between me and allies, it will be even more difficult for me to stay in my family. I'll have nothing to defend myself if somebody punishes me for exceeding my authority in the future.

"Therefore, I was wondering if Commander Yun could give me a secret order with a soul signature on it, proving that everything my fleet and I do on the battlefield is authorized by you.

"Then, when I gather the loose cannons, should I encounter the starships of the four families, they would certainly be awed by Commander Yun's prestige and follow my instructions as soon as I show Commander Yun's secret order to them."

"Hmm…"

Yun Xuefeng tapped his fingers for a long time. Looking at Li Wuji's ambitious yet sincere face, he naturally knew what was on the guy's mind. He was merely hoping to seize the opportunity to recruit more starships and expand his strength. When the loose soldiers were "temporarily" gathered by him, they would never be let back after the battle.

However, Li Wuji was his loyal dog right now. With a great war coming near, it would be highly inappropriate if he could not feed a few bones to him.

If he wanted to expand his strength, so be it. It was better to let Li Wuji expand his strength than to let the annoying guys of the Song family expand theirs after all.

Thinking that way, Yun Xuefeng waved his hands. "Alright, I'll give you two secret orders personally and in the name of the 'commander of the coalition fleet'. After we come to the chaotic battlefield, whoever disobeys you after reading the two secret orders shall be executed immediately!"

"Thank you, Commander Yun!"

Li Wuji's eyes immediately turned red, and he was almost choked by sobs. "Commander Yun, you trust and support me so much that I cannot return one-thousandth of your favor even with my life…"

He forwarded exactly what Boss Bai spoke to him a few months ago to Yun Xuefeng without changing anything.

"Haha! Hahahaha!"

Yun Xuefeng had never considered his life above a septic tank before. Naturally, he was unaware of the stinky venom contained in Li Wuji's words. He raised his head and laughed. "Alright, let go of your concerns and fight with your best. After the victory of the battle, our fate will be completely different from the past!"

"Yes."

Li Wuji lowered his head deeply, preventing the guy from seeing the maliciousness overflowing from his eyes, and said in the coldest voice, "I have no doubt about that."

Chapter 2560: A Sacrifice of 99%!

Three hours before the battle, besides the "Purple Cloud Sector" that was controlled by the Yun family, the military towns controlled by the other three families, as well as the jump points at the edge of the Imperium, had witnessed the final gathering of all the fleets. The four-dimensional jump devices including the "Solar Slingshot" were all activated.

Three hours later, the hundreds of thousands of starships from a hundred Sectors would be teleported to the tiny Seven Seas Space Zone and completely drown the place.

The battle of Seven Seas was about to begin, and the entire space reeked of the smoke of war. However, the capital, at the center of the turbulence, was as peaceful as the eye of a storm.

Deep below the capital in the Empyreal Terminus Sector, the underground relic that Li Yao and Long Yangjun explored a few months ago had been expanded dozens of times. Finally, an ancient building that looked like a pyramid had been exposed.

The transparent pyramid seemed to be covered in an unpredictable twisting force field on the surface, allowing it to emanate brilliant colors all the time that filled every inch of space below the ground. The darkness here was transformed into an ocean of colors.

The size of the building exposed out of the rock was already as magnificent as a palace. Every edge of the pyramid was at least five thousand meters long. There was still no telling how enormous the size that was buried below the rocks was.

What was most incredible was that the ancient building of such an enormous scale did not have the slightest gap on the surface. It seemed to be a natural and whole piece instead of a building piled up with rocks!

Around the pyramid, countless sophisticated primeval words had been carved densely. Every word was a maze-like cube that contained information a hundred times more abundant than the language of mankind. Under the contrast of the splendid colors, all the words were floating and revolving slowly in the midair.

The scene deep below the ground was like the most bizarre dream that was completely off reality.

At this moment, countless soldiers that belonged to the reformists were continuing the work around the pyramid. A team of researchers was exiting and entering the pyramid too.

Suddenly, the cubic rune arrays floating around the pyramid spread out dim ripples. One of the soldiers screamed and leaped, holding his head.

In his eyes, his skull was cracking and exploding. Even the helmet of his crystal suit could not stop the anomaly of his skull. His head was getting bigger and bigger, unleashing the mental power that felt like a volcano eruption.

"Aoooooooo!"

When his head was twice as huge, even blowing up the helmet of his crystal suit, the soldier finally went mad and attacked his companions around.

His companions seemed to have been used to such accidents. Everybody opened fire without batting an eye. A hundred cannons shot at the same time, blowing the crazy soldier into smithereens. Even his head completely exploded after a huge noise.

His corpse fell to the ground like mud, and spiritual puppets immediately went forth to clean up the mess.

The eyes of the rest of the soldiers were filled with zeal. Not bothering the minor episode at all, they simply continued their work.

Queen Li Linghai, in her form with a headful of white hair that looked like a waterfall, floated at the top of the underground space and observed everything coldly from the high stance.

"Another one who has lost sanity."

She said indifferently, without the slightest emotion in her voice.

"Such sacrifices are necessary for the rise of the new Imperium and for the tomorrow of the civilization of mankind."

Deep inside her brain, Blackstar the Great's voice echoed gloomily.

"Even if—"

Li Linghai was silent for a long time, before she went on again, "Even if everybody in the capital or the Empyreal Terminus Sector has to be sacrificed, will the sacrifices be necessary too?"

"Of course."

Wuying Qi said casually, "The birth of the new Imperium is a miracle like the nirvana of phoenix. How will a brand-new future arrive if the old world is not completely destroyed?"

Li Linghai hesitated. The nonchalant mask on her face that she carefully made fell apart, revealing part of her real feelings without her knowing it.

"Are you still thinking about your son?"

Wuying Qi immediately grasped her subtle change of feelings. He scorned, "The battle of Seven Seas will begin in three hours. Let him play the childish game with Li Yao and the people of the four families in the Seven Seas Space Zone. What we should pay more attention to is the civilization of mankind billions of years later, and how to focus the ambitions, bravery, and strength of all human beings to break out of this narrow, suffocating, tiny universe, right?"

Shivering, Li Linghai couldn't help but nod her head. "… Yes, Your Majesty."

"For billions of years, too many civilizations have been stranded in this tiny universe, including not just the Pangu and Nuwa civilizations but also the ancient civilizations that were even more powerful than Pangu and Nuwa."

Blackstar the Great sighed. "But no matter how powerful they once were, so what? As long as they cannot break out of the tiny universe, they would forever be restrained by the insufficiency of resources. Their doom was as inevitable as their splendor.

"The civilization of mankind is only just born and still in the growth period. Compared to Pangu, Nuwa, and other ancient civilizations, we are naturally rather weak. However, it is exactly because we are just born that infinite possibilities are still ahead of us. We should not walk on the old path of Pangu, Nuwa, and other ancient civilizations since the very beginning. Even if the odds are just one billionth, we should still summon all our courage and rush out of this damn universe not fearing any sacrifices!

"Even if destruction is unavoidable, we should be destroyed in the grand universe that is much vaster, instead of dying without a sound in this dry well!

"This is the greatest reason why I am determined to build a new Imperium. For such a new Imperium, for such a civilization of mankind, and in order to break into the unvisited lands that Pangu and Nuwa never reached, is a minor capital really a big deal?

"Let me tell you the truth. The civilization of mankind does not stand any chance if it goes on like this. For our most magnificent plan, even if half of all human beings must be sacrificed—no, even if nine-tenths or even 99% of them have to be sacrificed, the 1% strongest experts will still survive, break out, and evolve into brand-new forms that are thousands of times stronger than they are today like the legendary gods. Then, for the whole civilization, it will be the greatest fluke!"

Wuying Qi's crazy roars made Li Linghai barely able to catch her breath.

"I understand it now, Your Majesty."

Her mind unsteady, the queen of the Imperium did not dare ask any longer, fearing that she might be completely lost in Wuying Qi's crazy and profound soul.

Looking at the pyramid down below that was glowing in eerie brilliance deeply, she said, "All for the civilization of mankind. I'll listen to you on everything, Your Majesty."

…

There were the last five minutes until the battle.

Deep inside the Seven Seas Grand Market in the Seven Seas Space Zone, an overwhelming light beam had been slowly unfolded in Jin Yuyan's remote command center, displaying the hologram of the entire Seven Seas Space Zone before everyone, including Li Yao.

The spots on the hologram were the locations of the fleets that belonged to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, the half-fixed space fortresses on the orbit of every planet, and the distribution of the space torpedoes that had filled all the sailing routes.

The light spots were so dense that a new layer of stars seemed to have been added to the sky.

Even though the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had done all the preparations that could've been done, even though they had been more united than ever after the four families' scheme of assassination was disrupted, and even though the arrival of "Black Wind King Li Yao" boosted the morale to the highest, the smothering tranquility during the last few minutes before the great battle was still tearing their nerves brutally, making everybody in the command center pale and their heart race. Their eyeballs and their sweat seemed to be frozen.

…

Three minutes before the battle, on "Landslide", the flagship-in-general of the coalition fleet, in the Purple Cloud Sector, the most gargantuan light beam was also rising before Yun Xuefeng, manifesting the gathering status of the fleets from the hundred Sectors. The brilliance of the spiritual shields of so many starships that were activated at the same time almost congregated into an ocean of suns, almost too dazzling for him to watch.

Yun Xuefeng grinned and slowly unsheathed the gold command saber, raising it high.

Although it was a meaningless, symbolic movement, Yun Xuefeng still had the habit of doing it before every battle. When he held the command saber tightly in his hands, he had the feeling that he was melted with the million starships and filled with infinite strength.

"All branch fleet commanders, begin the jump now!"

Yun Xuefeng slashed the command saber heavily and declared, one word after another.

For a moment, all the light beams seemed to be involved in gigantic swirls as the pictures on them were blurred and twisted. The starships on them gradually turned transparent, before they were stretched and paved!

The stars were dragged into helical rays by the "Solar Slingshots". The rays were then interwoven into tunnels that tore apart the space and led to a different world. The starships all moved toward the "tunnel of stars" slowly, as if they were "falling" into the invisible wormholes. They were soon gone.

The blind jump and the sudden assault performed by hundreds of thousands of starships at the same time began!

…

"Ahhhhhhh!"

In the command center of the Seven Seas Grand Market, the hologram immediately reacted. Like a pond where it was drizzling, black ripples appeared and spread out nonstop. At first, there were only a few tiny raindrops, but they became a torrential storm very soon. The whole space zone was occupied by intense waves.

"So-So many space gaps that have been ripped by brute force!"

"They're almost everywhere. All the sailing routes, all the space, and all the surroundings of the planets are brimming with space ripples. How many starships have jumped over simultaneously?"

"Are the four families really so determined to win the battle?"

Chapter 2561: Fierce Battle Among Stars!

In the exclamations of the Immortal Cultivators of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, the black ripples expanded larger and larger, gradually turning from intangible waves into concrete lumps. When the lumps were connected, they almost filled up the whole hologram of the space zone.

A series of numbers ran down like a waterfall, suggesting that thousands of massive objects had appeared out of nowhere. It was impossible to detect the specific number at all.

In the boundless sea of stars, irregular gaps that were darker than abysses and muddier than swamps were ripped apart. Narrow, long glittering streams of materials and information spurted out of the gaps, congregating into their forms in the three-dimensional world.

The space warships that had been armed to teeth surfaced like whales. The emblem of the Imperium and the sigil of the four families glowed with each other, emanating devastating brilliance.

The space torpedoes that had been sprayed all over the space zone immediately reacted. As their power rune arrays unleashed glamorous brilliance, they darted at the invaders leaving aggressive exhaust flames behind.

For a supermassive warship to complete a space jump and get rid of the space ripples that lingered, it would take several to dozens of minutes.

In the meantime, it barely had any ability of defense or mobility. It was even very vulnerable when it was in the "half-unfolded" state between the three-dimension and the four-dimension.

Tremendous space torpedoes flooded forward like suicidal crystal bombs, exploding next to the nearest invader. Countless brilliant light balls were immediately glowing, and the storm of destruction swept across the entire starship.

The soldiers on those starships were mostly torn into shreds by the destructive storm before they recovered from the dizziness and nausea caused by the space jump.

The pieces of the bones and the starships were also jetting to all directions, leading to a chain reaction. As long as a piece the size of a fingernail happened to be at the spot where another starship jumped over, making the two parties overlap, it was possible that a fusion on the atomic level would be caused, leading to another explosion.

As a result, one area of space torpedoes could often trigger a series of intense explosions, transforming the space zone within ten thousand kilometers into a flickering ocean of fire. When the flames died out, the only things left behind were the scorched debris that was almost orange and the invaders' bodies that had almost been melted.

It wasn't the only trouble that the invaders encountered.

To resist the aggression of the coalition fleet of the four families, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had not only established countless space torpedoes in the whole space zone but also dumped garbage or even perfect goods on various sailing routes. Even the most inconspicuous and harmless goods would still raise catastrophic consequences once they were hit by the starships that were having a space jump, just like a tiny sparrow might've caused a destructive strike on a shuttle that was flying at a super high speed.

On the other hand, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors boasted countless space fortresses near the Seven Seas Grand Market as well as permanent bases modified from asteroids. The orbit of the Seven Seas Grand Market boasted the most brutal firepower too.

Some of the invaders, who were less fortunate, jumped into the range of the space fortresses, permanent bases, and orbit defense network of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in the very beginning.

The defenders were patient enough to not open fire until those starships were more than half teleported. Thousands of fiery snakes danced crazily in the dark vacuum, blowing those starships into wasted iron and copper before the enemy opened their spiritual shields.

Furthermore, even planets themselves were the most useful weapons.

The Seven Seas Space Zone had twelve planets in total. Among them, Planet No. 7 had a size and mass thousands of times than any of the other planets. If the starships of the invaders unfortunately jumped into the gravity sphere of the gigantic planet, it was often the case that they were ripped into smithereens by the huge gravity tide before they had any chance to react or dragged to the surface of the planet and squeezed into a thin plate before the power units were teleported yet.

Of course, even the most unbelievable ways of death could not compare to the starships that had unfortunately jumped to the nearby if not the inside of the sun. The starships whose landing points were too close to the star were vaporized at the very beginning.

The scourge of space torpedoes, the barrage of defense network, the ripping of gravity tides, and the direct immolation of stars made the invaders lose 5% of their strength in the first hour.

A "blind jump" without the navigation of space gates was like a "beach landing" in a dark night against a whole beach of bastions. 5% was an acceptable loss.

There were too many invaders who were too scattered. It was impossible for the space torpedoes of the defenders to lock onto all the targets.

Part of the starships of the invaders was mired in fierce fights, but most of the starships took the opportunity to evacuate to the edge of the Seven Seas Space Zone. Countless spiritual towers were released and turned into nodes of the Spiritual Nexus with super high performances. Tremendous data and information were flowing on the Spiritual Nexus, paving the invisible network to the whole space zone. The command chain of the invaders connected one warship after another before it weaved a highly-efficient battle network where all the data were shared among tens of thousands of warships.

At first, all the starships of the invaders were dizzy, not knowing where they or their allies were.

The arsenal warships did not have the ammunition supply from the comprehensive supply ships, the comprehensive supply ships did not have the defense of the shield warships, the shield warships lacked the ability of cyberwarfare, and the cyberwar warships did not have lethal spears and hard shields. It was impossible to fight the war when everybody was on their own.

However, as the "Flying Lightning Super Command Chain" system was unfolded, every arsenal warship automatically scanned the nearest shield warships, comprehensive supply ships, and the cyberwar warships. Also, according to the information that had been inputted into the database earlier, they were automatically matched into a temporary unit that was best to carry out their specialties. The optimal sailing route was also set so that those warships could approach each other.

Furthermore, based on the key parameters such as the military ranks and the levels of their commanders, different permissions were given to them, and a leader of the temporary battle unit was nominated for them in case the Immortal Cultivators who might not know each other had conflicts.

The four families had truly gone through a lot of trouble to unite the warlords and local magnates of the hundred Sectors.

The defenders, naturally, could not allow the attackers to gather easily.

The defenders were not 10% as strong as the attackers in terms of comprehensive capabilities. The only advantage they could take was the attackers' weakness when the attackers were separated after the jump.

If all the battle units of the attackers were gathered, and the battle units congregated into fleets in rigorous battle formations, the defenders would be doomed!

Therefore, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors put all the stakes on the table as soon as possible and released all the fleets!

Even the weakest half-armed garrison fleets, which were basically modified from carrier fleets, had temporarily accommodated abundant space torpedoes and interferential magical equipment, searching for the spiritual towers that the attackers had projected to space.

The spiritual towers had to send intense spiritual waves and abundant data to all directions all the time. They were like the glittering stars on a dark night and could be found very easily.

Once they detected the spiritual towers, they would destroy the devices without any hesitation or release powerful interferences to cover and block their signals.

After a spiritual tower was taken down, a node of the Spiritual Nexus of the attackers would be erased. The remote information transmission devices installed on the starships were far from enough to satisfy the demand of data transmission of the "Flying Lightning Super Command Chain" in a vast space zone with a diameter of almost ten billion kilometers by themselves.

Many starships of the attackers had just been connected to the battle network and matched with the most suitable allies around. They were about to join their allies when the battle network was suddenly cut off. The information of all their allies vanished. They were like a grain of dust compared to the vastness of the universe, and they had no idea where they were at all.

In the next second, the hunting fleets made of the mercenary and assassin groups would show up unexpectedly and force them to surrender.

Although the starships of the mercenary and assassin groups were mostly weaker than the regular army in terms of combat ability, they had the overwhelming advantage in number on the local battlefield. They had different units of starships and the most rigorous battle formation, which was certainly not something that the lone starships of the attackers could resist.

Even though their allies were around, they did not know their allies' specific locations, and it remained to be seen if those "allies" would reinforce them. Even if those allies came, their own starships would've been destroyed by then.

For the extremely selfish Immortal Cultivators, did they need to hesitate about what they should do in such a situation?

Besides, although the attackers claimed to have an army of a million starships, 90% of them were wavering and sitting on the fence, ready to swing with the wind and hop between the two sides anytime.

The commanders of many starships had learned the arrival of "Black Wind King" Li Yao. They had been planning to defect if things did not go well on the battlefield. Now that they had been unluckily surrounded by the troops of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in the very beginning, they were left with no choice but to surrender!

Therefore, the defenders' disarming work went quite well without meeting major resistance.

Only when they encountered the starships commanded by the central members of the four families would fierce battles really break out. However, such starships were not large in number, and they were soon drowned by the overwhelming firepower of the defenders.

As for the best garrison fleets of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, they simply turned a blind eye to the battles of the shrimps and pressed forward to the edge of the space zone, analyzing the information from the attackers' spiritual towers while searching for the attackers' sixteen Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships.

They had to give the enemy a heavy blow while the sixteen Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships were not stabilized yet, undermining their combat ability as much as possible and even destroying them.

Once the sixteen Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships completely got rid of the chaos and had enough support from allies, forming eight magnificent fleets, the defenders' efforts would be for nothing however hard they were trying right now!

Chapter 2562: Boss Bai's Rise!

For a moment, the dark universe was illuminated by the flashes of perishing lives. Countless remnants were swirling and drifting in the shining ocean like macula. Too many creatures were instantly vaporized. Even the experts in the Core Formation Stage or the Nascent Soul Stage were also obliterated physically and mentally despite their struggle in the turbulences raised by the barrage of thousands of cannons.

Compared to the vast sea of stars, the lives of human beings were so insignificant, and human beings seemed to have understood their insignificance so well that they did not consider their lives a big deal at all but simply squandered them in the bloodiest and goriest ways, enjoying the violent delights.

The defenders had done everything they could've done. From the strategies to the tactical details, their performance was almost perfect.

The space torpedoes they set up mostly hit the targets. It did not take them much trouble to surround or talk the lone starships of the attackers into surrender. Luckily, the main fleets discovered two Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships at the edge of the space zone in time and launched an attack when the enemy was still not steady. The two parties were caught in a fierce battle. The two Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships were unable to move and heavily damaged.

It was a textbook battle of defense.

But it could not change the fact that there were too many starships on the attackers' side. Even though the claim of "million starships" must be discounted, the warships among them still amounted to three to five hundred thousand. They were also scattered in the entire vast sea of stars. It was impossible for the defenders to lock onto them in time.

Therefore, wherever the sight and firepower of the defenders could not reach, more and more starships of the attackers were included in the "Flying Lightning Super Command Chain", allowing them to find their allies and their temporary flagships. Battle units were formed one after another and then joined into branch fleets. Many branch fleets had received the call from the Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships, and the "Adamantine Fleets" that the defenders were most scared of were about to take shape.

After half a day of the fierce fight, the battle was still unpredictable. The scales of victory were like an electrocardiogram. There was still no telling to which side it would be inclined to in the end.

At this moment, at a certain point at the periphery of the Seven Seas Space Zone…

This place was highly distant from the planets and the meteoroid belts. There were no usable resources around. It was relatively desolate and quiet.

A space gate that had been sealed for decades, however, was blossoming like a flower and glittering in a fixed rhythm in glamorous brilliance.

Under the navigation of the space gate, an oval wormhole with a clear edge and an intact form was ripped apart on the three-dimensional space, and a rigorous, organized, and magnificent fleet passed through it slowly.

Because of the navigation of the space gate, the teleportation of the fleet was very smooth and secure. Not a single unexpected collision or explosion happened when all the starships got rid of the space ripples that haunted them.

Compared to their allies who were essentially attacking a beach that was full of reefs in a thunderstorm at a dark night, the fleet that had just been teleported was as comfortable as if they were traveling on a yacht and enjoying the sunbath with iced juices.

It was exactly the "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs", or "National Protection Army", in the sequence of the coalition fleet of the four families.

However, the scale and the level of the fleet were much higher than what was reported.

Thousands of super warships that had been highly modified and strengthened were gradually paved in space, forming a round and soft battle formation. At first look, it was like a transparent, gargantuan jellyfish floating in the sea of stars, and the exhaust flames from every starship were exactly the glittering nerves of the jellyfish.

Any fleet command with the slightest knowledge would be palpitating intuitionally when they saw the naturalness and smoothness as the fleet changed the battle formation all the time.

It was too smooth, perfect, and horrifying!

When thousands of iron and cold objects were gathered, they were as soft and agile as a living creature. Every power unit on every starship seemed to be an extension of the commander's body and soul, changing quickly according to what was on the commander's mind.

Only a fleet commander who had two lives of battle experiences like Boss Bai and the super-intelligent lives like Xiaoming and Wenwen could've made such "miracles"!

Standing on the bridge of "Alloy Virus", the flagship, Boss Bai appreciated the bleeding sea of stars with a cold smile. The projection system had displayed the holograms of Long Yangjun, Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King next to him, as well as all the battle parameters and information on the battlefield. Everything was spreading out like a flood that had just broken the dam.

Li Wuji was standing in the shadow not far away and looked at Boss Bai full of expectations. For obvious reasons, the greatest traitor of the four families did not dare to go back to the Li family's fleet. After much deliberation, he had decided that Boss Bai's place was the safest shelter.

At this moment, he was only hoping for one thing to happen, which was that Boss Bai could defeat as many nobles as possible so that those people would all be hanged above a septic tank and taste what he had tasted before!

"Destroy the space gate. Do not let anybody notice that we have been teleported through the space gate."

Boss Bai gave the first order. Then, it was the second order. "Fist King, ask Xiaoming and Wenwen to help your fleet enter full stealth and silence. Do not let anybody detect you!"

It was what the Fist King was best at. Back in the Blue Sky Space Zone, it was thanks to the long-time stealth and silence that he successfully ambushed Li Wuji's fleet. At this moment, with the assistance of Xiaoming and Wenwen, the job was even easier for him.

Very soon, more than half of the flames of light dimmed and died out in the Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs. Even the spiritual waves that marked their previous existence were dispersing. Those starships were like ice melting into water, not leaving any trace at all.

It was not until then that Boss Bai ordered the rest of the starships to release abundant spiritual towers unhurriedly. He also activated the remote communication units on the starships and opened the many interfaces of the "Flying Lightning Super Command Chain", searching for the allies nearby.

Under Li Yao's instruction, the defenders intentionally loosened the defense of this area. Many of the attackers' starships were scattered in a range of a million kilometers nearby.

Hearing Boss Bai's call, they immediately moved close to the "National Protection Army".

Boss Bai had been a famous brutal bandit at the peripheral worlds of the Imperium in the first place. Everybody knew that he had gathered a bunch of most ferocious space pirates into the "National Protection Army", which had remarkable performances in the previous battles and demonstrated a high combat ability.

Naturally, most of the lone starships were willing to follow Boss Bai's command, because it would increase their odds of survival.

"Warship 11985, reporting to Commander Bai. Our warship has been connected to the tactical system of Alloy Virus. We will follow Commander Bai's commands!"

"Warship 56237, 'Sun Chaser', reporting to Commander Bai!"

"Warship 35223…"

The ignorant shield warships, arsenal warships, cyberwarfare warships, and comprehensive supply ships all approached the "National Protection Army" that was hiding in the dark.

"Very good."

Boss Bai sneered and gave orders to those starships. "Please fully open the ports of the Spiritual Nexus on your starship so that I can send the latest battlefield data and secret orders to you. I have the highest command from Commander Yun Xuefeng that needs to be conveyed and carried out in time!"

While talking, Boss Bai sent the secret orders that were endorsed by Yun Xuefeng's soul signature to those starships.

Although the orders were rather obscure and ambiguous, it at least suggested one thing: Boss Bai was Yun Xuefeng's trusted subordinate, and he was carrying out a top-secret mission that directly came from Yun Xuefeng.

"Now, hurry up and open your Spiritual Nexus and your mainframe crystal processors so that the data can be synchronized. Then, I can tell Commander Yun's specific commands to you!"

Boss Bai sneered, "You guys are terribly lucky to have been chosen for such a critical mission. Hehe, your future is nothing but promising!"

The starships that approached the "National Protection Army" were mostly the warlords' troops from the peripheral worlds of the Imperium. They did not have a high combat ability themselves or any influential supporter. How could they dare defy the National Protection Army, which had been Yun Xuefeng's favorite recently and which boasted a tough combat ability?

Some people were even secretly delighted, really thinking that they had been involved in a fortuitous incident!

Therefore, none of the commanders of those starships suspected anything but opened all the clearances of the battle network to "Alloy Virus", allowing abundant data to be filled into their mainframe crystal processors.

Chapter 2563: Coercion

However, after only three seconds, the commanders of those starships realized that something was not right. What Boss Bai sent over was not the harmless battlefield data but the lethal crystal processor viruses mixed in the data.

The crystal processor viruses that had never been seen before corrupted their feeble defense systems like ghosts instantly, slightly "modifying" their starships and locking their starships in the "absolute security mode" where the allies could not be attacked.

In the "absolute security mode", the identification system of the mainframe crystal processors would automatically detect all the allies within the firepower range and block all the attacks that were aimed at allies.

The mode was mainly applied in the scenarios where the enemy and the allies were too close and a barrage might accidentally damage the allies.

However, there was not a single hostile warship within hundreds of thousands of kilometers from either themselves or the National Protection Army. Why was Boss Bai blocking their attack ability?

Of course, starships were too important to be controlled by external forces. Through the soul signatures of the captain, the deputy captain, and the fire control officer, it was still possible for them to change the starship's status with "manual unlocking".

However, it would take at least a minute and a half for the starships to exit the "absolute security mode" through "manual unlocking".

However, when the captains realized that something terrible had happened, alarms that were as gloomy as the knells echoed on their bridges, implying that the scanning waves were hitting their spiritual shields hard. They had been locked onto by the "National Protection Army".

Caught in the "absolute security mode", they had temporarily lost the ability to attack their "allies". However, the "National Protection Army", as their ally, showed no sign of mercy at all. As long as it barraged, they would be destroyed and reduced to broken iron coffins drifting in the dark vacuum in half a minute!

"Boss Bai!"

"Commander Bai!"

"Bai Xingjian!"

The captains were all greatly shocked, their cold sweat dripping from their eyebrows to their nose. Fearful, they asked, "What's the meaning of this?"

"Nothing. It's just a… safety measure that Commander Yun asked me to take."

Boss Bai smiled casually in the communication channel. His eyes were like two deep wells where venomous fogs were floating as he stared at the frightened captains scarily. Dragging his tone, he said, one word after another, "Commander Yun told me that the future of the Imperium depends on this battle, and the victory of this battle depends on your valiance more than anything else. However, many of you are vacillating, disloyal people who have other plans. Not only are you slacking off, but a lot of people are also thinking about defecting on the battlefield and selling the comrades next to you, trading the Immortal Cultivators of the four families for your own fame and fortune… AREN'T YOU?"

He finished his sentence in a thunderous roar. Together with the angry look on his face, even Boss Bai's weaselly appearance became more dominant and intimidating than ever.

As if hit by lightning, the many captains subconsciously backed off even though Boss Bai was just on the light beam. They shook their heads and said, "No, we never have such plans!"

"You don't?"

His arms akimbo, Boss Bai burst into laughter. "Do you really think that the four families are blind and deaf, and that Commander Yun is an idiot that you can deceive and manipulate freely? Hehe. Let me tell you the truth. Commander Yun knows exactly what you did in the past few days!

"Many of you have shares in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors or the major stores, warehouses, and carriers fleets under the Business Alliance, don't you?

"Every one of you has a dozen accounts where tremendous free coins are saved. Should the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors be destroyed, those free coins will immediately become nothing, won't they?

"A lot of you have sent your trusted subordinates as your spokespersons to the Seven Seas Grand Market, where they are in the closest contact with the leaders of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors right now, right?

"A lot of you have done things that are against the interests of the Imperium. You even intentionally let the new warships encounter 'space storms', while you actually smuggled them to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Those warships are exactly on our opposite side right now and are used against us, aren't they?"

The four consecutive questions flabbergasted all of the captains. They were almost hugging each other to shudder together.

"What should I say about you?"

With one arm on his hip, Boss Bai pointed at the camera with the other hand, almost reaching the tip of the noses of the captains through the light beam, as if he couldn't have felt more regretful. After a long rampage, he finally took a deep breath and calmed down, saying sullenly, "Of course, you all had your reasons. There are too many people exactly like you in the entire Imperium. It is truly unfair to blame you alone."

"That… That's right."

The captains who had fallen into the trap, as if seeing a life-saving buoy, hurried to nod their heads and said contritely, "It is indeed unfair. We-We were forced to do that!"

"Therefore, Commander Yun is willing to give you an opportunity. As long as you listen to me obediently and accomplish Commander Yun's upcoming mission, not only will your misdeeds in the past be forgiven, there will also be great benefits for you."

Boss Bai grinned hideously. "Of course, in case any of you goes feverish and does anything stupid in desperation, it is not outrageous that I have taken a few 'precautions', is it?"

"No-No, it isn't!"

They were the weaker party in the negotiation, and their secrets had been grasped by him. What else could the captains have done except for shaking their heads hard?

"In that case, join the battle formation now!"

While talking, Boss Bai sent different coordinates, as well as the sailing routes that were set according to the coordinates, to the dizzy lone starships, letting them continue to approach his main troop.

It was not until those starships were included in Boss Bai's fleet that they discovered the starships controlled by the Fist King, Xiaoming, and Wenwen were hidden right next to them. They couldn't help but be stunned by the unbelievable scale and control of the "National Protection Army".

"How-How is it possible?"

"How could the National Protection Army have jumped to the Seven Seas Space Zone in such an organized way? It's… It's an impossible miracle without the navigation of space gates!"

"Why is the National Protection Army so huge? Is this really a temporary fleet jumbled by pirate gangs?"

"The starships only less than fifty thousand kilometers away from us are like cold meteoroids. We did not detect them at all in the beginning. What kind of stealth modules are they using? What kind of crew is maneuvering those 'invisible' starships?"

All the captains who joined the battle formation just now had a headful of questions.

But they did not have the courage to ask any of them.

It was because they discovered that they had been separated and divided by the "National Protection Army" neatly. Almost every starship was surrounded by a couple of Boss Bai's starships, which were showing the bloodstained tusks to them blatantly, as if they could rip the newcomers apart in ten seconds.

The newcomers were like the members of the daring team, or cannon fodder, who were forced to lead the charge by the fully-armed elite soldiers in their behind in the ancient battles.

If they backed off, the sharpest blades would chop their heads without any hesitation.

In such a massive and chaotic battlefield, destroying their minor starships was even easier than crumbling a few ants.

Whether or not they believed in Boss Bai's bulls*it, the captains were left with no choice except to walk on with him until the dead end.

"Good."

Boss Bai's creepy laughter echoed again. "It seems that you are all smart people who understand 'silence is gold'. I believe that our cooperation will be very pleasant. You will not only survive but also be complimented by Commander Yun. You may even have the privilege to meet Commander Yun himself in an unexpected place!

"Now, let's go and gather more lone warships into our battle formation. Remember, only when new starships join and are placed at the front will you be moved to the second echelon. The more starships join us, the more behind you will be in the battle formation, and, naturally, the safer you will be.

"So, I do not need to explain to you what you should do, right?"

The whole of the next day, Boss Bai cruised at the edge of the Seven Seas Space Zone.

Under his command, the weird coalition fleet was like a shark, which had a keen nose but was almost transparent, allowing it to avoid the hunting fleets of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors from hundreds of thousands of kilometers away while gathering more and more lone warships.

Most of the lone warships did not have any objection to his command. After he showed his high-level permission in the "Flying Lightning Super Command Chain", they volunteered to open their Spiritual Nexus and mainframe crystal processors, before they joined Boss Bai's battle formation obediently.

It was exactly like rolling a snowball. At first, it took great efforts, but as Boss Bai's fleet took shape and expanded into one of the largest temporary fleets that had gathered most smoothly, more and more starships of the attackers were attracted to him. In the end, he did not even need to search for them, and the starships were already approaching him in a hurry.

Naturally, there were indeed certain disobedient "rogues" that he countered.

Some of the warships of the warlords who thought they were powerful, or the warships of the experts who had multiple Colossi, or those controlled by the Immortal Cultivators of the four Kurfürst families, despised Boss Bai who was just a space pirate that had only risen recently, unwilling to follow his command.

However, most of them, after reading the secret orders issued by Yun Xuefeng in person and witnessing Boss Bai's demonstrational barrage, decided to swallow their pride to avoid trouble and succumbed.

By the time the fierce battle of the first day was about over, the starships under Boss Bai's command had been multiplied. It was not until then that he encountered a real tricky target.

The target was not one starship but another temporary fleet that had just been temporarily gathered.

The commander of the fleet was an expert from the Song family, one of the four Kurfürst families.

Coincidentally, the expert of the Song family was also a marquess of the Imperium, who was known as "Marquess Hushan"!

Chapter 2564: Tiger at Dawn!

"What 'National Protection Army'? What 'Boss Bai'? Who the f*ck do you think you are? You are asking a marquess of the Imperium to follow your command?"

On the bridge of "Alloy Virus", the hologram of a tall and cocky noble was displayed in front of Boss Bai.

Song Changlie, Marquess Hushan, was exactly like Li Wuji, Marquess Yongchun, two months ago. Raising his head high and looking at Boss Bai with his nostrils, he said in despise, "Are you too blind to see the emblem on my flagship, or are you mentally deranged because you have taken wrong medications?"

Song Changlie, Marquess Hushan, had every reason to be arrogant.

His confidence came not just from his identity as a center noble of the four families, or his title as a marquess of the Imperium, or the three Colossi on his flagship "Tiger Roar", but also from his remarkable command ability and reasonably good luck, which allowed him to avoid the hunting and sweeping of the defenders in one day and gather a thousand loose starships to form his own temporary fleet.

While it was slightly less impressive than Boss Bai's side, Song Changlie did not believe that Boss Bai would be bold enough to challenge him with those untrained soldiers.

If the guy dared to challenge him, so be it. He still had three Colossi, all used by his most trusted family members. He was not afraid at all!

"Please calm down, Marquess Hushan."

Faced with the guy's haughty reply, Boss Bai retained remarkable humbleness. He said rather hesitantly, "It is the order from Commander Yun that I must gather the loose starships at the edge of the space zone and condense them into a battle formation as soon as possible to attack the Seven Seas Grand Market at the center of the space zone.

"Look, it has been a whole day since we jumped over, and Marquess Hushan's team is still wandering at the edge so far away from the central battlefield. It doesn't seem very appropriate, does it?"

"Are you insinuating that I am a coward?"

Song Changlie's eyes immediately grew sharp. Pointing at the tip of Boss Bai's nose, he cursed aloud, "You damn prisoner, bold slave, and disobedient dog, do you have any idea who you are talking to and whose tactics you are questioning?

"Don't presume that I do not know your background. Commander of the bulls*it 'National Protection Army'? Hahahaha. It's truly the greatest joke! You were just a bunch of humble space pirates and stray dogs who lived in panic all day. Yet, taking advantage of the competition between the four families and the reformists, you have assumed the skins of humans and pretend that you are humans too.

"Remember who you are. A dog is always a dog. It cannot change into a human because it wears the skin of the 'commander of the National Protection Army'! I have my own plans about how to fight the battle. It is not the place of a dog like you to comment on my decision!

"Besides, hehe, you accused me of cruising at the edge of the space zone for a whole day, gathering the fleet instead of launching an attack at the Seven Seas Grand Market? Haven't you been doing the same?"

"If I may let Marquess Hushan know—"

Boss Bai was truly a man of manners. Looking at Li Wuji who was secretly chuckling in a corner, he said unhurriedly, "I was doing it under the direct instruction from Commander Yun…"

"Then, let Yun Xuefeng speak to me in person!"

Song Changlie interrupted him rudely before Boss Bai finished. He sneered and said, "Don't presume that I do not know what's on Yun Xuefeng's mind. He's hoping that the fleets of the other families will lead the charge and die with the desperate rebels of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors before he jumps out and claims all the benefits, isn't he?

"Hehe, does Yun Xuefeng really think that other people are idiots who will risk their lives for him? Help me forward my reply to your 'Commander Yun'. Since he is eager for accomplishments, why doesn't he lead the charge with his 'Landslide'? Isn't he one of the 'two pearls of the expedition army' on par with Lei Chenghu? I'm sure that the rebels of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors would immediately kneel and surrender the moment they hear his name, right? Haha, hahahaha!"

"Marquess Hushan, if you insist on that, it will be rather tricky for me."

Boss Bai sighed rather helplessly. "You've seen Commander Yun's secret orders just now and testified my permission in the 'Flying Lightning Super Command Chain', right? Under such circumstances, if you are still unwilling to listen to my command, according to the martial law of the Imperium…"

"The martial law?"

Song Changlie couldn't help but burst into laughter. He laughed so hard that his face was red and his tears were almost flowing out. Patting his belly, he said, "Forgive me, but the 'Flying Lightning Super Command Chain' system installed on my warship, as well as all the communication units on all the starships, have been compromised in the space jump. So, it is impossible for us to be merged into the unified battle network of…'Commander Bai'. Why don't we keep cruising in this space zone until all the communication units are recovered?"

After a long silence, Boss Bai looked at Li Wuji.

Li Wuji was almost laughing aloud.

Delightedly, he walked to Boss Bai's side as well as Song Changlie's sight.

"Marquess Yongchun?"

Song Changlie was slightly dazed. "Why are you not on your own flagship but on your subordinate's? Where's your fleet?"

Marquess Hushan immediately grew alert, but it was already too late.

"You will know it very soon, Marquess Hushan."

At the same time, thousands of streaks of gold brilliance burst out from the rear of Song Changlie's fleet, piercing into the rather rigorous battle formation like scorching, glittering spears. Countless turbulences of destruction were raised!

As it turned out, Boss Bai paved the lone warships that he subdued in the past day in the front, distracting and deceiving Song Changlie.

The main force of the real "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs", on the other hand, detoured to the rear of Song Changlie's fleet, launching an attack when the guy was most arrogant and unprepared.

Song Changlie could never have dreamed that Boss Bai, a humble space pirate, was bold enough to fight him under such circumstances.

When the destructive mystic rays glowed around his flagship, what they illuminated was his dumbfounded face that was beaded with sweat.

Song Changlie was perhaps a qualified, experienced commander.

But what he commanded right now was not entirely his own subordinates. Most of the starships were the random starships that had been temporarily jumbled.

Those people did not have a high fighting will in the first place or any cooperation with him. Since even he did not foresee Boss Bai's sudden attack, it was even less possible for those people to be braced for it.

The combat ability of the "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs" was multiple times higher than the temporarily jumbled fleet, and it was attacking all of a sudden. How could the enemy put up any resistance?

Immediately, Song Changlie's fleet collapsed like a fortress made of glass. The Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs split into dozens of transparent scalpels that were as thin as paper, conducting the most accurate dissection. Very soon, Song Changlie's fleet was separated.

The dreamy scene was not only beyond the expectation of the soldiers in Song Changlie's fleet but also took the fleet commanders who joined Boss Bai's fleet in the past day unprepared. They did not expect that the mission Yun Xuefeng gave to Boss Bai was to fight the people on their own side. Dumbfounded and in a trance, they completely lost the ability of thinking.

"What are you waiting for? Those people are not connected to our command chain. They are not 'allies' at all. Lock onto Song Changlie's 'Tiger Roar' and blow it up!"

Boss Bai commanded all the starships that were forced to join him. "This is the secret mission that 'Commander Yun' has entrusted to us. We are the supervisors of the battle. All the cowards who waver in the battle shall be executed immediately!

"You don't need to have any concerns. Just fight with me. All the consequences will be taken care of by Commander Yun or even bigger shots than him!

"Otherwise, just use your idiotic head to think, how could I have been bold enough to attack Marquess Hushan as a space pirate?

"Besides, I've sent the information of all your starships to Song Changlie just now. He has remembered your names! If you don't kill him now, are you waiting for him to return to the Song family and take down each one of you later?

"One wrong decision will turn you into bones, and one right decision will make you a lord. You don't have a choice at all. Let's go and kill!"

In Boss Bai's almost twisted roars, all the fleet commanders seemed to have been woken up from a dream. Thinking quickly for a moment, they made the same decision.

Of course, the decision was in the spirit of the Immortal Cultivators' usual selfishness.

"Let's go and kill!"

The random warships on Boss Bai's side seemed to have been injected with excitants. Even the warships were on fire as they charged at the broken battle formation of Song Changlie's fleet fearlessly.

The random warships in Song Changlie's fleet, on the other hand, also received the message that Boss Bai sent over via the public channel half a minute later.

"Song Changlie, Marquess Hushan, has violated the martial law by wavering in the battle. His crime is unpardonable!

"In the name of 'Commander Yun', I am inspecting the battlefield and supervising the situation, and I have been authorized to enforce punishment before reporting it to the superior!

"Song Changlie's head will hit the ground soon. Are you going to resist idiotically and die together with him?

"Get away from Song Changlie's command, exit the battlefield, lower your spiritual shield, and wait to be regrouped into my fleet. This is your last chance. Those who stay in the hotspot three minutes later will die together with Song Changlie!"

Those random warships were dizzy at first because of the ferocious barrage of the "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs". They were then scared out of their wits by Boss Bai's most cold and cruel declaration.

Many smart Immortal Cultivators had immediately realized that it was a power struggle between the Yun family and the Song family or even an extension of the competition about the choice of the new emperor. There was simply too much involved in it.

A fight between the Yun family and the Song family was not something that the small fries like them were qualified to participate in.

They were not connected to Song Changlie's command chain, and they were not commanded by him until several hours ago. Seeing that Boss Bai was obviously ten times stronger than Song Changlie, how could they die for Song Changlie for no good reason?

Those who suited their actions to the times were wise. They might as well listen to the warning and save their own lives first!

Chapter 2565: Iron Ball I!

Faced with the scourge of the ferocious barrage, those starships boosted their power units to the maximum without any exception, fleeing to all directions in panic.

Boss Bai's fleet did leave the escaping starships alone and focused the firepower on the starships at the center of the enemy's battle formation.

The starships that were lingering in Song Changlie's battle formation mostly belonged to the Song family, the Li family, and the Dongfang family. Some of them were even the warships of the Yun family.

The warships of the Yun family were the most befuddled ones of all, having no idea if Boss Bai was really supervising the battle under Yun Xuefeng's instruction.

However, the situation was too emergent for them to think or decide. Before they could react, they had been locked onto by the warships of the Song family that were right next to them. Even though the fire of destruction had not been spurted out of the warships of the Song family yet, their coldness and hostility couldn't have been more apparent.

When two warships had locked onto each other, their mutual destruction would only be a matter of time.

There was no telling who opened fire first, but all in all, before Boss Bai's fleet reached near, the warships of the Song family and those of the Yun family had already started to fight.

The warships of the Li family and the Dongfang family, on the other hand, decided to not get involved in the mess after a brief hesitation. Together with other random warships, they fled as quickly as possible, implying that they had given up their resistance and were waiting to be recruited by Boss Bai.

"How dare you!"

In only half an hour, an enormous fleet had fallen apart, and the starships were dancing and sparkling like burning fireflies. Song Changlie was fuming in fury as he watched it.

He tried to stop the starships that were escaping from him, but almost all the starships shut down the communication with him and ran further and further without looking back.

Even some of the warships of the Song family had secretly left the hotspot, abandoning him!

To this moment, Song Changlie still felt that he was in a most absurd nightmare, finding it impossible to believe what was happening before his eyes at all.

Boss Bai was merely a lowly space pirate who was only good at adulating his master Li Wuji, and Li Wuji was only good at adulating Yun Xuefeng. That was how the guy became the commander of "National Protection Army".

The starships around him should've been temporarily gathered and couldn't have been the original team of the "National Protect Army". How could he command them so neatly, and how could they have such a high combat ability?

Besides, how was he intrepid enough to launch lethal attacks on a marquess of the Imperium from the Song family? Was he not scared of the thunderous fury of the Song family? Was he not afraid that the alliance of a million starships would fall apart, and the trust and cooperation among the four families would suddenly be gone?

It was a battlefield of paramount importance. The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' army, which had been cornered, was ready to launch a desperate counterattack anytime. How was he cold enough to do this?

Song Changlie couldn't have been more baffled.

"You're out of your mind. You're truly out of your mind!"

Song Changlie roared, "After I catch you, skin you, and toss you to Yun Xuefeng's front, let's see how you are going to explain it to the four families!"

Together with his roar, the three Colossi, Song Changlie's last trump cards, finally showed up. In three streaks of rushing brilliance, they darted at "Alloy Virus", Boss Bai's flagship!

"Hehehehe, even though you control more starships than I do and you've launched a shameless ambush, what can you do after I capture your flagship and dig you and Li Wuji out of your nest?"

Song Changlie was aware that Li Wuji had a Colossus.

However, Li Wuji was not purely a battle-type Immortal Cultivator. He was more of an admin-type one who was better at the conspiracies within the family as well as the construction and command of fleets.

The users of the three Colossi under Song Changlie's command, on the other hand, were out-and-out battle-type Immortal Cultivators. They were among the tough warriors even in the entire Imperium!

In the past half an hour, Song Changlie did not idle by but had been trying to locate and lock onto "Alloy Virus". At this moment, after the enemy was clearly located, he released the fatal weapons!

The light from the three Colossi immediately eclipsed the battlefield that was brimming with fireworks.

Every Colossus was unleashing the tides of spiritual energy tens of thousands of meters tall, which brushed the detection magical equipment of all the starships in huge noises.
