Even the fleeing starships, after sensing the arrival of the Colossi, couldn't help but slow down their escape, doubting if the scales of victory would be tipped to Song Changlie's side.

The starships that Boss Bai had browbeat into his side did not have a strong fighting will either. Seeing that the Colossi were coming, they hurried to back off, exposing "Alloy Virus" to the enemy. Or rather, "Alloy Virus" had pressed forward on its way and exposed itself to the three aggressive Colossi!

From "Alloy Virus" and the few starships of the "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs", tremendous battle puppets that were specifically designed for the zero gravity in space were deployed.

Those battle puppets did not have hands, feet, or any limb. They were purely round iron balls that were dashing briefly with the dozens of spouts all over their bodies. However, they could also be assembled with each other through the magnetic field. The form came from one of the "Hyper Spirits" that Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan designed. Li Yao and Long Yangjun had once fought similar battle puppets in space.

Xiaoming and Wenwen originated from the "Hyper Spirits" in the first place. Naturally, they inherited the relevant design and construction data. After being comprehensively upgraded and strengthened by the Fist King, they became the new generation of "Colossus Killer"!

Thousands of unattractive iron balls, congregating into a bloodcurdling torrent, swept at the three Colossi.

The great headache that once haunted Li Yao and Long Yangjun now harassed the Immortal Cultivators of the Song family.

The iron balls were massive in number and extremely fast. Also, they had spouts all over their bodies, and there were no patterns in their movements at all, making it terribly difficult for them to be locked onto or destroyed.

Also, the powerful magnetic fields that they brought attacked the Colossi like a storm from all directions. Sometimes, they were even attached to the surface of the Colossi, unleashing intense electric arcs, releasing strident interferential waves, or even exploding directly. All the three Colossi were in a flurry as they dealt with the enemy.

In the meantime, Boss Bai's starships were not idling by. Including Alloy Virus and Steel Mouse, the main warships with strong firepower locked onto the three Colossi and almost emptied their entire arsenal.

Thousands of surging fiery snakes piled an overwhelming fire wall that even the magnificent Colossi could not pass!

It was the ideology that supported the "Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships" that the extreme firepower, together with battle puppets and drones commanded by the state-of-the-art artificial intelligence, was able to compete with the Colossi. At this moment, it was preliminarily tested on the Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs.

Of course, Colossi were still the invincible ultimate weapons in space.

Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King's attempts in "unmanned war" were still in the phase of exploration and experimentation too.

The firepower suppression from Alloy Virus and Steel Mouse was also far from the Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships.

Therefore, after paying a price of almost half of their spiritual energy reserve, the three Colossi still tore apart the seeming indestructible fire wall and appeared before Alloy Virus magnificently.

"You're screwed!"

On "Tiger Roar", Song Changlie gnashed his teeth and bashed the control panel so hard that it was sparking and smoking while his own hands were bleeding too. "Bai and Li, both of you are screwed!"

"The 'Iron Ball I Fully-Automatic War System' is indeed still deeply flawed!"

On the bridge of Alloy Virus, Xiaoming and Wenwen, who existed in all the broken battle puppets as well as in the command chain at the same time, sighed gloomily. "Although our idea to use the computational ability of the idle crystal processors on all the starships and control all the iron balls remotely is good, we have underestimated the interferences on the battlefield and the instability of the network. At least 40% of the iron balls cannot be controlled precisely. Even those which are controlled precisely are too rigid. They are not half as agile and swift as real human beings."

"Let's take it slowly. Even the information lives have to abide by the law of evolution too. 'Iron Ball II' that is still being experimented with will certainly be better than 'Iron Ball I'. Perhaps it will be dozens of iterations later when the battle puppets or drones of such a model can be finalized!"

The Fist King's calm voice came over. "It took hundreds of years for me to evolve into who I am today. I don't mind waiting for another several hundred years. Time is the most worthless thing of all for us."

"So, are you done chattering?"

Long Yangjun's impatient voice chimed in. "If you're done, it will be my turn, right?"

"Thank you for your trouble, Aunt Long."

Xiaoming and Wenwen said, "We will control the remaining iron balls to provide fire support and battlefield assistance. It will be the best if the intactness of the three Colossi is ensured as much as possible. Those Colossi will be very suitable for the experts from the federation, like 'Sword Maniac' Yan Liren, after preliminary modifications! I believe that they will hone his unstoppable blade and make it even sharper than before, right?"

"Yan Liren…"

Long Yangjun smiled. "I had almost forgotten about the 'Sword Maniac' if you didn't mention him. I truly wonder, how strong will the guy be after he is equipped with a more advanced Colossus? Will it be possible for him to slash the gods in the universe in one attack?"

Smiling, Long Yangjun stepped onto the battlefield casually in her super Colossus,

"Heavenly Crystal",

When she appeared behind the Colossi of the three Immortal Cultivators without a sound under the cover of the overwhelming iron balls, the battle did not have any more suspense.

Chapter 2566: It's All Commander Yun's Order!

Everybody on the battlefield saw the most unbelievable scene.

"Heavenly Crystal", the transparent Colossus that seemed to be carved out of a whole piece of diamond, danced the splendid death of dance while it dragged the other three Colossi into its rhythm.

The three Colossi were like marionettes that were under its control, cramping and colliding with each other.

Countless iron balls were darting close from all directions and attached to the three Colossi. Other than locking the Colossi with the magnetic fields, they also spurted out highly-sticky glue that stuck themselves to the Colossi, as if countless burdens that weighed thousands of kilograms had been added to them.

The burdens were also spurting out flames of light to all directions, making the Colossi completely out of control at the mercy of the iron balls.

In the end, "Heavenly Crystal" dug open their spiritual residence and blew the users inside into smithereens without any mercy!

The heated battlefield a moment ago turned into disheartening silence.

Of course, it was Song Changlie who was disheartened.

"You-You-You—"

Song Changlie stepped back and fell to his own command chair. His lips trembled nonstop, and he could not even say a whole sentence.

"Boss Bai, what are you trying to do?"

His voice was full of confusion and desperation as he wailed, "I am Marquess Hushan of the Imperium. How dare you do this? You-You can't kill me. If you kill me, you will be killed too! There's no way that you can explain it to the four families!"

"Idiot, do you still not understand what's going on?"

Boss Bai's hologram put on a smile of disdain and mockery. "Everything I do is exactly the instruction of Commander Yun. Do you think that the fleets of a hundred Sectors have jumped to this tiny Seven Seas Space Zone at the cost of tremendous labor and resources just in order to annihilate a bunch of insignificant rebels?

"Hehe, the rebelling reformists are just rats and cockroaches that are not worth mentioning. If the purpose is to annihilate them, they would've been obliterated by nothing more than a gentle sneeze!

"Commander Yun's real purpose, however, is to grasp the army of a million starships in his own hands with the great opportunity and transform it into a loyal, invincible, obedient force that only listens to the command of himself and the Yun family!"

"Ah!"

Song Changlie's eyes bulged as he blurted out, "How dare Yun Xuefeng? How dare the Yun family?"

"If you don't accept fate's gift, you will only be punished by fate. Now that such a great opportunity has been present, why daren't Commander Yun?"

Boss Bai grinned hideously. "After the battle, while the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors will be gone, hundreds of thousands of starships, including the sixteen Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships, will also belong to Commander Yun and the Yun family. By then, who in the entire universe can compete with Commander Yun and the Yun family?

"Hehe, after I make such great contributions for Commander Yun and the Yun family, I will be qualified to be knighted myself. Why can't I explain it?"

"You're out of your mind!"

Song Changlie pointed at the light beam, his hair rising. There was no telling whether he was angry or shocked. "You, Yun Xuefeng, and everybody else in the Yun family are all out of your mind! You… You do not care about the big picture at all!"

"The big picture? Hehe, if the Song family truly cares about the big picture, why are you competing with the Yun family about the choice of the new emperor? If the Song family truly cares about the big picture, why has your fleet been wandering at the edge of the space zone without any intention of marching at the Seven Seas Grand Market? Are you not waiting for Commander Yun's fleet to crash and bleed first before the Song family cleans up the mess? Furthermore, if Commander Yun doesn't crash and bleed, you may even stab him in the back!"

Boss Bai spat and smiled. "Alright, you've wasted enough time. I know that you are secretly preparing escape capsules. It's useless. You know too much now. Go to hell!"

The hundred starships that belonged to the "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs" opened fire at the same time. The ten thousand rays of destruction ignited the dark space again. Powerful spiritual waves ripped ripples in the vacuum, making it look like an ocean that was in the middle of a storm!

Under the collective attack of ten thousand pillars of light, the spiritual shield of "Tiger Roar" endured only half a minute before it turned from green to orange, and from orange to deep red, until it fell apart like glass that was full of cracks.

Without the protection of the spiritual shield, it was impossible for the armor that was made of superalloys inlaid with reflective porcelains to resist the burning when the temperature of almost ten thousand degrees was focused on one point.

Furthermore, the real-entity weapons, including the high-energy particles, the explosion bullets shot from the railguns, and the space torpedoes, hit the target too.

Fireballs burst out like a supernova outbreak. At first, it was just a tiny, insignificant spot of light, but it expanded into a gargantuan object hundreds of meters in diameter in the blink of an eye, enshrouding "Tiger Roar" without leaving a single gap. The plate armor that was several meters thick was ripped apart easily, and tremendous materials, fuel, and even crew members inside were sucked out. Congregating into an overwhelming torrent of debris, they were spurted into the infinite vacuum, making "Tiger Roar" look like a heavily-wounded and bleeding tiger.

The paralysis of "Tiger Roar" marked the end of the brief battle.

Nine out of ten starships chose to surrender to Boss Bai. They did not care who their commander was in the first place, as long as he was enough to bring them better chances of survival and more trophies.

Therefore, the commanders of those starships almost cheered when they joined Boss Bai's side.

Of course, they were more or less nervous since Boss Bai had proved brutal enough to kill a marquess of the Imperium.

However, despite their concerns, Boss Bai would certainly not hesitate to kill the unimportant small fries like them if he was bold enough to kill Marquess Hushan. Who of them was willing to die with Song Changlie?

Only part of the starships that belonged to the Song family, after witnessing the destruction of "Tiger Roar", fled further away to the edge of the Seven Seas Space Zone desperately.

What they were using were the most rapid attack starships that were fast and inconspicuous. When the two parties were a million kilometers away from each other, it was very difficult to catch up with them again.

Boss Bai did not intend to catch them but simply allowed the starships to flee. He seized every moment to regroup the subordinates that he had just subdued.

What he did not know was that, while Song Changlie was unable to get away from the vortex of destruction on "Tiger Roar", he had sent the record of his conversation with Boss Bai to the other starships of the Song family, which had brought it out of the battlefield.

Or perhaps he knew it, but he did not care about it at all.

"Alright, the size of our fleet has reached the limit. If we continue to expand, it will be beyond what we can control and command."

Boss Bai said to Xiao Lishui, his chief of staff, with a smile, "Give the order that all the starships that just surrendered to us should clean the battlefield. Ask them to blow up the debris of 'Tiger Roar' into pieces. Make sure that everyone's hands are stained with blood.

"Whoever dawdles and dare not do it, let the others blow up his starship too.

"I want the most complete regrouping finished in three hours. Then, we will set off and search for our most beloved 'Commander Yun'!"

…

On "Landslide", the flagship-in-general of the coalition fleet of the four families, Yun Xuefeng was pacing back and forth anxiously on the diagonals of the bridge, making the 100,037th step in the past twenty-four hours.

At the center of the bridge, the hologram of the Seven Seas Space Zone was still covered in the dense fog, and the result of the battle was still nowhere to be seen.

A war in reality was entirely different from a game.

In a game, the commanders could learn everything on the battlefield like omniscient gods. Even though they could not see the numbers and movements of the enemies, they could at least see their own forces clearly. Their every order could be delivered to the battle units in the frontline instantly, and the battle units would be absolutely loyal to them and carry out their orders without any hesitation.

A war in reality was far from that.

The universe was so vast that the battlefield was billions of kilometers in diameter, and too many battle units had been scattered in it. Due to the incessant interferences from the enemy, the communication between two battle units was already very difficult, not to mention the cross-connection between Yun Xuefeng's flagship and the other battle units.

As a matter of fact, it had taken Yun Xuefeng thirty hours, but he still hadn't reached out to all of the starships under his command.

The status icon that implied the connection of every starship to the command chain showed 70% of the starships were still dark. It meant that they were still not connected to the battle network yet. There was no telling at which point in the dark universe they were struggling in, if they hadn't been knocked down by the defenders.

Yun Xuefeng felt that he was a lone traveler sitting in the middle of a dark fog, waiting for the starry lights to be ignited around him. Every light was a warship under his command, and he did not know how much longer he had to wait before he gathered enough strength to attack the Seven Seas Grand Market.

The good news was that "Landslide", his flagship-in-general, had finally found a peaceful and quiet rendezvous that had not been discovered by the defenders yet. It could stay here and summon more starships to join it.

Besides, only two Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships were caught in fierce battles after being detected by the defenders. Of the other fourteen Adamantine Super Arsenal warships, ten had been reached. They were all in perfect condition and were summoning the shield warships, the arsenal warships, and the comprehensive supply ships around, condensing into powerful Adamantine Fleets.

Yun Xuefeng estimated that as soon as three to four Adamantine Fleets took shape, he would be able to march toward the Seven Seas Grand Market and suppress the defenders.

The bad news was that, although the ten Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships had been reached, the commanders of those ultimate warships were much more undetermined than he expected. Almost everybody was complaining that abundant garrisons of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had appeared around him. They reported that they were about to be engaged in fierce battles and were doing their best to escape. Therefore, it was temporarily impossible for them to cooperate with the commander's operation in the next step.

At first, Yun Xuefeng thought that those Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships did meet unexpected enemies.

However, nine out of ten commanders of the Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships that he had reached said the same, which almost made him burst into fury. How on earth could the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors have so many starships to attack ten Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships at the same time?

Chapter 2567: Infuriated Song Yushi

"Marquess Weiyuan, where is your fleet, and why is it still not coming close to me? What? It is still stalled by the main fleets of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, which probably has more than two thousand warships? You cannot get rid of them but have to ask the allies nearby to reinforce you as soon as possible? You-You-You—

"Then, pack the real-time battle data on the battlefield and send them to me. As soon as I confirm the number and level of the hostile warships, I'll immediately dispatch reinforcements to you!

"What? Due to the strong interference that the enemy imposes on you, your network is on the brink of collapse, and you cannot send out any data? You are even unable to sustain the 'Flying Lightning Super Command Chain' anymore, and you are about to exit the system and fight on your own? Marquess Weiyuan? Marquess Weiyuan!

"Leader Peng, where is your fleet? What? You were at Coordinates 233.343.445 six hours ago, where you gathered more than five hundred starships, and you are still lingering there now? What are you doing exactly, and why haven't you approached me yet? What? You just suffered an assault from the main force of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, and you are in dire need of immediate maintenance due to the heavy losses, which will probably take seventy-two hours? A*shole, tell me, where did the main force of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors show up when you had allies all around you? Have they mastered such advanced short-distance space jump technologies that they are able to be teleported right above your head?

"Elder Zhao, please lead your fleet to join 'Landslide' immediately. I repeat, for the sake of the Imperium, hurry and approach 'Landslide' with your fleet so that we can fight together!"

After every five minutes, Yun Xuefeng would refresh the fleets that were connected most recently on the command chain and yell at the temporary commanders of those fleets, demanding them to approach him as soon as possible or to directly march at the Seven Seas Grand Market when enough starships had been gathered.

It was a pity that most of the temporary commanders hesitated, replying that it was beyond their abilities. Their loyalty to the Imperium, the four families, and to Commander Yun was certainly unquestionable, and they were fearless of death. The problem was that their starships were either seriously twisted and damaged during the jump or hit by the defenders brutally immediately after the jump. At this moment, they were still dealing with the hunting fleets of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, unable to get away at all.

Together with their convincing bulls*it, the bridges of many starships were filled with smoke and even minor explosions, as if they were truly in such a fierce battle that even the flagship had to charge in person.

However, when Yun Xuefeng requested those people to send over the real-time data on the battlefield so that he could learn the battle better, they simply gave excuses like their Spiritual Nexus had been torn apart due to the severe interferences and it was barely possible to keep the basic communication, let alone delivering astronomical information.

When Yun Xuefeng pursued further, those people simply cut off the communication quickly, not obeying Yun Xuefeng's command at all.

Yun Xuefeng was so mad that he was almost smashing the dashboard into pieces. He really did not see it coming that those wretched Immortal Cultivators would be preserving their own strength with no regard to the big picture in such a shameless way!

Thankfully, even though other troops were indifferent, the Yun family's own troops were still loyal and tough.

Of the four Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships that belonged to the Yun family, the other two apart from his "Landslide" had been reached.

The two Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships had also attracted starships of a thousand different models, which formed two temporary fleets around them and were marching at Yun Xuefeng's coordinates fast.

Besides the Adamantine Super Arsenal Warship, the other starships of the Yun family had also been given a very high permission in the "Flying Lightning Super Command Chain". They were, without an exception, the centers of the temporary fleets. Managing dozens of temporary fleets large and small, they joined him from all directions.

As long as those fleets were in position, he would never fail. By then, he would be able to fight a neat battle of annihilation with only the strength of the Yun family and claim all the credit. The guys of the Song family, the Li family, and the Dongfang family would all be dumbfounded and regretful by then!

While Yun Xuefeng was fantasizing, a series of hasty messages suddenly came from the communication channel. It was the commander of another Adamantine Super Arsenal Warship who wanted to speak to him.

"Song Yushi?"

Yun Xuefeng found it hard to believe.

Song Yushi was an elder of the Song family, an earl of the Imperium, an expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage, an experienced fleet commander, and an extraordinary person who was one generation senior to Yun Xuefeng. He was also a strong competitor for the commander-in-general of the four families' coalition fleet.

From many perspectives, Song Yushi, as a battle-tested general, was even more suitable as the commander of the battle than Yun Xuefeng.

However, during the competition for the commander-in-general, Yun Xuefeng was determined to get it, while Song Yushi acted rather indifferent to the outcome. Eventually, he simply gave the job away to Yun Xuefeng.

At first, Yun Xuefeng felt good and thought that Song Yushi's age had worn out his ambitions, making him scared to compete with a "rising star" like himself.

After he did become the challenging but unrewarding "commander-in-general", and especially after he was faced with the mess and the rogue subordinates after he jumped to the Seven Seas Space Zone, he finally realized how hot the potato that he snatched voluntarily was, and how many thorns had been buried in it.

Song Yushi, on the other hand, did reach out to him immediately after the jump. He did not make up any lie that he had encountered massive hostile forces.

However, the fleet commander of the older generation of the four families told Yun Xuefeng coldly that he would search for the hunting fleets on the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' side after gathering a temporary fleet and fight them immediately in order to ease the pressure of the allies.

In the meantime, he would very likely keep radio silence and the Spiritual Nexus offline. He asked Yue Xuefeng not to contact him unless there was an emergence, and it was possible that he could not be reached even if Yun Xuefeng tried to.

"When Commander Yun was still being breastfed, I was already a general shedding my blood on the battlefield. I have my own plans to annihilate the insignificant rebels. Commander Yun might as well focus your attention on the starships of the Li family and the Dongfang family!"

Song Yushi simply slapped the blunt words on Yun Xuefeng's face.

Yue Xuefeng, however, could barely do anything about the old man who was more experienced than him, had more connections than him, and was as strong as him, if not better. He could only watch the guy's Adamantine Super Arsenal Warship disappearing among the glittering icons of the command chain.

Yun Xuefeng had thought that the guy would be hiding at the edge of the Imperium until the garrisons of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and him were exhausted in the battle. Out of his expectation, the old man had popped up on his own after only thirty hours. What was the meaning of that? Had his conscience got the better of him, and he was finally ready to show his loyalty to the Imperium?

While thinking, Yun Xuefeng activated the communication channel, allowing Song Yushi's hologram to emerge before him.

Song Yushi at this moment, however, did not have the slightest calmness as expected of an experienced general. Instead, he looked like a firecracker that would explode at first touch.

His hair and his beard were rising, as if hundreds of black blades had been stabbed to his head and his chin. Before the hologram took the full shape, he already pointed at Yun Xuefeng's nose and cursed aloud, "Yue Xuefeng, you are truly bold! I did not know that you were as insane as such! Are you out of your mind, or is the whole Yun family? Are you going against the other three families, planning the turn the universe upside down?"

Yun Xuefeng was utterly baffled under Song Yushi's saliva that almost shot through the light beam. After a long daze, he replied, "… Elder Song, what do you mean?"

"What do I mean? Are you still pretending?"

Song Yushi was more than infuriated. "You incited your 'National Protection Army' and Li Wuji's fleet to swallow the temporary fleet that Song Changlie, Marquess Hushan, had just regrouped. 'Tiger Roar', Song Changlie's flagship, was even blown into pieces, and none of the Immortal Cultivators, including Song Changlie himself, escaped! Yun Xuefeng, a great foe is ahead of us. As the commander of the coalition army, you have done such insane, unpardonable things. How are you going to explain yourself?"

"What?"

Ten thousand bolts of lightning seemed to be bursting out inside Yun Xuefeng's head, which completely went blank. He said subconsciously, "How-How is it possible? It must be a misunderstanding. Don't be instigated by the bad guys, Elder Song!"

"Misunderstanding? Instigated by the bad guys? Damn you!"

His eyes bloodshot, Song Yushi spat hard.

Song Changlie, Marquess Hushan, was not only a central member of the Song family but also a nephew to Song Yushi. The three Colossi on Song Changlie's flagship were also jumbled under his support. Never had he expected that they would be wasted for no good reason in a civil war. No wonder Song Yushi was so mad that he was almost on a rampage.

"Many Immortal Cultivators of the Song family who escaped told me in person that Song Changlie's warship was blown up because the 'National Protection Army' and Li Wuji's fleet attacked it from the front and the back together. Everybody knows that Li Wuji has been very close to you recently. He is your trusted subordinate. You also offered abundant resources to arm his 'National Protection Army'. Are you still denying that it was your instruction?"

Song Yushi gnashed his teeth and said, "I know Song Changlie's strength very well. If it were not for your strong support, how could Li Wuji and the Bai-surnamed space pirate have done anything to him?"

"W-Well… Please hold your anger, Elder Song. I really did not know anything about it!"

Yun Xuefeng's forehead was covered in a cold sweat. He understood the consequences of pissing off the older-generation experts like Song Yushi too well. Besides, such an insane thing as swallowing allies when a great enemy was ahead could not be justified at all.

Even more fuel had been added to the fire on the chaotic battlefield. Yun Xuefeng started to regret that he had been too close to Li Wuji and Boss Bai. Who could've thought that the two ambitious guys would be so senseless?

Chapter 2568: Your Words Against Mine

"Elder Song, the battlefield is in such a mess right now that countless starships from the hundred worlds have been messed up together. It is possible that the spies of the rebels infiltrated some of them. Therefore, misunderstandings can happen. All in all, I will definitely investigate the whole thing and give Marquess Hushan justice and Elder Song an explanation!"

Yun Xuefeng said sincerely, "I would also like to ask Elder Song to bear the big picture in mind and remember your loyalty to the Imperium. Don't do anything that will satisfy your foes and hurt your friends!"

"You'd better find out the truth soon and give me an explanation, or I'll ask the leader of your family for an explanation!"

Song Yushi sniffed and said, "If you understand the importance of solidarity, do not let such a thing happen! I'll give you half a day. If it turns out in the end that you were truly behind the incident, the Song family will withdraw from the battle and ask the Li family and the Dongfang family to do the same. The Yun family can fight on your own!"

"Well—"

Yun Xuefeng cursed aloud in his heart for a long time, but he did not know whether he should curse Song Yushi, or Li Wuji, or Boss Bai, or himself. His face more tightened than ever, he said in a hurry, "Rest assured, Elder Song, I'll certainly get to the bottom of it—"

Before he finished his sentence, Song Yushi had already cut off the communication, leaving the cold, dark light beam to him.

In the next second, his subordinates reported that most of the warships of the Song family in the command chain had left together with the loose warships that they had gathered. They cut off the communication with the flagship, and their whereabouts were unknown!

Yun Xuefeng was stunned for a long time. He finally couldn't help but punch the control panel before him hard and howled, "Song Yushi, just wait for it! Just wait for it!"

He rose abruptly and paced back and forth, his every step thudding on the ground like a hammer of ten thousand kilograms. In only half a minute, hundreds of white hair had appeared on his head. Wrinkles deep to the bones appeared on his face, making him look more terrifying than ever.

He was about to ask his subordinates to search for Li Wuji's fleet and the National Protection army when his subordinates reported that "Alloy Virus", the flagship of the National Protection Army, had been connected to the command chain and reached out to "Landslide"!

"Connect him!"

Yun Xuefeng shrieked and dashed to the communication dashboard.

He had a mouthful of imprecations that he intended to throw on Boss Bai and Li Wuji, but after he saw their looks on the light beam, he was so shocked that all his curses and roars were stuffed in his throat.

He saw that the bridge of Alloy Virus was full of smoke and burn marks. Including Boss Bai, most crew members had bandages and medical gelatins on their bodies. Some people were bleeding hard, but they were still working hard to extinguish the fire at the corner with foam tanks, having no time to treat themselves.

Boss Bai seemed to have just been dug out of a coal mine. Except for his teeth that were still white, his whole body was as dark as a gorilla, mixed with redness that was the dried blood.

Li Wuji, on the other hand, was a hundred times more miserable than Boss Bai. His whole body had been blackened, full of scabs that had been saturated with blood and body tissues. He was soaked in a medical cabin and floating in the middle of the deep green nutrition liquids. The curve nearby that represented his biophysical parameters was rollercoasting, implying that his life was as impossible to predict as a candle in the storm.

"Commander Yun, we've finally reached out to you! A terrible thing has happened! You must avenge me and Marquess Yongchun!"

Boss Bai said in tears, "My 'Alloy Virus' followed your instruction and gathered the loose warships to form a temporary fleet immediately after we jumped to the Seven Seas Space Zone. We searched for your coordinates and hoped to approach you.

"As it turned out, thanks to Commander Yun's bliss, although we did not find you, we found Marquess Yongchun's fleet. We joined our warships and continued the search for the flagship-in-general.

"Out of our expectation, we encountered the fleet of Song Changlie, Marquess Hushan. His permission in the 'Flying Lightning Super Command Chain' was lower than ours, and he was supposed to obey our command. We tried to persuade him too. Marquess Yongchun and he were both marquesses of the Imperium after all. If he was unwilling to listen to our command, we would not force him to. Our fleets could simply cooperate with and protect each other while we searched for 'Landslide' before we reported to you.

"However, to our surprise, Song Changlie was so arrogant that he despised Commander Yun, claiming that Commander Yun was just a crying baby and that you were not even qualified to wipe the shoes for Lei Chenghu.

"Yes, that's exactly what he said back then. He also said that the fleet of the Song family would never follow the command of Commander Yun. He was going to search for Song Yushi's fleet at the edge of the space zone, claiming that Song Yushi was the real commander of the Song family's fleet. There was no way that they were going to die with Commander Yun!"

"Enough!"

While he listened, Yun Xuefeng's face was as red as the color of pig liver. He roared, "What happened later?"

"Later—"

Boss Bai coughed hard and vomited yet another mouthful of thick black blood. He said, "If Song Changlie was only talking, there was nothing we could've done about it. After all, he was Marquess Hushan of the Imperium, and the Song family is best known for their haughtiness.

"However, not only was the guy running himself, but he was also encouraging the starships that Marquess Yongchun and I just gathered to join him and escape with him!"

"What?!"

Yun Xuefeng's eyes popped out as he shouted, "That's completely unacceptable!"

"If I may inform Commander Yun, if it were the fleet of my own or Marquess Yongchun's, they would never have flinched because we had been teaching them to return Commander Yun's favors and to serve the country loyally."

With a bitter face, Boss Bai said, "But at that time, what we commanded was only part of our own warships, and most of them were the troops of the warlords from the peripheral worlds.

"The private troops of the warlords did not have a strong fighting will in the first place. Many of them had been secretly in contact with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. In the first place, they were thinking to preserve their strength and claim the benefits later. Marquess Yongchun and I went through a lot of trouble to regroup them, but they were still sloppy, loose troops that could not stand instigation.

"Song Changlie spoke to those starships openly through the public channels that if they continued to follow me and Marquess Yongchun, they would certainly be asked to attack the Seven Seas Grand Market after we found Commander Yun. It would be a life-and-death battle when they fought the desperate, ferocious dogs. In the meantime, in order to eliminate the losses of your own people, Commander Yun—Commander Yun would certainly place the starships of the local forces at the vanguard. So, the odds that they were killed were 99%.

"Therefore, they might as well go with Marquess Hushan and lay low at the edge of the space zone for now, leaving the other dummies to fight and bleed hard. After the troops were all exhausted and the situation was clear, they could then make a decision unhurriedly.

"After he said that, most of the commanders of the starships under Marquess Yongchun and me somewhat wavered and were thinking to leave with him. How could Marquess Yongchun and I support Commander Yun to accomplish your success if we had no subordinates?

"I did not know that Marquess Hushan could've been so shameless. Even a prodigal space pirate like me knew that defending the Imperium was defending my own interests. Why were the Immortal Cultivators of the Song family so selfish and shortsighted? Aya, too shameless! I was infuriated… too infuriated, and I…"

"And you swallowed Song Changlie's fleet and even killed him?"

Narrowing his eyes, Yun Xuefeng asked coldly.

"Huh, how did you know, Commander Yun?"

Dumbfounded, Boss Bai lost his calmness and exclaimed.

"Huh…"

Frowning, Yun Xuefeng asked, "Bai Xingjian, just tell me the truth honestly. Who attacked first, your 'Alloy Virus' or Song Changlie's 'Tiger Roar'?"

"About that…"

Boss Bai lowered his head, somewhat in embarrassment, and said, "If we examine who attacked first, it might have been me, but I was only meaning to intimidate Marquess Hushan and force him to retreat. Then, we could just go our separate ways. How could I have been bold enough to challenge the experts of the Song family? Wouldn't that have been suicide? Therefore, most of my attacks were aimed at the vacuum instead of Tiger Roar.

"However, Marquess Hushan was so brutal that Tiger Roar's first barrage hit my Alloy Virus precisely."

Yun Xuefeng was not convinced. "Do you have any proof?"

Boss Bai looked at the messy bridge and said with a bitter smile, "Our mainframe crystal processors were all hit. The data are in a mess. For now, nothing can be retrieved. However, as long as Commander Yun supports Marquess Yongchun and I, we will certainly be able to 'find' some proof in half a day."

Yun Xuefeng naturally understood what Boss Bai meant perfectly. Thinking for a moment, he asked again, "Then, explain to me honestly how you knocked down 'Tiger Roar'. How could it have been possible with your combat ability?"

"Marquess Yongchun and I probably benefited from Commander Yun's unbelievable luck."

Boss Bai said solemnly, "Supposedly, my 'Alloy Virus' was just a ragged, rapid attack ship that was absolutely no match for 'Tiger Roar'. However, during the barrages of the two parties, one round of my attacks hit the arsenal or the fuel cabin of 'Tiger Roar' precisely, raising a serial explosion that shed a light of hope for Marquess Yongchun and I.

"Please don't blame us for being too brutal, Commander Yun. At that time, most of the loose warships were vacillating and even vaguely more inclined to Marquess Hushan. If we hadn't crossed the line and knocked down 'Tiger Roar' completely, it would've been Marquess Yongchun and I who were killed after those starships openly joined Marquess Hushan.

"In order to bring more troops to Commander Yun, we could only set an example for everybody else by knocking Tiger Roar down when it had a serial explosion. Naturally, Tiger Roar's counterattack before its death was rather remarkable too. Marquess Yongchun's flagship is now on the verge of total-loss, and my Alloy Virus has been reduced to such conditions too.

"I truly dare not think what miserable outcome it would've been if Marquess Yongchun and I had hesitated a while longer. Perhaps we would never be able to reach out to 'Landslide' or continue to serve Commander Yun!"

Chapter 2569: Victory Is Justice!

Seeing Boss Bai's look that was mixed with regret, adulation, and anger, Yue Xuefeng somehow became unconfident.

He paced back and forth for a long time, only to come up with a meaningless question. "Are you speaking the truth?"

"Absolutely, Commander Yun!"

Boss Bai was almost bashing his chest and swearing as he said, "How can I possibly have the courage to say anything untrue to Commander Yun? If it weren't because Marquess Hushan was too outrageous and attacked my flagship vehemently first, how could I have been bold enough to attack a marquess of the Imperium without reserve? Could I have benefited from it at all? Was I not worried about the thunderous revenge from the Song family later?

"If I should be bold enough to lie to Commander Yun about the matter, I would be pissing off the Song family and the Yun family at the same time. Then, however vast the sea of stars is, would there be a single piece of land that I could stand on? Please consider it carefully, Commander Yun!"

"Huh…"

Yun Xuefeng was untouched. Sullenly, he said, "But that is not what Song Yushi said. Part of Song Changlie's subordinates escaped. Their theory is the opposite of yours. They all claimed that you attacked first, and you meant to swallow Song Changlie's fleet!"

"Of course they would talk like that. Could they have spoken the truth that they were encouraging a lot of starships to escape with them only to be crushed later?"

Boss Bai almost cut his chest open to show Yun Xuefeng his heart. "Commander Yun, right now, none of us has solid proof, but please consider it with common sense. Whose words are more reasonable, mine or the words of Song Changlie's men? Did I really lose my mind and attack a marquess of the Imperium for no good reason other than to get myself killed later? It is utterly illogical!"

Yue Xuefeng pondered for a long time but still couldn't sort it through. Rather upset, he said, "I too know that the Immortal Cultivators of the Song family have always been arrogant. The disobedience of this Song Changlie is universally known. I do not believe that you would've pissed him off without a good reason.

"But still, it is a very important matter. After all, a marquess of the Imperium has been killed, by none other than his 'allies'. Song Yushi demanded that I give him an explanation. Otherwise, the morale of the army would be affected, and the alliance of the million starships might fall apart immediately. Therefore…

"Commander Yun, I am an uneducated man who doesn't understand rules. I'll just speak whatever is on my mind. If what I say is too farfetched, please forgive me, Commander Yun."

Boss Bai swallowed hard. As if he had made up his mind, he said straightforwardly, "As a matter of fact, while the four families' coalition army seems to be in one piece on the surface during the attack of the Seven Seas Grand Market, everybody in fact has their own plans, especially the people of the Song family, who never planned to let Commander Yun win the battle comfortably.

"If Commander Yun wins the battle unstoppably and invincibly, Commander Yun and the Yun family's prestige will soar ten times. How can the Song family compete with the Yun family in the choice of the next emperor then?

"Therefore, the Song family will certainly try to challenge Commander Yun with all kinds of excuses and create trouble for Commander Yun.

"The conflict between me and Song Changlie is a great excuse, but even without it, they can find other excuses as well!

"Now that a million starships have been mixed up in a mess, isn't it more than easy to make some minor frictions? I even suspect that Song Changlie intentionally came to make frictions by the order of Song Yushi, except that things went out of control in the end, and Song Changlie got burnt by the very fire he started.

"All in all, the Song family is causing trouble and slacking off, reluctant to approach Command Yun or follow your command, isn't it? If my guess is correct, there are few warships from the Song family around Commander Yun right now, right? All the starships of the Song family have fled far away and joined each other, haven't they?"

The more Boss Bai talked, the more excited Boss Bai was. He completely ignored Yun Xuefeng's face that was getting more and more gloomy.

"How dare you!"

The dark clouds on Yun Xuefeng's face finally exploded as he roared aloud.

Boss Bai shivered hard on the other side of the light beam. As if realizing that he had crossed the line just now, he hurried to apologize, "I was too presumptuous. Please forgive me, Commander Yun! My loyalty to Commander Yun can be proved by the sun and the moon! What I'm trying to say is that those people of the Song family cannot be depended on in any case. Only the Yun family's own troops, Marquess' fleet, and my 'National Protection Army' are the forces that Commander Yun can absolutely trust. Please take a look, Commander Yun. These are the forces that Marquess Yongchun and I gathered in the past day."

Boss Bai sent over the data of most starships that had been gathered under him. The numbers and names of the warships occupied the entire light beam, and they were still growing.

Of course, most warships of the "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs" were hidden.

"That's a lot!"

Rather surprised, Yun Xuefeng was more delighted than he was angry.

"There weren't as many in the beginning, but after we knocked down 'Tiger Roar', the starships under Marquess Hushan naturally switched to our side."

Boss Bai said, "Please consider this, Commander Yun. If we hadn't done anything at that time, at least 90% of the starships here would've been brought away by Marquess Hushan to build up the strength of the Song family. How could we have been confident to meet Commander Yun with only 10% of the starships? As their strength grew and ours diminished, the Song family would be much stronger than the Yun family on the battlefield. By then, who would still acknowledge Commander Yun as the 'commander' except for the loyal men like us?"

"About that…"

The moment he thought of Song Yushi's cockiness a moment ago, Yun Xuefeng felt that fury was burning up all the way from his heels to his skull.

When a great enemy was looming ahead, the Song family was still starting a civil war. That was too outrageous!

"Commander Yun, there's still one more thing that I don't know if I should say. It is the truth that works both in the circle of the space pirates and the world of the Immortal Cultivators—'victory is justice'."

Observing his face, Boss Bai said what was on Yun Xuefeng's mind, "Right now, your friction with Song Yushi is already on the table. If the battle is lost, or if it is won slowly with heavy losses, the Song family will not be satisfied however you explain to Song Yushi, even if you chop off my head and give it to Song Yushi to kick as a ball. They will still blame Commander Yun.

"After all, how much is my dirty and stinky head worth? Is it really something that Song Yushi and the leadership of the Song family cares about? Their real target is not me, but Commander Yun and the Yun family!

"Then, how many steps will you have to back off in order to satisfy Song Yushi and the whole Song family? Can the aftermath of the incident be resolved only by letting the candidate that the Song family supports be the emperor? Will Commander Yun or the Yun family be able to afford such a price?

"On the other hand, if Commander Yun wins the battle cleanly and quickly, who can possibly attack Commander Yun with such trivial matter? Song Changlie wavered in the battle, only to be executed by Commander Yun decisively, which significantly boosted morale. That's why such a glorious triumph was secured. If that is the case, it will be the Song family that will be caught in an awkward situation. Song Yushi will have to explain why the Immortal Cultivators of the Song family were all uncommitted cowards in the battle!"

"Enough!"

Yun Xuefeng's eyes were more profound than ever, like swamps where venom was surging. He immediately made up his mind, but he waved his hands and interrupted Boss Bai. "I have my own plans about what to do. It's not your place to comment!"

"Of course, I was being too talkative."

Boss Bai lowered his head deeply. "Commander Yun is already confident of the victory after seeing through the chaotic battle with your unparalleled wisdom. All I need to do is to stay loyal and carry out Commander Yun's orders obediently. How will Commander Yun ever need my stupid brain to resolve any trouble?"

"I did not realize that you were such a talent, Bai Xingjian. It seems that you truly have some capabilities for you to rise among the bloody space pirates in only a couple of years. Being the commander of the 'National Protection Army' is a waste of your gifts!"

Yun Xuefeng looked at Boss Bai thoughtfully and said, "Where are you right now, and how long will it take for you to meet with 'Landslide'?"

While talking, Yun Xuefeng sent over his own coordinates.

His eyes glowing profoundly, Boss Bai said, "If everything goes well, we will reach Commander Yun in twelve hours at most. However, I was wondering about one thing. If we encounter other starships of the four families, particularly those which are escaping to the edge of the space zone because of the coercion of the Song family, what should I do?"

"Bring them over, of course. Have they jumped to the Seven Seas Space Zone from so far away just to enjoy a show?"

Yun Xuefeng said coldly, "If they resist, just do what you have to do. Like what you said just now, victory is justice!"

…

"Yun Xuefeng's coordinates have been found!"

Soon after Yun Xuefeng and Boss Bai's dialogue, the command center of the garrisons of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in the internal district of the Seven Seas Grand Market was echoing with cheers.

In the hologram of the space zone that covered the whole command center, dense red and blue spots of light were all over it. They were the symbols that represented the hostile starships that had been detected as well as the starships on their own side.

Many red and blue spots were entangled and glowing quickly, which suggested that those starships were fighting heatedly.

As long as anybody touched the light spots with telepathic thoughts, they would also receive astronomical information that was the real-time dynamic data on the battlefield.

Before, Li Yao had always been charging at the frontline of battles as a special warrior. But now, he could only observe the battle from a very narrow perspective and could hardly watch the whole battlefield from the command center comfortably. For a moment, he did not even know what he should do in his idleness.

However, he also knew that the victory of the battle hundreds of thousands of kilometers away had already been decided. Boss Bai had torn an enormous gap in the four families' coalition fleet and teleported the accurate coordinates of the enemy's flagship. It meant that the battle did not have any suspense anymore!

Chapter 2570: Unexpected Accident!

Through the 3D hologram of the space zone, even an amateur like Li Yao who was not good at commanding was able to see the basic situation of the battle.

It could be clearly seen that after a day and a half of hunting and sweeping, the invaders who had unfortunately jumped to the places near the defense circle of the Seven Seas Grand Market had been annihilated, captured, or had "battlefield uprisings". After the fierce battle, the defenders did not have heavy losses at all but were even greater than before.

In the meantime, the invaders also took the opportunity to gather dozens of fleets in different scales at the edge of the space zone. The problem was that the fleets showed no sign of getting close to each other but simply minded their own business. They were even jumping further and further away to the border of the space zone.

It appeared that they had all made up their minds to watch other people crash at the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors first so that they could claim the benefits later.

It gave the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors an opportunity to divide and conquer them.

Right now, Li Yao had gotten the accurate coordinates of the enemy's flagship-in-general. A few most elite hunting warships under the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were rushing at "Landslide". Boss Bai was "reinforcing" Yun Xuefeng with the Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs too.

As long as the enemy was surrounded, even if Yun Xuefeng could gather three to four Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships, it would still be inevitable that his warships were knocked down and his blood was shed among the stars!

"The situation is in our favor. According to the spies on the enemy's side, it seems that the four families have deep contradictions. The Song family is blatantly encouraging the starships of the Li family and the Dongfang family to withdraw from the battlefield and watch the Yun family humiliate itself. The starships of the Yun family, on the other hand, are getting more and more brutal after receiving Yun Xuefeng's command, not reluctant to force the starships of other families to move forward with violence. A few minor battles have even broken out between the two parties! As a result, even if we do attack Yun Xuefeng's flagship-in-general, it is possible that the main warships of the other families will simply turn a blind eye to it!"

Jin Yuyan said with great delight, "As for the starships of the warlords and local magnates, they have been half-convinced by us in the first place and do not have any determination at all. Now, it is even less likely that they will fight for the four families!

"I dare say that as long as we capture or knock down Yun Xuefeng's 'Landslide', the so-called 'alliance of a million starships' will fall out completely, and their failure will be inevitable. Nothing can possibly stop us from the glorious victory!"

Rationally, Li Yao thought so too.

He had absolute confidence in his wisdom and Boss Bai's shamelessness.

But intuitionally, he was more or less disturbed, as if he were walking on the transparent tempered glass between two cliffs. Although he knew that the glass was extremely hard, he still did not feel confident as he looked at the bottomless abyss underneath.

The main reason was that he had never won a battle as easily and enjoyably before.

Every one of his victories before had been earned through tremendous efforts by risking his life. It was rather rare that he could enjoy the privileges of a commander and celebrate the victory by doing nothing other than drinking tea and taking the snacks in the command room. There certainly couldn't have been a more gratifying thing!

"There can't be any accident now, right?"

Li Yao went through every procedure from the beginning to the end, unable to find any flaw.

"Look, our hunting fleet has locked onto the coordinates of 'Landslide', and it is already engaged with the alarm fleet next to the enemy's flagship-in-general!"

Jin Yuyan said in excitement, "The Song family, the Li family, and the Dongfang family still aren't showing any responses. There's no sign that they're coming to rescue Yun Xuefeng. Three hours. As long as they keep idling by for another three hours, Landslide will truly have a landslide!

"Accident? What accident can there possibly be? I can't think of any accident unless all the Immortal Cultivators of the four families suddenly understand the importance of solidarity and devotion and congregate into a united whole, and the local warlords and magnates suddenly abandon their interests in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and their free coins in their accounts, attacking us vehemently without caring about themselves!

"Now, even if the capital learns the situation here, nothing can be done to change it. What can Blackstar the Great do? Even if his belief is incompatible with ours, he still needs the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to win the battle. Otherwise, if the Seven Seas Space Zone falls and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors collapses, his ambitions of eternal reign will be doomed too!"

Li Yao said, "It does make sense. So to speak, we are the sure winners now, but…"

Jin Yuyan asked, "But what?"

Li Yao replied, "But why are my eyelids twitching [span]nonstop?"[/span]

Hardly had he completed his sentence when a deafening explosion echoed somewhere near them.

It was followed by such an intense earthquake that the whole command center was shaking violently. All the lights were flashing crazily, and crystal wires and tubes were broken, with colorful sparks cracking everywhere.

Alarms and shrieks burst out at the same time.

Jin Yuyan's smile earlier was frozen on his face, and it was not entirely gone yet, like a dry mask.

Li Yao, on the other hand, looked as calm, if not desperate, as before, as if he had already been used to the treacherous fate.

The two of them ran to the spot of the explosion together. Very soon, they figured out that it was not the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors that exploded a moment ago, but a building more than seventy floors high abutting it.

The explosion took place somewhere between the thirtieth and fortieth floors, cutting the magnificent skyscraper into halves from the middle. At this moment, the forty floors on the upper half of the building had already slanted and was leaning toward the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors in earsplitting noises of fracture.

The estimated point of impact was so accurate that it couldn't have been an accident but definitely a man-made explosion.

Li Yao cursed aloud in his heart and turned to Jin Yuyan whose face had changed greatly. "What's inside the plaza? Why do you look so awful?"

"Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin."

His facial muscles cramping, Jin Yuyan gnashed his teeth and snapped, "The sister and the brother were locked inside!"

Then, the second and the third explosions took place consecutively.

Those two explosions broke out in the areas that were relatively far away from the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, but they were also the most prosperous areas in the internal district. The crowded skyscrapers, the complicated tube systems, and a population of almost ten million were all gathered there.

After the three explosions, a furious fire was rising, and overwhelming smoke was surging out and spreading to all directions in outrage, soon covering dozens of blocks in the internal district as if it were alive.

"I'm going to find Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin. You stay here and supervise the situation, Director Jin!"

Li Yao gnashed his teeth hard and leaped off from the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors directly. "Remember, stability is what matters most right now. Should the warlords and local magnates in the battlefield in space know the chaos here, everything will be screwed!"

Shua!

After modifications and upgrades, the Mystic Skeleton Battlesuit of the latest generation covered Li Yao's body like wings made of the darkness of night. He dashed into the fire and smoke like a flash of black lightning.

Under his remote perception, none of the details of the twisted plaza ahead of him that was about to collapse escaped his attention.

He could see clearly that countless Immortal Cultivators were roaring and rushing out of the plaza, trying to escape in their crystal suits, but it was inevitable that some people were shocked by the blast and fell into a coma or forever kept in the collapsed debris after being smashed by the burning rocks.

Thankfully, the plaza was reserved by Jin Yuyan to cage Qi Yuanbao, Wuying Qinxin, and some of their subordinates who were involved in the assassination. It was naturally Jin Yuyan's trusted subordinates, who were all strong Immortal Cultivators, that were guarding the building. Not a single ordinary person was inside. So, Li Yao's dilemma was saved.

The artificial body that confined Qi Yuanbao's soul was made by Li Yao. Naturally, Li Yao had implanted a tiny "mark" on it before. With a telepathic thought, a glittering artificial body immediately appeared in his sight. It was moving quickly between the fortieth and fiftieth floors that were collapsing nonstop.

Hiu!

Li Yao slashed into the burning plaza like a black saber and appeared right before Qi Yuanbao.

As it turned out, behind Qi Yuanbao were two unconscious guards, and the man was trying to cross the window in his clumsy artificial body.

"Qi Yuanbao!"

More than infuriated, Li Yao kicked Qi Yuanbao in the chest, and his breastplate was deeply dented. "I was lenient enough to let you go, but you are still determined to get yourself killed!"

"B-Black Wind King!"

Qi Yuanbao rolled on the ground because of the kick and did not stop until he hit the corner. Faced with Li Yao's overwhelming killing intent, he quickly begged for mercy without the demeanor of a godly assassin at all. "I didn't! It wasn't me! I do not know what happened at all. The building simply exploded uncannily and is even slanting. It's about to fall! Those two guards were not knocked out by me either. I just wanted to survive!"

Li Yao was naturally unconvinced by his bulls*it. Making a gesture, he remotely activated the punishment mode in Qi Yuanbao's artificial body. Hundreds of electric arcs immediately surged out from the artificial body, electrocuting Qi Yuanbao's soul, making him cry nonstop.

The pain that was directly imposed on the soul was dozens of times more intense than the extreme pain that the biological nerves could feel. No wonder even the legendary "Hanged Man" was screaming as miserably as a pig being butchered.

Li Yao sniffed and went forward to check the status of the two guards, only to be slightly stunned by what he saw.

He discovered that the guards were knocked out by the blast but not by Qi Yuanbao.

As for why Qi Yuanbao did not pass out because of the blast, it was probably because he was a ghost hiding inside an artificial body, and the frequency of his soul was different from a person of flesh and blood.

"It really was not done by you?"

Li Yao said in surprise, "Excuse me for my misunderstanding. I'll help you exit the 'punishment mode' right now. But let me check with you first, you are not blaming me, are you?"

[ol][li data-annotation-id="2d5396c7-9bd7-4eb8-9f0a-1eca2ae68a15"]Note: It is believed that twitching eyelid (particularly the right eyelid) is a bad omen.[/ol]

Chapter 2571: Sudden Aggression!

Qi Yuanbao was cramping so hard that he was almost vomiting fuel. He cried, "No, I do not blame the Black Wind King. It was all my own fault…"

"It's good that you have such an understanding personality. You're improving very fast!"

Li Yao quickly restored Qi Yuanbao's artificial body into the normal mode. Jabbing his head so hard that his iron skull was almost cracking, Li Yao demanded, "Confess now. Are you behind the accident, or is there anything else that you have been hiding? Did Blackstar the Great give you any other secret orders?"

"No! It wasn't me! It really wasn't me!"

Qi Yuanbao felt so wronged that he was almost shedding lubricant. He whined, "I really don't know anything. I confessed all the arrangements I made in the Seven Seas Grand Market a long time ago. Blackstar the Great did not give us any other secret orders either. He merely asked us to undermine Jin Yuyan's power, but he told us not to kill Jin Yuyan unless it was absolutely necessary. Even if we had to take care of Jin Yuyan, we should at least wait until the battle of Seven Seas was won.

"It was I who acted recklessly because of my greed. If Wuying Qi had given us other secret orders, I wouldn't have been bold to kill him at such a moment at all!"

"It does make sense."

Li Yao considered for a moment and felt that Qi Yuanbao really did not look like a mastermind with his wisdom. The main reason was that the guy jumped out too soon and had been on the stage all the time, which was not expected of a schemer in the dark. Shouldn't a real schemer be keeping a low profile all the time, always ignored by everyone even if they were observed all the time?

His heart palpitating, Li Yao suddenly thought of someone. "Where is your sister, Wuying Qinxin?"

"I-I don't know."

Qi Yuanbao looked confused. "She and I were caged separately. It was impossible for us to communicate with each other. I have no idea where he is, too."

"Let me ask you something else. It is a very critical question. You must answer it honestly!"

Li Yao demanded, "Was it you or your sister who came up with the whole plan of assassinating Jin Yuyan with the help of the 'Soul Transfer Technique'?"

"About that…"

Stunned for a long time, Qi Yuanbao replied in uncertainty, "I can't tell."

Li Yao said in a hurry, "What do you mean by 'you can't tell'? You don't know who came up with the idea first?"

"I was the one who proposed the complete plan, but the technological feasibility was come up by my sister, which triggered my ambition."

Qi Yuanbao said, "In the first place, Wuying Qinxin is a very excellent Meditation Healer. She even grasped many bizarre tricks that even I had never heard of in terms of soul training. One day, she told me excitedly that she had made breakthroughs in the training of 'Soul Transfer Technique', allowing her to exchange the souls of two people without any damage or trace. Even the experts above the Nascent Soul Stage could be swapped too. I was piqued and came up with the idea."

"Wait—"

Narrowing his eyes, Li Yao grasped the key point quickly. "Known as 'Hanged Man', you were the most mysterious ace assassin at the peripheral worlds of the Imperium because of your remarkable expertise in soul techniques, whereas Wuying Qinxin had been spending the last hundred years in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and next to Jin Yuyan. She was a talent in the business world. How on earth could she have known more soul training tricks than you did? Weren't you of the same origin?"

"I don't know about that."

Qi Yuanbao said helplessly, "Although my sister and I both had the heritages of the family, we were separated for decades after our family was massacred, and only the two of us escaped. We did not meet again until we both had made minor achievements. Naturally, she did not tell me her experiences, like whom she met, what techniques she practiced, or which relics she visited.

"We were both Immortal Cultivators. It was perfectly normal for two biological siblings to keep certain secrets from each other. I did not tell her some of my ultimate techniques either!"

Li Yao couldn't have looked more awful. "In that case, why did she tell you that she had grasped the new 'Soul Transfer Technique'?"

Slightly stunned, Qi Yuanbao said, "That's right. Come to think of it, it was indeed rather strange. Usually, my sister and I simply trained on our own. Even if we grasped any unparalleled technique, we hardly told each other. Only when we encountered obstacles or needed external protection in training would we talk to each other.

"I-I did not think too much at that time. I only thought that she told it accidentally when she was too excited…"

Gnashing his teeth, Li Yao asked, one word after another, "Therefore, without Wuying Qinxin's breakthrough in the 'Soul Transfer Technique', your ambitions wouldn't have burst out, and the assassination against Jin Yuyan wouldn't have been activated. In other words, while you were the main perpetrator on the surface, she was actually the mastermind behind the curtain. Everybody, including you, was led by her with extremely subtle psychological hints!"

Stunned for a long time, Qi Yuanbao said, "Now that I think of it, it is possible."

Li Yao shouted angrily, "Why didn't you say it sooner?"

With a bitter expression, Qi Yuanbao said, "I didn't realize it until now. Besides, would the Black Wind King have believed me even if I had said it sooner? Wouldn't you have believed that I was assigning all the blame to Wuying Qinxin and understating my responsibility, and therefore despised, loathed, and tortured me even harder?"

Lost for words, Li Yao took a deep breath and searched for Wuying Qinxin's prison with telepathic thoughts.

To localize the few important persons, other than leaving a mark on Qi Yuanbao's artificial body, he had also put a neck ring on Wuying Qinxin that released waves of a special frequency every second. Li Yao should be able to detect it as long as it was within several thousand meters.

However, Li Yao's scan had no result at all.

It was impossible. Li Yao's tracing neck ring was extremely solid, and it was fully charged with spiritual energy yesterday. Even if Wuying Qinxin had been blown into smithereens or crumpled by the collapsed building, there was no reason why the tracing neck ring would lose effect.

The only possibility was that Wuying Qinxin had escaped with the tracing neck ring, after killing all the guards next to her the moment of the explosion.

Li Yao cursed himself hard in his heart again, wondering why he hadn't learned any lesson from all the setbacks. A guy who jumped out and caused trouble openly since the beginning like Qi Yuanbao must be a lackey or a scapegoat. Wuying Qinxin, being so low-key, should've been treated with more attention!

"I'm going to believe you one more time. If I ever find that you're lying, I'll rip your soul apart and drink wine with it!"

Li Yao grabbed Qi Yuanbao by his neck and threw him to the midair before he kicked him right out of the window at an altitude of almost a hundred meters. He also sealed the guy's artificial body by the way, simply letting him drop while screaming.

The man was a ghost after all, and the artificial body was sturdy enough. Even though it wouldn't be the most pleasant experience, the fall would not kill him.

It was the fastest way to get him away from the burning plaza.

Next, Li Yao stomped on the floor, breaking it and descending to three floors below, before he dashed to the room where Wuying Qinxin had been held in custody.

As he expected, the corridor and the room were empty. There was nothing except for the dead bodies of almost ten guards. Wuying Qinxin had vanished.

On one of his knees, Li Yao examined the corpses of the guards. The more he examined, the more scared he became.

The guards were not knocked out by the blast of the explosion or smashed by the falling ceiling, but blown up by somebody, who crumbled their skulls, squeezed their brains, and shattered their souls into pieces.

Everybody's head was nothing more than an empty balloon. Not the slightest remaining soul could be found inside. No gods in the entire universe could save them now.

Two guards summoned their crystal suits in time. Their heads were protected by the hardest helmet, but they were still killed.

Judging from the clear marks of fists on the helmets, the attacker was not even wearing a crystal suit but still managed to kill the muscular men in crystal suits barehandedly.

Li Yao compared his own fist to it, only to discover that the fist mark on the helmet was much smaller than his own. It was obviously a woman's fist.

It was Wuying Qinxin.

Li Yao's heart sank into a bottomless abyss.

All the files stated that Wuying Qinxin was a pure Meditation Healer who was between an admin-type Immortal Cultivator and a research-type one. Her combat ability was not very tough.

Jin Yuyan also told Li Yao that he had barely seen his wife attack anyone in the past hundred years. Wuying Qinxin preferred to use her brain than her fists.

Li Yao had performed a full examination on Wuying Qinxin himself before she was locked and did not notice any anomaly.

If she had hypnotized the guards through the unique mental attacks of the Meditation Healers, forcing them to brutalize and die with each other, Li Yao would have found it more acceptable.

However, punching so many guards to death with her own fists, with a strength that pierced through two crystal suits as if they were just paper—how was it possible?

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao suddenly thought of something. His whole face was almost blackened, and he hurried to go to the other side of the corridor to check.

Before he broke into the room, he already discovered another body on the corridor, the body of another woman.

It was a woman who had a rising cheekbone, huge eyes, and long arms that reached her knees.

Her eyes were still open after her death. Inside the frozen eyes were questions and fear. Her mouth was wide open, as if she were about to exclaim in disbelief.

However, her chest had been deeply dented, and her spine could almost be seen.

There was also a tiny hole on her forehead, which pierced to the back of her head. All the brain tissues in between had been burnt up by the flames of spiritual energy.

She was Red Lady, one of the four "gods of death" on par with Qi Yuanbao, leader of Bloody Skull, and one of the strongest snipers in Imperium.

Chapter 2572: The Most Furious Burning!

While Red Lady was also one of the assassins who tried to kill Jin Yuyan, she was hired by the four families, which were on the opposite side of Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin.

Also, Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin intentionally indulged her to carry on the assassination although they had seen through her plan, essentially regarding her as a bait. Naturally, she had every reason to hate Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin.

Later, Red Lady's shooting from the high altitude contributed a lot to Qi Yuanbao's easy capture too.

Therefore, Li Yao had suggested to Jin Yuyan that Red Lady be caged somewhere not far away from Qi Yuanbao and Wuying Qinxin. It would be easier for the prisoners to be controlled if they stalled and fought each other because they belonged to different forces.

He did not expect that Red Lady, a legendary expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage, had died so abruptly.

Li Yao looked around, only to notice several deep footprints on the wall and even the ceiling of the corridor. Somebody seemed to have stomped on them heavily.

The footprints were very small, leaving no doubt that they belonged to a woman.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Thinking quickly, Li Yao calculated and recreated the scene back then.

At that time, the explosion inside the building destroyed the security measures and barriers on all floors, allowing Wuying Qinxin and Red Lady to escape.

Red Lady had long reached an agreement with Li Yao and Jin Yuyan, and she was more or less joining them. She was not really imprisoned here but was meant to watch over Wuying Qinxin and Qi Yuanbao.

After the failed assassination, it was impossible for Red Lady to return to the four families. Naturally, she could only be loyal to Li Yao and Jin Yuyan. Therefore, the moment she discovered that Wuying Qinxin had escaped, she went forward to stop her.

Perhaps, in her eyes, Wuying Qinxin was merely a Meditation Healer with barely any combat ability, and she could capture her without much trouble.

But unexpectedly, Wuying Qinxin made small-scale dodging movements on the wall and the ceiling without a pattern at an extremely high speed, so fast that even the eyes of an ace sniper like Red Lady could not catch her trace.

Measuring Wuying Qinxin's acceleration from the depth of the footprints, she must've crossed a hundred meters in 0.1 seconds and appeared before Red Lady. Then, she destroyed Red Lady's internal organs in a heavy attack, before she stabbed a finger into Red Lady's forehead. Flames of spiritual energy surged out of her finger, burning Red Lady's spiritual root and even the whole brain into nothingness.

The whole process couldn't have lasted longer than one second. The top sniper of the Imperium was killed before taking any action.

How exactly… did she manage it?

Walking in the shoes of Wuying Qinxin, Li Yao inferred again and again but still couldn't think it through.

Although Red Lady was a sniper who was not best at melee combat, she was a battle-type Immortal Cultivator after all, and her melee combat ability must've been higher than that of a battle-type Nascent Soul Stage expert in any case.

Of course, since she attempted to murder Jin Yuyan, neither Li Yao nor Jin Yuyan could've allowed her to keep all her strength. Therefore, Jin Yuyan had forced Red Lady to take medicines that would confine her soul, and Li Yao had also put manacles and shackles on Red Lady that could block the cycle of spiritual energy. At most, she could unleash the combat ability at the starting level of the Nascent Soul Stage.

But even if she were merely a sniper at the beginning level of the Nascent Soul Stage, she was still not someone that a Meditation Healer like Wuying Qinxin could've killed!

Wuying Qinxin's body was relatively soft and vulnerable, just as all the other non-battle-type Immortal Cultivators. It had been examined by Li Yao and Jin Yuyan carefully and couldn't possibly be wrong.

Even though she had certain secret techniques of instant outbreaks, say, imposing strong psychological hints on herself to make her soul overload and unleash a ten times higher combat ability, her weak body would not be able to handle it at all.

The outbreak of spiritual energy of such a high intensity would burn her into ashes after only a few hours.

Was she out of her mind? What was she trying to do?

While thinking, Li Yao suddenly felt something wrong in his hand. Rubbing Red Lady's forehead carefully, he picked up a broken bone that was somewhat weird.

It was a bone that was smaller than a tooth. It was unlike a piece of the skull and more like one's fingerbone.

Red Lady's hands, on the other hand, were absolutely intact. Even the skin was not damaged, much less the bones.

Seeing the broken bone, Li Yao immediately had a picture in his head—

In the mayhem of the explosion, Wuying Qinxin rushed out of her prison cell like a crazy devil. With unusual flames of spiritual energy surging around her body, she lunged at the few tall, strong guards in crystal suits.

There was no telling where she got the strength that was far beyond her capacity, but she killed all the guards in the end. However, her weak body could not withstand the recoil of the flooding spiritual energy as well as the heavy punches. Her fingers cracked like fireworks, and even the finger bones were broken and fell out.

But she was like an animal that had lost the sense of pain. Not caring about the damage that was inflicted on her hands at all, she went on and killed Red Lady with the broken hands, before she crashed the glass window at the end of the corridor that was embedded with dozens of defense rune arrays, fleeing successfully.

Li Yao turned around and checked the wounds of the few guards. As he expected, he did find more broken bones from the dented helmets of two guards.

After assembling the broken bones, several intact fingers would be formed.

Li Yao shuddered in coldness.

Wuying Qinxin gave him the feeling that she was not a Meditation Healer at all. She was not even a battle-type Immortal Cultivator, but an animal that had completely lost sanity and got controlled by fiends!

Picking up all the broken bones, Li Yao dashed out from the broken window through which Wuying Qinxin escaped.

"Neltharion, attack!"

The "Blood Stripe Virus", the antecedent to the mental devil, was an alien virus that lived on and moved with blood. It had the keenest senses on the smell and type of blood. From the tiny blood left on the broken pieces, it was possible to track the source of the blood.

Besides, since Wuying Qinxin was burning her soul so crazily and blatantly, the waves of her spiritual energy couldn't have been normal. It couldn't be covered at all.
