Li Yao spread out his telepathic thoughts and soon discovered Wuying Qinxin's trace in a populated downtown area.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

At this moment, intense explosions burst out in all directions at the same time. The earthquakes were so devastating that the whole planet seemed to be falling apart. Countless skyscrapers were shaking, and the whole world was occupied by a black fog.

"Damn it. How many accomplices does Wuying Qinxin have? How many crystal bombs have they planted in the Seven Seas Grand Market? Why are they doing all this?"

Li Yao hopped and swung among the crowded buildings, tracing Wuying Qinxin's blood.

New explosions echoed from all directions incessantly, gradually approaching the downtown area. The super metropolis where tens of millions of people lived was reduced into chaos, the order completely gone.

Suddenly, dazzling sparks burst out between the blue sky and the white clouds above everybody's head. In the next second, the blue sky and the white clouds were all twisted into a messy swirl, before they were completely gone, revealing the cold, iron dome behind them.

The artificial sky had collapsed, and the whole planet fell into darkness. Even all the enemy supply had been cut off. Cries, screams, and collisions of shuttles were echoing in the dark. The shuttles were crashing into each other or even the buildings around like headless flies after losing automatic navigation, resulting in countless tragedies.

Only the emergent illumination arrays, which had independent powers, were emanating dim red brightness, but what they lighted was the panicked faces, which made people even more anxious.

This is truly tricky!

Li Yao was even more certain that it was definitely not Wuying Qinxin's improvisation, and she did not create the chaos in order to find a way to escape. Judging from the operations that she had done by consuming her spiritual energy and damaging her body without control, she did not intend to escape alive at all.

In order to completely cut off the energy supply of the internal district of the Seven Seas Grand Market, the mainframe crystal processors of the whole planet must be controlled first, or at least implanted with abundant viruses.

Li Yao hated the smart yet shameless enemies who disregarded their own lives for their dreams more than anything else!

When he was about to catch the enemy's tail, Wuying Qinxin's coordinates suddenly disappeared off the surface and reached the tubular world below the planet that was as complicated as a maze.

While the surface was a mess with smoke everywhere, the underworld was even darker.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Explosions were breaking out nonstop among the interconnected cables and spiritual energy tubes, bringing a flickering light to the dark world but making the places beyond the reach of the light even darker.

After they reached so deep below the planet that the exit to the surface of the planet was nowhere to be seen, Wuying Qinxin suddenly stopped, as if she were waiting for Li Yao's arrival intentionally.

Her shadow under the shimmer looked like a fiend with a headful of crazily dancing snakes.

Cautiously, Li Yao stepped into Wuying Qinxin's nest and finally saw the woman's real appearance in the middle of the fierce flames. He couldn't help but gasp hard.

As he estimated, the series of attacks and escapes that were beyond her limits had significantly damaged Wuying Qinxin's own body.

Not only was her hands shattered and her palms scrubbed to nothing left but two bleeding arms, but even her legs were twisted weirdly. Countless bones protruded out of her skin. One would feel the excruciating pain just by looking at her.

Also, she had lost so much weight after only half a day that she was almost beyond recognition. Her flesh and blood seemed to have been sacrificed to mysterious forces, making her look like a wrinkled, living skeleton.

Hundreds of wires around had also pierced into her body deeply, connected with her nerve clusters and brain cells. She looked like a puppet controlled by countless strings, waving her bleeding hands and feet and floating in the midair in the shape of a cross while she stared at Li Yao without blinking and with a weird smile.

Chapter 2573: Black Emperor and Black King!

Her eyes were like two gargantuan swirls, but the flames of light resulted from the explosions could not raise the slightest waves inside. The black air surrounding her stabbed at Li Yao nonstop like tentacles, making her look a hundred times more hideous and evil than when Li Yao played the Black Wind King.

Gulu! Gulu! Gulu! Gulu!

Weird noises echoed inside Wuying Qinxin's abdomen, as if her internal organs had been reduced into clusters of magma, supporting the drained body to unleash the last bit of its vitality.

Looking at the woman's hollow and evil face, Li Yao suddenly had a weird intuition—she was not Wuying Qinxin!

It was almost like Jin Yuyan who changed his body through the "Soul Transfer Technique". The person who lurked inside Wuying Qinxin's brain and controlled her incomplete body right now was not herself but a more mysterious, strong, and even… familiar soul.

While Li Yao was thinking hard, "Wuying Qinxin" slowly extended her bare wrists and began to clap them hard.

If she still had her two hands, there would've been loud applause.

However, both of her hands had been eroded to the point that the bones were sticking out. Naturally, she could not make any sound.

However, she couldn't have cared less about it. Wearing a rigid smile, she controlled her bleeding arms and continued the movement of "applauding hard".

The silent applause spread out in the burning darkness, making the scene even more creepy.

"You are not Wuying Qinxin!"

Even Li Yao found it hard to contain himself when faced with such a creepy picture. In full wariness, he gnashed his teeth and asked, "Who are you exactly?"

"Fabulous!"

The stranger did not give a straight answer to his question after the long applause. Instead, she observed with a high-pitched voice that sounded like a crow, "Vulture Li Yao, your speech at the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was truly marvelous. You were so adept at pretending and bluffing that the powerful and wealthy men from the many peripheral worlds of the Imperium were tricked by you without any exception. I almost couldn't refrain myself from applauding and cheering for you on the spot. How many years has it been since I last saw such a shameless Cultivator like you? If there were more Cultivators like you in the Star Ocean Republic years ago, I wouldn't have accomplished my enterprise as easily!"

"Your enterprise?"

Li Yao immediately thought everything through. He grimaced with a bitter face.

When he observed the black flames of spiritual energy surging out of Wuying Qinxin's body, he found that they were condensing into a magnificent throne above her head after floating for a while.

A dominating and brutal shadow, like a raging black dragon, was vaguely looming on the throne.

"Blackstar the Great!"

Li Yao almost found it impossible to believe his eyes. "It's you again! How is it possible? Are you not regrouping the Imperial Guards in the capital? How on earth did you come here?"

"This is exactly the mysteriousness and majesty of the Divinity Branching Stage, which is something that you perhaps will never perceive."

Wuying Qinxin—or Wuying Qi—sniffed and said, "My soul is naturally supervising the situation in the capital inside Li Linghai's body, but I have split part of my soul to hide inside Wuying Qinxin's body right next to Jin Yuyan in order to control the progress in the Seven Seas Space Zone.

"The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the free coin are too important. Do you really think that I would let go of them and allow Jin Yuyan to manage everything on his own?"

It was then that Li Yao realized that Wuying Qi must've hijacked Wuying Qinxin's body through some kind of "remote manipulation".

That explained why Wuying Qinxin's combat ability was improved so much all of a sudden, or why she did not care about the physical damage on her body, not even her broken hands and feet.

She was Wuying Qi's puppet in the first place who had lost her own will. Even if this body was completely destroyed, Wuying Qi wouldn't be hurt very much.

No. Li Yao estimated that even "hijack" was unnecessary. The moment Wuying Qi declared his identity to Wuying Qinxin, he would definitely have her complete loyalty. Wuying Qinxin would not hesitate to die for him, much less serving as a mere carrier.

As for the many crystal wires that were connected to Wuying Qinxin's body, they must be the medium to transmit Wuying Qi's soul power via the Spiritual Nexus covering hundreds of Sectors. Only such a way of "wired communication" could've possibly unleashed such intense flames of spiritual energy.

Li Yao had once been so terribly beaten up by Wuying Qi that he was almost half killed. Therefore, he would never forget the feature of Wuying Qi's soul waves even in his dreams. He was absolutely certain that the dominant vibe of the purple true dragon was not something that other people could fabricate.

However, if Wuying Qi had been controlling Wuying Qinxin and was aware of Jin Yuyan and Qi Yuanbao's whole plan, the whole thing still didn't make sense!

Why would Wuying Qi indulge, if not encourage, Qi Yuanbao to assassinate Jin Yuyan?

Wuying Qi couldn't have foreseen the arrival of Li Yao and Li Jialing. Had it not been for Li Yao, Jin Yuyan would've been assassinated, and the Seven Seas Grand Market would be in chaos right now, unable to support the confidence of the many warlords and local magnates from the peripheral worlds of the Imperium, who would definitely fail the battle of Seven Seas.

So to speak, Wuying Qi wanted the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to fail or even to be completely destroyed by the four families?

There was no reason for that. No matter how Wuying Qi disliked the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the free coin, he and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were on the same boat. The operations of the reformists in many fields needed the free coin as the "lubricant". The capital and the Seven Seas Space Zone depended on each other. If one of them was destroyed, the other would be doomed too.

Therefore, what moves were Wuying Qi playing exactly? Li Yao couldn't possibly think it through however hard he considered.

"Do you feel that your head is in a mess? That's only reasonable!"

Wuying Qi said with a smile, "After the chaotic battle, do you still not understand that the real universe and the real Imperium are exactly such a mess?

"Everybody is selfish, everybody considers for themselves, and everybody wants to preserve their strength and lure other people to fight for them. The four families are exactly like that. It has been two days since the so-called 'grand alliance of a million starships' jumped to the Seven Seas Space Zone, but they are still not regrouped yet and are struggling in the swamp.

"On the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' side, there are just as many plots and conspiracies. Everybody is considering for their petty interests. None of them are willing to sacrifice themselves for what they believe in.

"Either the leaders of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors or the leaders of the four families' coalition fleet must be feeling a great headache and lament their helplessness when they are faced with the subordinates who are as idiotic as pigs, as greedy as wolves, and as cowardly as rabbits, right?

"I, on the other hand, had to govern countless forces exactly like the four families and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to fight against the entire chaotic sea of stars!

"Vulture Li Yao, tell me, how can you resolve the mess without some iron and blood? How can peace and prosperity be restored without somebody losing their head?"

To this moment, Li Yao was still unaware of Wuying Qi's real purpose. Rolling his eyes quickly, he secretly sent a message to Li Jialing while he asked, "'Our enemy is not each other but the entire vast sea of stars'. It was said by Dongfang Wang, the former prime minister of the Imperium. But I still don't understand. What's your plan, Your Majesty? You look confident of your victory, and you are certain that you can finish off the four families easily even without the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors?"

"Did you know that you have a very bad habit, Vulture Li Yao? When you are making evil plans, your eyes roll too fast!"

Wuying Qi said casually, "Also, when you start to address me as 'Your Majesty', you are thinking harder and harder on your evil plans.

"Cut your useless petty tricks. It won't help. I have disrupted all the spiritual waves within a thousand meters. You are not going to send a single message out. It's not easy for me to finally meet the 'Black Wind King', whose loyalty to the Imperium is unquestionable. Why are you in such a hurry to go?

"As for the four families, hehe, they have never been in my eyes in the first place. What I'm doing right now is not as simple as destroying the four families, but to completely eradicate the soil from which the four families grow. I will shatter all the corrupt officials, privileged classes, and interest groups and create a new Imperium of fairness and justice where all the Immortal Cultivators boast lofty ideals and are willing to be sacrificed for the civilization of mankind! Only such a new Imperium will be capable of setting sail to explore… the vaster universe!"

Wuying Qinxin, or whatever left of her, waved her broken limbs crazily under Wuying Qi's control. Blood was spurting out like crimson fireworks.

"Cut the crap. Are you not the greatest corrupt official, privileged class, and interest group yourself? Fish begins to rot from the head. When you kicked off a 'great start', it was only natural that your offspring copied what you did!"

Rubbing the goosebumps on his body hard, Li Yao spat hard. He was then deeply attracted by a phrase that Wuying Qi said and asked subconsciously, "Setting sail to explore the vaster universe? What do you mean by that?"

"Huh?"

Wuying Qi keenly captured Li Yao's shock. Slightly surprised, he said, "I didn't expect that you would know the things about the extraterrestrial lands too. I'm getting more and more curious about the 'Black Wind King'."

"What-What are you saying?"

Li Yao felt that a black fog was pressing his head and making it barely possible for him to breathe. "What are the 'extraterrestrial lands' that you talked about?"

"Do you want to know everything? Then step onto my side genuinely. When you realize how vast the grand universe is and how insignificant and emergent the civilization of mankind is, you will discover how hilarious the conflicts between the Cultivators and the Immortal Cultivators are."

Wuying Qi extended his bleeding hands at Li Yao and said in a seemingly sincere way, "The promise I made in the capital still stands. Vulture Li Yao, stand by my side with the country of Cultivators behind you, and I promise you that everything you've done in the past will be pardoned!"

Chapter 2574: We Are the Same Kind of People!

Li Yao was about to spat on Wuying Qi's face and curse that his words were nothing more than bulls*it. How could he have been so shameless as to say that "everything in the past will be pardoned"? Did he really think that "Vulture Li Yao, the Black Wind King" was a three-year-old?

His heart tightened, he suddenly realized what was wrong, and his back was covered in a cold sweat. Pretending to be shocked, he blurted out, "A country of Cultivators, what is that? I have said that Firefly was already destroyed in civil wars, and the Star Ocean Republic is no more. What country of Cultivators are you talking about?"

"Hehe, your reaction was quick enough and did not give yourself away."

Wuying Qi said with a smile, "However, it is useless. You and I are both clear that what I am talking about is not the Star Ocean Republic but a country at the edge of the cosmos whose name is unknown but whose strength is remarkable."

"There's no need for you to cover it or waste your time explaining. It's just a simple reasoning. The medals, the scepter, and the soul signatures of the Black Wind Fleet that you presented were all authentic. So, it is doubtless that you are the authentic Black Wind King. However, I am also certain that you are a Cultivator with firm beliefs, although you can be quite shameless.

"How can a firm Cultivator become the Black Wind King? There can only be one answer. Qi Yuanbao's guess was correct. The Black Wind Fleet was indeed crushed by the Cultivators. The Immortal Cultivators of the Black Wind Fleet, too cowardly to die, succumbed to you.

"Here comes the question. It was absolutely impossible for the defeated soldiers in the government-in-exile of the Star Ocean Republic to crush the Black Wind Fleet. In that case, wouldn't the answer be obvious? Besides Firefly, there's another country of Cultivators whose comprehensive combat ability is higher than that of the Black Wind Fleet.

"Vulture Li Yao, what is the name of your motherland? How many Sectors do you have? Five? Eight? Ten? Also, the native of the Covenant Alliance who explored the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum with Li Linghai years ago actually did not escape to Firefly but to the country of Cultivators, didn't he?

"Firefly had been drifting in the sea of stars all the time and paid great attention to the starships of the Imperium to avoid their hunting. How could the native of the Covenant Alliance have found Firefly? It was too much a coincidence. I've been suspicious about that all the time. However, if he had in fact ended up in a country at the border of the universe, everything would make sense!"

Stunned for a long time, Li Yao laughed in amusement.

"I have no idea what you are talking about!"

Li Yao shouted, "As such an imaginative man, you should be a writer instead of an emperor!"

"Tsk, tsk, tsk. Do you know how people behave when they are panicked? It is exactly what you look like right now. Although your breath, heartbeat, temperature, blood flow, and even secretion of adrenalin are absolutely normal, I have sensed that your consumption rate of your spiritual energy has increased by more than 10% all of a sudden. Why are you wasting so much of your spiritual energy for no good reason? Are you not suppressing your biophysical parameters so that you would not reveal your panic?"

Wuying Qi's smile on Wuying Qinxin's face was more and more intense and creepy. Pointing the bleeding broken hand at Li Yao, he said, one word after another, "Now, both you and the country of Cultivators behind you have been busted. The general coordinates of the Black Wind Fleet are not a secret to me. In the meantime, unlike Firefly, it is impossible for your motherland to get away easily. So, if I launch an expedition toward the border of the cosmos with a great army in person after settling the chaos at the center of the cosmos, how is your motherland going to resist?"

Li Yao's heart was finally beating crazily beyond his control, like a flood that had just surpassed the dam.

"Hahahaha. It seems that you have finally admitted, Vulture Li Yao!"

Wuying Qi burst into laughter. "How should I call you? The last survivor of Firefly, the Black Wind King, or the special representative of this country of Cultivators which must be very interesting although nobody knows it? Let me see. Right, after subduing the Black Wind Fleet and learning the magnificence of the Imperium of True Human Beings at the center of the cosmos, all of your Cultivators must've been overwhelmed and felt that it was the end of the world, right?

"At that time, the war between the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance was almost your only hope. Therefore, you came to the center of the cosmos, seeing if there was any way to strike a balance between the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance so that your tiny motherland could take advantage and rise, right?

"Don't deny it just yet. It will be useless even if you do. Your credit has gone zero. I do not trust a single word that you say; I only believe in my own reasoning and deduction.

"Abandon your illusions. It is impossible for a tiny mouse to take advantage of the collision between the behemothic beasts.

"However, if you are merely hoping to survive, I may show mercy and spare you as an exception.

"There's no need to doubt my sincerity. Before I learned how vast the grand universe was, I would never have let go of a single Cultivator. However, there is a greater world in front of me that is waiting for my conquest, and there are more brutal and mysterious enemies that must be slain. Then, it is necessary to concentrate all the precious strength of the civilization of mankind and to find as many helpers as possible, including the Cultivators that I absolutely dislike.

"If you know a thing or two about the secrets beyond our tiny universe, and I think you do, you definitely know what I mean.

"I don't need to lie to you. Although I do not like Cultivators, I do appreciate you very much, Vulture Li Yao, because there is something in common between the two of us."

Li Yao scorned, "There's something in common between us?"

"Exactly. Despite our disagreement in our approaches, we are both the purest idealists. We have been striving for the prosperity of the civilization of mankind, and we are even willing to sacrifice our whole lives for our great civilization. It is exactly because I'm hoping to work and sacrifice for mankind for another ten thousand years that I've been seeking greater strength!"

Opening the broken arms, Wuying Qi said sincerely, "But what about the other people, all of them, apart from us? Just look at the ridiculous war in the Seven Seas Space Zone right now! While the troops of the four families and the warlords are selfish, unorganized, and utterly devoid of dignity and courage, aren't the fleets of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors shameless scoundrels who will defect for a few coins?

"My expedition is too distant and difficult. Am I going to command such a bunch of selfish, fake Immortal Cultivators to explore the universe beyond the universe?

"Hehe. Vulture Li Yao, I prefer to cooperate with a real Cultivator like you, which will probably be easier and more pleasant, than to work with those 'fake Immortal Cultivators' who disgust me by their sheer presence. After all, we are the same kind of people who share the same dream, which is to march out of this universe!"

"Damn you!"

New goosebumps were growing on the goosebumps on Li Yao's body. "Who's the same kind of people as you? I'm a man of principles and boundaries, not to mention that I'm also kindhearted and handsome!"

"Hehe, for the dream, any principles or boundaries can be abandoned! If you cling to the hilarious principles and boundaries without letting them go, the dream will be nothing but a joke!"

Wuying Qi suddenly changed his face and demanded, "If you do not dare to resort to whatever it takes and step on all the rules of mankind for your dream, it will only mean that you are not persistent about your dream or qualified to talk about your dream, especially one concerning the future of mankind!

"Vulture Li Yao, let me ask you one last time. Are you and the country of Cultivators behind you willing to stand on my side? We will fight together and unite this tiny universe first before we raise the bloodstained banner of the civilization of mankind on every Sector in every universe!

"Or are you really dogged enough to be crushed into pieces by my unstoppable wheels?"

BOOM!

The black flames of spiritual energy around Wuying Qi surged out. Even Wuying Qinxin's skin and flesh were torn apart, melted like the coal in fire into the purest energy.

His vibe was so unprecedentedly dominating that his real self seemed to have been teleported to Li Yao's front!

Li Yao could barely keep his eyes open under Wuying Qi's blowing air.

"Wuying Qi, I—"

The moment Li Yao opened his mouth, it was filled with wind. His cheeks were puffing like two balloons, and countless blades seemed to be scraping his oral cavity. It took him a long time before he finally vomited the wind out. Gritting his teeth, he shouted solemnly, "Can-Can I consider it for a few days? It's such an important matter. You have to let me consider for ten to fifteen days before I make a prudent decision, right? Even if I make a promise to you right now, you wouldn't believe me at all, would you?"

"Hehe. Fair enough. Then, take your time to consider it. I'm looking forward to your answer."

Wuying Qi said with a smile, "I believe that you will not disappoint me. I'll be waiting for you at the capital!"

Hu!

Wuying Qinxin's body finally couldn't withstand Blackstar the Great's remote control anymore and was burnt into black charcoal by the furious flames of spiritual energy.

"Huh?"

He did not expect that Wuying Qi would be so gullible. Could he have fooled the man with such an obvious pretense?

While he was surprised, he discovered that the fire did not burn up Wuying Qinxin's entire body but kept her head that was like a living skeleton. The skull absorbed all the crazy waves and spiritual energy wandering in the air. In cracking noises, it grew larger and larger still, expanding to three times its original size, like a clear crystal ball.

The eyes that had been magnified exponentially and looked like abysses stared at Li Yao in a feigned smile.

"Not good!"

Li Yao's pupils constricted violently. The bones all over his body were cracking, and every cluster of his nerves was shrieking.

There was no time. In the next second, Wuying Qinxin's abnormally expanded head, mixed with the invincible might left by Blackstar the Great, exploded brutally!

Chapter 2575: Brain Seal

Wuying Qinxin's brain seemed to have expanded billions of times in the blink of an eye, dragging Li Yao into a different world.

The complicated cables, tubes, wires, and burning buildings were gone, replaced by darkness and vague stars that swirled around Li Yao slowly.

However, as Li Yao observed more closely, the glittering stars all turned into Blackstar the Great's deep and intimidating eyes, which stared at him without blinking.

Thousands of Wuying Qi's eyes stared at Li Yao at the same time, raising the most disturbing waves deep inside Li Yao's soul.

The eyes were like mystic rays of a temperature of ten thousand degrees, trying to brand certain unquestionable, inviolable, and non-erasable information to the deepest part of Li Yao's soul.

"Long live Your Majesty! Long live the Imperium of True Human Beings! Long live the civilization of mankind!"

Li Yao seemed to have heard the crazy roars of countless people inside his head.

Consumed in the torrent, Li Yao drifted forward beyond his control for he did not know how long. Gradually, the messy noises and roars became clear and organized. They all turned into… his own voice!

Li Yao heard himself shouting desperately and unwaveringly, "Long live Blackstar the Great! Long live Blackstar the Great! Long live Blackstar the Great!"

He shuddered hard in the coldness. The hole leading to Earth from the deepest part of his soul opened again, unleashing ten thousand electric arcs that struck the terrible and weird illusions into pieces.

The ovations came to an abrupt halt. Among the stars, Wuying Qi's eyes were closed. However, purple tides surged over from the infinite darkness, which turned out to be made of thousands of raging true dragons of purple fire.

The tides of true dragons swept close and collided into Li Yao's soul brutally, resulting in a deafening noise.

BOOM!

Li Yao felt that every pillar in his mind palace was shivering. Vomiting a mouthful of blood crazily, he fell to the deepest part of the maze and passed out instantly, not knowing anything anymore.

He did not know how long he groped in the darkness.

The powerful soul in the Divinity Transformation Stage retained the last bit of consciousness, allowing him to perceive the information that Wuying Qi had inculcated into his head.

He saw an overwhelming, destructive fleet sailing toward the Star Glory Federation under the command of Wuying Qi, razing the country to the ground in only a year and a half.

He saw countless Cultivators of the federation, including himself, kneeling before Wuying Qi's feet, paying tributes to the ancestor of Immortal Cultivators and cheering "long live Blackstar the Great" from the bottom of their hearts.

He saw that under Wuying Qi's governance, a new country of Immortal Cultivators that was fairer and brighter was truly born, and it defeated the Covenant Alliance, united the whole Pangu universe, and marched toward the infinite stars beyond the black wall.

Billions of years later, when the civilization of mankind truly thrived and occupied every corner of the multiverse, the people by then would probably not be bothered by the minor "tricks" that Blackstar the Great adopted a long, long time ago.

"Only Blackstar the Great can save the civilization of mankind! Only Blackstar the Great can lead us to get rid of the destiny that has tripped countless civilizations for billions of years, to surpass the Pangu, Nuwa, and all the other primordial civilizations, and to make the civilization of mankind the only 'eternal civilization' in history!"

Li Yao couldn't tell if it was Wuying Qi's murmur in his head or that he was really thinking that in his own mind.

Li Yao was woken up by the horrible thought and jumped so high that he hit the broken ceiling. He then fell to a gutter among the dilapidated walls.

"I… What happened…"

Li Yao groped on his body, only to discover no wound on his body, except that his head hurt very much and had a numb feeling, as if his head was thrice its previous size.

Fine. After he extended his hand and felt it, he realized that it was not "as if" but real. His head was truly swollen and bleeding, and dry blood was all over his lips and ears. When he slightly shook his head, the whole world was swirling before his eyes, and he could barely sit steadily.

It was a symptom of serious brain damage, at least severe brain concussion plus intracranial hemorrhage. He was not paralyzed or turned vegetative all thanks to his magnificent body in the Divinity Transformation Stage.

The soul blast from Blackstar the Great, which was enhanced by sacrificing the brain of Wuying Qinxin, a super Meditation Healer, turned into the shocking "suicide mental bomb". It was unbelievably powerful.

Thankfully, Li Yao's soul was unbelievably sturdy, which allowed him to manage to resist Wuying Qi's suicide blast. If it were any other Cultivator in the Divinity Transformation Stage, they probably would've been shocked into morons or faced even more terrible consequences.

Blackstar the Great was truly immeasurable and invincible!

Only such an unparalleled expert like Wuying Qi was qualified to lead all the Cultivators, the Immortal Cultivators, and human beings to unite the federation, the Imperium, and the Covenant Alliance so that all the experts of the Pangu universe could be gathered to march out and find a way out for the civilization of mankind.

Nothing good would happen if he continued to resist His Majesty, which would only give the scoundrels an opportunity to take advantage of the situation. However terrible Wuying Qi was, he was definitely much better than the selfish, fake Immortal Cultivators of the four families or the emotionless "mechanical humans" of the Covenant Alliance, wasn't he?

Honestly speaking, it was hard to tell which of them was more terrible, Wuying Qi or Boss Bai. If he could cooperate with Boss Bai, why couldn't he work with His Majesty?

Wait—

Li Yao's eyes suddenly bulged, and he slapped himself in the face hard.

Pa!

A crisp slap echoed from the darkness.

What am I thinking of exactly? How can I possibly have the idea of submitting to Blackstar the Great?

Li Yao found it very unbelievable. He was very certain that before he passed out, or to be more exact, before he suffered the blast of Wuying Qi's suicide mental bomb, he absolutely, absolutely, absolutely never thought to compromise or cooperate with Wuying Qi, much less submitting to the guy.

He even considered temporarily cooperating with "real Immortal Cultivators" like Lei Chenghu, but Wuying Qi was definitely not on the list of his potential partners.

That was because Li Yao vaguely felt that Wuying Qi was simply too dangerous. Indescribably dangerous and far exceeding the "real Immortal Cultivators".

However, his soul was truly wavering right now, and he could not help but have the idea of giving in to Wuying Qi or even bending his knees to surrender.

It was absolutely not his own intention, but it was impossible for him to get rid of the idea all of a sudden either. The idea had been branded on his brain by an iron with a temperature of thousands of degrees, even piercing through his brain and reaching his soul!

What the heck? Why would I suddenly have the idea of succumbing to Wuying Qi? Is he so charismatic that I have been swayed by him before I know it?

Creeped out, Li Yao pulled his hair hard. You've got to be kidding me. It's absolutely impossible. There must be something wrong with his "mental bomb" just now. He "implanted" such a dreadful idea inside my head!

Li Yao took a deep, deep breath and forced himself to calm down and observe the idea that had been deeply branded into his soul.

With his powerful soul in the Divinity Transformation Stage and his firm beliefs, he could analyze the whole thing rationally and calmly, reaching the objective conclusion that he would never lower his head before Wuying Qi.

However, it was still not enough to stop certain intuitional thoughts and the deep reverence for Blackstar the Great.

It was like tiny spiders and innocuous snakes could not hurt human beings, but some people were still scared of them for no reason.

Li Yao meditated for more than ten minutes before he finally calmed himself down and erased the reverence for Wuying Qi deep inside his soul bit by bit.

However, he still sensed that the hideous "mark" was still lingering inside his soul, and it was not to be completely wiped out anytime soon.

As a result, there would be a flaw in his heart, which would hinder him if he had a head-on clash against Wuying Qi before the mark was completely wiped out.

Is this the simple dominating vibe of the true dragons that belong to the supreme sovereigns, or a technique that is more daunting and creepy?

It inevitably occurred to Li Yao that if an unparalleled expert beyond the Divinity Transformation Stage like him almost failed to survive it, the people in the Refinement Stage, the Building Foundation Stage, or the Core Formation Stage who were significantly weaker must've been completely controlled by the mark, unable to free themselves, right?

Wuying Qi wasted so much time talking to me only because he was preparing for such an "ultimate skill". Thankfully, I had similar experiences before. If it were another common expert, would they have been controlled by the thought, crying to kneel before Wuying Qi and fight for him?

Such a creepy "implantation of thoughts" is almost the scariest brainwashing method. Does it have anything to do with Wuying Qi's illogical operations recently?

Li Yao wiped the blood on his lips and in his ears. Thinking hard for a long time in the darkness, he still couldn't figure out what exactly Wuying Qi wanted.

He meant to contact Li Jialing and Jin Yuyan, only to discover that his wrist crystal processor and his crystal suit were both under interference. Around him was an extremely chaotic ocean of spiritual energy where no message could be sent out. It must be another consequence of Wuying Qi controlling Wuying Qinxin to blow up her brain.

Thankfully, his body was not greatly affected. On all fours, he held back his headache and climbed upward one floor after another. When he was about to reach the surface of the planet, he finally heard Li Jialing's panicked shouting.

"Brother Yao! Where are you, Brother Yao?"

Li Yao leaped up in his crystal suit, before he fell on the ground and gasped hard. "I'm here!"

"Brother Yao—"

Li Jialing hurried to jump at him. Anxiety and concerns were all over his face. "Where have you been? You were out of reach for more than half a day. Also, why are your soul waves so disordered, as if you just suffered severe wounds? What happened exactly?"

"It's been more than half a day?"

Li Yao was slightly stunned. He did not expect that the brief unconsciousness in his self-perception would be as long as a couple of hours.

He listened carefully, only to notice that the sounds around were not right. Explosions, gunshots, and riots were echoing everywhere. Frowning, he asked, "What's going on outside? Why does the internal district of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors sound like it is in a war?"

Chapter 2576: Collapse of Confidence

"Something terrible has happened, Brother Yao!"

Li Jialing said anxiously, "Another internal conflict has broken out in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Jin Yuyan has been—"

"You've got to be kidding me!"

Li Yao stood up straight immediately. His eyes popping out, he asked, "Please don't tell me that Jin Yuyan has been assassinated again."

"I didn't say that."

Pausing for a moment to catch his breath, Li Jialing went on, "However, it may be even worse than him being assassinated. In my opinion, it would've been better if the accident was that he was shot! But in fact, it-it is the trade data in the central database of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors that have been cleared, disrupted, and covered!"

"What?!"

As if falling into an ice cave, Li Yao felt a chill from the bottom of his heart.

The core trade data of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was concerned with the interests of countless Immortal Cultivators. It was the biggest reason why thousands of experts were willing to fight for the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the only reason why the free coin was valuable.

It was exactly because the free coin could be exchanged for almost all the resources and magical equipment through the trade network of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors that it was acknowledged by all the Immortal Cultivators, making it even more reliable than the legal tender of the Imperium.

If the trade network of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors collapsed, and all the core trade data were gone or messed up, it would mean the total collapse of the free coin as well as the collapse of all the Immortal Cultivators' confidence in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Who would ever fight for the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors?

Of course, even if all the data collapsed, they could always be gradually recovered given a couple of days.

However, at this critical moment, when the coalition fleet of the four families almost marched to the nose of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, it was certainly a catastrophe.

As long as twenty to thirty percent Immortal Cultivators, if not all of them, lost confidence, a calamitous chain reaction would be triggered.

"How did it happen?"

His face pale, Li Yao demanded, "Shouldn't the core trade data of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors be the top secrets? The dozens of defense measures were a great headache even for me. How could they have been sabotaged easily?"

"Nobody knew what exactly happened."

Li Jialing said quickly, "The grand explosions at the beginning were just a disguise to distract everybody's attention. When everybody, including Jin Yuyan, went to search the culprits of the explosions, the central database of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was uncannily compromised. Also, the enemy had Jin Yuyan's highest clearances, allowing them to import a hundred crystal processor viruses of the latest model. All the data were ruined unstoppably. By the time the specialists of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors discovered it, the damage was already irreversible. It was estimated that it would take at least 48 hours before the data could be recovered!

"To-to make things worse, after clearing the core trade data of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, the enemy also implanted abundant other information, mostly the clandestine scandals and deals of the leaders of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, including a lot of scandals of Jin Yuyan himself. Everybody is panicked and wary of each other now!"

Survival of the fittest was the basic law that all the Immortal Cultivators abided by. Both the Immortal Cultivators from the four families and those of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were the same. None of them was close to being kind or generous.

Having rise all the way up to be the director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors from an unknown guy a hundred years ago, Jin Yuyan certainly had done plenty of betraying, conspiring, and backstabbing.

As for hoarding, gang fighting, or even hiring assassins to annihilate his business competitors, such things were not rare either. Many of them were in violation of the rules of the business world and the rules of the Immortal Cultivators.

All the senior managers, managers, shareholders, and business partners of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors were more or less the same. Hanging around in the upper class in fancy clothes, which of them had clean hands? The unspoken tradition was that as long as the things were cleaned up, leaving no evidence behind, nobody would care about them.

After all, the victors enjoyed impunity!

However, it would be a whole different matter when somebody presented such things in public with solid evidence.

Now, not just the crime evidence of Jin Yuyan, but also the crime evidence of the four senior managers and many other common managers had been exposed to the public and sent to the highly-influential shareholders and business partners through the network of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

It was not until then that many people realized that their certain batches of goods were actually swallowed by a certain scumbag who was smiling at them on the other side of the table and that a certain astonishing assassination that shocked all the Immortal Cultivators was actually directed by Jin Yuyan. Besides, Jin Yuyan had resorted to too many shameless, despicable approaches and misappropriated the interests of too many shareholders and business partners, before he made it to the bloodstained throne.

The stone raised a huge splash. Although a great enemy was up ahead, nobody could hold it back anymore. They demanded an explanation from Jin Yuyan. Somebody even started fighting each other.

"It was Wuying Qinxin—no, it was Wuying Qi, who remotely controlled her brain!"

Li Yao looked so awful that he was like a poisoned frog. He gnashed his teeth and said, "Wuying Qinxin and Jin Yuyan were a couple for a hundred years. A lot of Jin Yuyan's clandestine deals were conducted by Wuying Qinxin. Naturally, she had plenty of evidence in her hands.

"As for Jin Yuyan's highest clearances to modify the central database, hehe, when Wuying Qinxin helped Jin Yuyan with the Soul Transfer Technique, she could naturally scan Jin Yuyan's brain and soul to analyze and copy his soul signature.

"I wonder how long she—he had been planning for this. However, the problem remains. What is it that he wants? Just like what we discussed before, smudging Jin Yuyan, or even collapsing the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, will bring him absolutely no returns. It's not like he is going to take the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors down with him and let the four families claim the fruit?"

"Brother Yao, what do you mean?"

Li Jialing replied in surprise, "I don't understand. How is this related to Wuying Qi? Shouldn't Wuying Qi be utterly ignorant of everything that happened in the Seven Seas Grand Market? If he had known it, how could he have allowed the Seven Seas Grand Market to fall into chaos? The more chaotic this place is, the more unfavorable the situation will be for him!"

"Exactly. I thought so too."

Li Yao said with a bitter smile, "This is the greatest problem and the key to solve the dilemma. However, let's not rush into anything yet. Tell me, has Jin Yuyan got everything under control?"

"On the ground, yes."

Li Jialing said worriedly, "However, the situation in the space battlefield couldn't have been worse!"

Li Jialing told Li Yao that Jin Yuyan had been the director for decades after all. He had a lot of trusted subordinates and supporters in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Despite the disadvantageous circumstances, he did not lose his calmness but resorted to all the methods possible, winning the forgiveness and support of many big shots by giving away tremendous interests without batting an eye. He then punished the people who fought each other and who intended to flee heavily and ensured the order of the Seven Seas Grand Market on the surface.

The gunshots and explosions they heard were exactly from the troops loyal to Jin Yuyan when they were suppressing the people who were trying to flee through the spaceports.

However, while Jin Yuyan could control the order of the Seven Seas Grand Market on the surface, he could not control people's trust and confidence, particularly not the trust of the warlords and local magnates who were vacillating and observing the situation at the edge of the Seven Seas Space Zone with their armies.

In the battle of Seven Seas, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was outnumbered. They were fighting with their morale and confidence, and they had to crush the enemy unstoppably.

Should anything go wrong in the process, preventing the wavering warlords and local magnates from taking their side, they would be absolutely no match for the coalition fleet of the four families with only the forces in their headquarters.

Right now, it was not just that something had gone wrong; half of the sky had essentially collapsed.

The nature of the limp, compromising, and vacillation of the business class couldn't have been better revealed.

The moment they heard the news that a civil war broke out within the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and abundant core trade data had been changed or cleared, many warlords and local magnates changed their attitudes and threw away all the agreements they had reached with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, starting to fight the fleets of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors for real.

The fleets cruising at the edge of the Seven Seas Space Zone lazily seemed to have been injected with excitants too. Refreshed, they all approached Yun Xuefeng's flagship-in-general, eager to pledge their loyalty to the commander.

As long as somebody started it, there would certainly be a lot of followers. Even the Immortal Cultivators who did not intend to abandon their agreements with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, seeing that other people were returning to the four families' side and the scales of victory were more and more inclined, certainly had no reason to die together with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

Li Jialing said helplessly, "The whole Seven Seas Grand Market was a mess just now. Since we couldn't find you, many people said that even 'Black Wind King' had slipped away when the situation did not look promising. Some even say that Brother Yao was a fake Black Wind King that Jin Yuyan fabricated as a bluff because not even one ship of the Black Wind Fleet could be seen. The rumors are spreading on every street. It is even less possible to stop the collapse of confidence!"

"Understood. What about Boss Bai?"

Narrowing his eyes, Li Yao said calmly, "Has Boss Bai sent any message? Is he exposed?"

"Not yet. Seeing that it was not good, Boss Bai chose to be Yun Xuefeng's 'loyal subordinate' at the earliest time. So, he is not exposed yet."

Li Jialing said, "But more and more fleets are gathered next to Yun Xuefeng now. Boss Bai is caught in an awkward situation now that he is surrounded, and it's hard for him to do anything. It must be noted that we intended to watch the tigers fight, but one of the tigers has collapsed before fighting. Are we going to charge forward and fight the tiger on our own?"

Chapter 2577: Drinking Poison to Quench Thirst!

Li Yao did not expect Blackstar the Great's fatal moves to be so consecutive and brutal. It was like an incessant flood that did not give them any chance to catch their breath. During the first half of the day when he was unconscious, the situation in the outside world had been drastically changed.

The admiration for Wuying Qi rose up deep inside his heart again, which scourged his brain like lethal venom.

He saw Wuying Qi, who was ensconced on the high throne while pointing at the picture of the universe magnificently, and he almost couldn't stop himself from worshipping Wuying Qi.

"Damn it!"

Li Yao spat hard; his saliva piercing through Wuying Qi's illusion.

The illusions collapsed like a palace of glass, turning into thousands of dancing butterflies. Behind every butterfly was a series of patterns that looked like swirls. When two swirls were combined, they became Wuying Qi's eyes precisely.

Even if Li Yao closed his eyes, he could not move the thousands of aggressive eyes of Wuying Qi away from his head.

He could only slap himself heavily as quickly as lightning, managing to suppress his reverence and admiration for Wuying Qi.

"This is—"

Li Jialing was taken aback by Li Yao's sudden movement. Looking at Li Yao's highly-rising cheeks where two bloody handprints were particularly obvious, which implied that Li Yao certainly did not hold any strength back, the young lion was dumbfounded. "Brother Yao, what was that about? We may have been outplayed, but there is no need to blame yourself so hard. We-We'll work something out together."

"Who says that I'm blaming myself?"

With his nose bleeding, Li Yao grimaced. "I was merely refreshing myself through that special technique in order to resist Wuying Qi's dominance. The specific mechanism is too mysterious for me to explain right now. Don't be surprised if I continue to slap myself later.

"Also, bring me to Jin Yuyan immediately. This whole thing was planned by Wuying Qi, but there must be more to it. He certainly has more moves!"

Li Jialing found it hard to believe. "How do you know, Brother Yao?"

"Very simple. Considering the situation, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors will collapse if no changes happen."

Li Yao said, "I believe it is definitely not Wuying Qi's purpose, or at least not his only purpose. The sudden collapse of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors will bring him no good whatsoever. If I were Wuying Qi, I would achieve the most benefits if the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the four families are mutually destroyed by each other.

"Therefore, I don't believe that Wuying Qi's scheme will be so simple. There will certainly be more changes in the next moment. Hehe, he narrowly won the game just now, but next time, it will be the time for our counterattack!"

Li Yao and Li Jialing boosted their crystal suits and flew toward Jin Yuyan's command center in two streaks of brilliance.

On their way, they saw countless soldiers stationed at the streets and alleys in the Seven Seas Grand Market. Barricades had been set up in many critical locations. Tremendous armed shuttles, spiritual puppets, and Exos were patrolling in full wariness, braced for the arrival of the great enemy.

Fierce street battles seemed to have just happened among many streets. Pits of cannonballs and remains of tanks and crystal suits were everywhere. The soldiers were also pulling out the dead bodies and piling them up, which added to the solemnity in the atmosphere.

The citizens who lived in the Seven Seas Grand Market in the first place and the merchants who had come to the place for trades only dared to hide behind the glass windows and stare at the warring world outside in panic.

The pictures filled with rust, blood, and smoke made Li Yao sigh to himself.

Although Jin Yuyan got the situation under control for now through iron measures, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was already half lost judging from the tension in the internal district.

Compared to the four Kurfürst families and Blackstar the Great, the greatest advantage of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was "freedom". At least on the surface, people could travel and trade freely without supervision from anybody. As long as they abided by the basic rules, this place would be absolutely free. They could come and leave whenever they wanted and do whatever they wanted here.

"Welcome to the freest place in the entire universe", the slogan floating at the edge of the Seven Seas Space Zone, pointed out the greatest value of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

The Immortal Cultivators here were also fighting for "freedom".

However, after the unexpected incidents, Jin Yuyan, in his desperation, stomped on the freedom that the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had always been proud of and suppressed the merchants that did not belong to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors with violence. Those people were only here for trades, and they wanted to escape to avoid the war. Was Jin Yuyan qualified to keep them in this place?

It was a typical case of quenching thirst with poison.

If the game in question was "violence", ten Business Alliances of Ten Thousand Sectors would've been no match for the four Kurfürst families, let alone Blackstar the Great.

If those people were succumbing to violence, why would they succumb to Jin Yuyan instead of the four Kurfürst or Blackstar the Great?

The Seven Seas Grand Market was like a suppressed volcano right now. Even though Jin Yuyan could hold the situation for now, the chaos would burst out a hundred times more intensely soon enough.

If the battle in space remained unchanged, Li Yao estimated that the Seven Seas Grand Market would fall into complete mayhem in two to three days at most.

However—

When they were about to return to the headquarters of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, they were suddenly surrounded by a tide of cheers. The panicked, frustrated soldiers a moment ago were shouting and dancing in wild joy as if they had just injected excitants.

"What is this about?"

Li Yao was slightly dazed, knowing that something unexpected must've happened. However, there was no need to ask the cheering soldiers, and he simply dived into Jin Yuyan's headquarters.

The command center was a total mess. A minor riot seemed to have taken place a moment ago because broken crystal processors and crystal wires were still on the ground. In some places, there were also tiny pits and explosion marks. There were even soldiers who were scrubbing the blood busily.

However, the leaders and military commanders, including Jin Yuyan, looked as if they were in a rather high spirit, so high that they were almost delirious.

Their eyes were all popping out and bloodshot, and they were biting their lips hard. Their hands were shivering beyond their control. Feverish anxiety was emanating from all of them.

Li Yao had seen their looks on countless gamblers; the gamblers who had lost everything in desperation, only to run into a windfall, which they brought into the casino, hoping to win back their losses.

Jin Yuyan obviously saw Li Yao walking in, but he did not have time to ask where Li Yao had been to, whether or not he had caught the masterminds of the explosions or his wife Wuying Qinxin, or any other critical questions. He still stared at the holographic map of the space zone, totally focused.

It seemed that the information on the map was even more important than "Vulture Li Yao, Black Wind King".

What's going on? Have those people lost their minds?

Li Yao looked at where they were looking. On the holographic map of the space zone, the blue spots, which represented the garrison fleet of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, were not in the best condition. Most of the blue spots were surrounded and stalled by red spots that were multiple times more, implying that the fleets of the two parties were having fierce fights.

What was even more dreadful was that, in places far away from the blue spots, a large cluster of dense red spots were congregating into a dazzling light ball, and even more red spots were joining it from various directions, making the red light ball larger and larger, to the extent that it was almost swallowing the entire space zone.

The red light ball must be the main force that Yun Xuefeng commanded.

Judging from the blinking and changing data nearby, Yun Xuefeng had gathered quite a few Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships. The torrents of destruction were about to take shape!

Almost all the blue spots were rooted by the red spots, making it impossible for them to stop Yun Xuefeng before his fleet completely took shape. They could only watch Yun Xuefeng's fleet proliferate like an abnormal tumor.

However, Li Yao noticed something uncanny. Not far away from Yun Xuefeng's fleet, on the orbit of a planet whose back was against the battlefield, a few triangular light spots were shining rapidly, and the three triangular light spots constituted a new triangle.

It was a sign that a space gate was being gradually opened!

In such a crisis, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had opened another space zone. Who were they navigating to reach this place? Could there be more reinforcements?

If it were the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors' fleets in other places of the cosmos that needed to jump back, wouldn't they be blown up immediately after the jump, considering that they were so close to Yun Xuefeng's main force?

It was certainly suicide. What were they doing exactly!

Li Yao leaped to Jin Yuyan in two steps and pulled him aside, before he asked in a low voice, "Director Jin, what happened? Why have you opened a new space gate? Who are you directing to jump to this place? Even if there are reinforcements, aren't they too close to the enemy's main force? In such a distance, they will be discovered and destroyed by Yun Xuefeng the moment they are teleported!"

Jin Yuyan's eyeballs moved rigidly. Stunned for a long time, he finally put on a helpless expression and said in a low voice, "They won't. Perhaps other reinforcements will be blown up by Yun Xuefeng's Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships, but not this fleet. They are the last chance for us to win the battle of Seven Seas!"

Li Yao's eyes bulged as he shouted, "Are you serious? Does the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors have such a remarkable garrison fleet? Who's the reinforcement?"

"It's not a garrison fleet of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors but Lei Chenghu."

Jin Yuyan said solemnly, "Lei Chenghu's Astounding Thunder Fleet has come to our aid in person!"

"What?!"

A flash of lightning burst out inside Li Yao's head, illuminating his every brain cell. Wuying Qi, Li Linghai, Lei Chenghu, the real Immortal Cultivators, the loyal Immortal Cultivators, and the Immortal Cultivators who were devoted to Blackstar the Great. Many clues and pieces were entangled. He seemed to understand everything, only to be confused in the next second. Before he figured out the general picture, he cried subconsciously, "No! You cannot allow Lei Chenghu's fleet to jump over! Close the space gate now! It's a scheme!"

Chapter 2578: Fatal Loophole!

Jin Yuyan stared at Li Yao for a long time sullenly and asked, "What kind of scheme?"

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao tried to calm himself down first before he said quickly without any stop, "I don't know if you have captured the culprits of the serial explosions, but no matter how many criminals you have captured, they all point to the same person—the mastermind behind the whole thing is none other than Blackstar the Great himself!

"Wuying Qi remotely controlled your wife by some sort of super-remote telepathy. Perhaps he had been lurking next to you since a couple of years ago, if not decades ago. He knows about your details and the internal operation of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors as well as he knows his own fingers. That's why he could hack the internal network of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors easily, erase the core data, and slander you. Fine, technically, it's not slander. But the bottom line is, the whole thing is Wuying Qi's trap!

"Think about it, why has Wuying Qi been doing what he has been doing? He tried to assassinate you first. When it failed, he hacked the internal network of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, modifying the data and ruining your reputation. Does he not know that it will result in the complete chaos of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the failure of the battle of Seven Seas.

"No, he must've seen it coming. It is exactly his purpose. He has been trying to press the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors to the hopeless corner and force you to accept the assistance from the outside world, the assistance of Lei Chenghu!"

As he continued, Li Yao was yelling more than talking.

Jin Yuyan's eyes were hollow, and his face showed no reaction. There was no telling if he heard what Li Yao said.

"You must believe me. I swear to god that everything I said is true. I just fought Wuying Qi hard and heavily wounded his soul, forcing him to flee in a panic with his remaining soul, although I was more or less injured and unconscious for half a day. What I saw and heard was firsthand information!"

Li Yao continued anxiously, "Although Lei Chenghu joined the reformists after you and I did, and the queen and he have been merely using each other, don't forget that he is a 'real Immortal Cultivator'!

"As the guy who rescued Lei Chenghu from the Godly Might Prison in person, I was in a not bad relationship with him and communicated with him many times. He expressed his disgust toward the four Kurfürst families, his disdain for the selfish 'false Immortal Cultivators', and his hope that the Imperium could be restored to the age of Blackstar the Great many times.

"Can't you see? Lei Chenghu is an avid admirer of Wuying Qi. As long as Wuying Qi identifies himself and introduces his bulls*it theories of a new world of Immortal Cultivators that is full of 'justice, openness, and fairness', it is very possible that Lei Chenghu will follow his command without any hesitation!

"In conclusion, Wuying Qi must have malicious plans now that he has asked Lei Chenghu to jump to the Seven Seas Space Zone at such a moment.

"The Astounding Thunder Fleet's strength is certainly unparalleled, and it will possibly change the whole battle. However, if it is the Astounding Thunder Fleet that saves the day in the end, the warlords' fleets scattered in the Seven Seas Space Zone will surrender to Lei Chenghu and accept his command. Even the garrison fleet of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors may be stolen by Lei Chenghu. Blackstar the Great will essentially be swallowing them all.

"In other words, although the battle is won, the fruit of victory will be picked by Wuying Qi, and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors will still be dead for sure.

"Yes, this is exactly their scheme, this is Wuying Qi's purpose! Director Jin, please consider carefully!"

Jin Yuyan listened quietly without saying or doing anything.

He was silent for such a long time that Li Yao almost couldn't hold himself from slapping him and refreshing him too. It was not until then that he said gloomily, "Vulture Li Yao, if you think that I opened the gate allowing the Astounding Thunder Fleet to jump over in organization without noticing the anomaly of the incident at all, you would be thinking too little of me as the director of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

"It's too coincidental. A few days ago, I suffered a dual assassination, and a series of explosions took place a few days later. Hardly had the flames of the explosions died out when our internal network was compromised and the data were changed, which immediately reversed the situation on the battlefield.

"Then, when the situation had just been reversed, and we were in the greatest crisis ever, the Astounding Thunder Fleet was already prepared to reinforce us and could jump over anytime. Do you think that I'm a three-year-old who could not see the shadiness?

"However, you must've seen the tension and depression outside when you were on your way here, didn't you? The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors had lost its unity. How can I keep it together with violence? Such a method could only buy us a few days, or even only 24 hours.

"If I cannot find any chance to change the situation in 24 hours, I might as well draw my sword and kill myself!

"Therefore, tell me, when Lei Chenghu sent us a message that he could reinforce us, what choices do we have except to accept it in ecstasy and open the gate for them?

"'Vulture Li Yao, Black Wind King'… Maybe your Black Wind Fleet can come here in 24 hours? If the Black Wind Fleet can miraculously show up, I will shoulder all the pressure and shut the space gate, blocking the Astounding Thunder Fleet from the battlefield!"

"About that…"

Li Yao was lost for words.

"Yes, the Black Wind Fleet cannot arrive in time, and your 'good friends' lurking in the coalition fleet of the four families cannot just declare war on hundreds of thousands of starships around them fearlessly. Then, what can we do except for counting on the Astounding Thunder Fleet, our 'last chance'? Keeping our eyes open while we are being slaughtered?"

Jin Yuyan smiled bitterly. "Even if Lei Chenghu is truly Wuying Qi's guy, we do not have a second choice. Right now, I can only place my hope on Lei Chenghu's integrity and his subtle stance.

"After all, Lei Chenghu was from the peripheral worlds of the Imperium too. He was a rather special warlord. The construction and maintenance of the Astounding Thunder Fleet, as well as his marvelous performance in the Imperium's counterattack, couldn't have been accomplished without the secret resource support and the trophy businesses of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

"Let's hope that Lei Chenghu still remembers the old times and does not go too far!"

Li Yao opened his mouth, only to come up with nothing.

A silver tongue couldn't compare to tens of thousands of state-of-the-art warships, which were loaded with countless experts and Colossi!

"Also, I think that the scheme you inferred had a huge loophole."

Jin Yuyan stared at Li Yao thoughtfully. "If the whole thing is Wuying Qi's scheme and that he wants to watch the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the four families destroy each other before letting the Astounding Thunder Fleet claim the fruit, don't you think that it is too soon? Shouldn't he have postponed the havoc in the Seven Seas Grand Market for a day or two and waited to activate his plan when we were vehemently fighting against Yun Xuefeng?

"More critically, now that Lei Chenghu's fleet has jumped to the Seven Seas Space Zone, what about the capital?"

Li Yao was stunned again. He almost couldn't hold himself from slapping his forehead. How could he have neglected such a crucial question?

Among the reformists, Lei Chenghu's Astounding Thunder Fleet was, without a doubt, the best troop. Neither the Imperial Guards who were being regrouped and trained in the capital nor the garrison fleet of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors was a match for the Astounding Thunder Fleet.

However, when the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the four families planned for the battle of Seven Seas, neither of them took the Astounding Thunder Fleet into consideration.

It was not because of the commanders' mistake, but because the Astounding Thunder Fleet would never have left their base by logic.

In the game between the reformists and the four Kurfürst families, the most important battlefield was not the Seven Seas Space Zone; it was the capital.

The fleet made of sixteen Adamantine Super Arsenal Warships was definitely not the "ace troops" of the four families either. The most elite forces of the four families were still waiting to launch a critical strike at the capital at the right moment.

At this moment, there was nothing but the rookies who had just joined the reformists in the capital besides the Imperial Guards. However talented Blackstar the Great was at commanding and training, it was impossible for him to train the Imperial Guards and the rookies into battle-tested warriors in only a couple of months.

The Imperial Guards alone couldn't stop the ace fleets of the four families at all.

The reason why the four families hadn't attacked the capital was something else. It was partly because of the conflicts of the four families regarding the new emperor and partly because of the Astounding Thunder Fleet.

The Astounding Thunder Fleet was like a cutthroat dagger that had been placed on the neck of the Li family, hamstringing the ace troops of the Li family.

The subtle conflicts among the four families, on the other hand, led to a chain reaction that hamstrung the ace troops of the other three families.

Lei Chenghu was known as the "God of War". Who had the courage to summon the best forces of their family to attack the capital when the God of War was coveting them?

Even though the Imperial Guards were no match for the ace troops of the four families, it would still be possible for them to delay the enemy for a couple of months with the iron defense of the capital.

A couple of months were more than enough for Lei Chenghu to cut the guts of the four families in their heartlands with the Astounding Thunder Fleet.

The reason was very obvious. The Astounding Thunder Fleet and the ace fleets of the four families were stalled and locked by each other. That was why a war started at the Seven Seas Space Zone at the edge of the Imperium earlier than the one started in the capital.

However, the Astounding Thunder Fleet had left the location that was most in their favor. Moving the poisoned dagger away from the Li family's throat, they had jumped into the Seven Seas Space Zone, diving into the gigantic mess.

It was easy to leave the heartland of the Li family, but it was impossible for the Astounding Thunder Fleet to jump back in an organized way. There was no way that the Li family was going to open the space gate to the Astounding Thunder Fleet again.

It meant that the shackles that confined the ace fleets of the four families had been shattered. They no longer needed to worry about the Astounding Thunder Fleet's threat and could launch an attack at the capital without concerns, and the whole situation was exactly Blackstar the Great's plan!

Chapter 2579: Unstoppable Thunder!

"However strong the Astounding Thunder Fleet is, it is terribly outnumbered after all. It's easy to crush the four families' coalition fleet, but they cannot be destroyed or swallowed overnight."

Jin Yuyan analyzed calmly, "It must be noted that Yun Xuefeng is commanding a fleet of a million starships. Even if Yun Xuefeng is a pig whose subordinates are a million pigs that can breathe and fly in the vacuum, how long will it take Lei Chenghu to completely capture and butcher them?

"Also, according to your theory, Lei Chenghu is not just crushing and swallowing Yun Xuefeng's fleet but also taking the opportunity to control the fleets of the many warlords from the peripherical worlds of the Imperium and even the garrison fleets of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. None of the goals can be achieved anytime soon. Can Lei Chenghu completely control the Seven Seas Space Zone given one month?"

Li Yao was lost for words.

Having been through the battle between the Black Wind Fleet and the Star Glory Federation in person, he knew that it was much easier to defeat an army in the boundless space than to annihilate or capture the enemies.

With the Astounding Thunder Fleet's sudden arrival and unexpected assault, it would naturally be invincible and unstoppable.

However, as time went by, the shining saber would be sinking into the mud, and it would not be easy to draw it out.

Jin Yuyan claimed that it would be a month, but Li Yao felt that it was an overestimation of the Astounding Thunder Fleet's abilities.

Like Jin Yuyan said, even if everybody in the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the coalition fleet of the four families were pigs, as long as they were capable of breathing and wandering in space, it would take Lei Chenghu at least a couple of months before he finally got the Seven Seas Space Zone under control.

In other words, the moment Lei Chenghu's fleet jumped over, it would be stalled by the four families and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors who each had their own plans, thereby losing mobility.

Without the threat provided by the Astounding Thunder Fleet, the capital would be wide open. As long as the highest commanders of the ace fleets of the four families were not dumb and blind, they would never let go of such a rare opportunity!

"I believe I do not need to explain which between the capital and the Seven Seas Space Zone is more important."

Jin Yuyan continued coldly, "If I were the highest commanders of the ace fleets of the four families, and I discovered that the Astounding Thunder Fleet had disappeared to reinforce the Seven Seas Space Zone, I would certainly not be chasing after the Astounding Thunder Fleet like a dummy, joining the war of attrition in the gigantic, messy swamp that was the Seven Seas Space Zone.

"I would certainly seize the momentary opportunity and drop the conflicts with the other few families for now. We would launch our ace fleets at the same time toward the capital, destroy the nest of the reformists, and capture Li Linghai, Yue Wushuang, and the other rebels in the 'Reform Committee of the Council of Elders'!

"If the 'Reform Committee of the Council of Elders' is annihilated and the capital falls back to the four families' control, it would be the doom of the reformists. By then, even if the reformists secure a minor victory in the battle of Seven Seas, it would be useless. Everybody would be inclined toward the four families. Even the expedition army would not stand behind Lei Chenghu. Everything would be over.

"Vulture Li Yao, if you were the highest commander of the ace fleets of the four families, would you find my previous analysis reasonable?"

Thinking for a long time, Li Yao struggled to reply. "Fair enough, the Astounding Thunder Fleet is truly the key to stop the four families from attacking the capital. Now that the Astounding Thunder Fleet has volunteered to leave the heartland of the four families for an insignificant battle at the edge of the cosmos, if I were the highest commander of the ace fleets of the four families, I would certainly attack the capital without any hesitation!

"Then, even if Wuying Qi has combined all the strengths in the capital, could he resist the ace fleets of the four families with the Imperial Guards who are exceptionally young and untrained?"

Jin Yuyan asked again, "Or maybe Wuying Qi could get reinforcements as strong as the Astounding Thunder Fleet from elsewhere?"

"It's impossible."

Li Yao closed his eyes and heaved a sigh. "Even Wuying Qi cannot turn a stone into gold. He cannot transform the Imperial Guards who haven't fought any hard battles for hundreds of years into an invincible army in only a couple of months.

"As for the other strong reinforcements, I fear that the expedition army is the only choice.

"However, the expedition army has been reluctant to publicly state their support for the reformists. It is obvious that they are still observing the situation. Now that Wuying Qi has made such a horrible mistake, it is even less likely for them to help him at such a critical moment. They would be showing Lei Chenghu enough respect if they do not stab the reformists in the back.

"Besides, even if the expedition army does reinforce the capital from far away, the ace fleets of the four families would not necessarily be scared of them. Those few ace fleets are the greatest reliance of the four families. From the commanders to the middle-level officers and even to the bottom-level soldiers, they are made of the most loyal, central descendants of the families. The resources consumed in their daily training and the configuration of warships are multiple times more than those of common fleets. Even the Adamantine Fleet that Yun Xuefeng commands is far from them.

"Many people say that the soldiers of the few ace fleets are more expensive than marrow crystals of the same weight. They are the real tough warriors and the greatest support for the four families to dominate the Imperium. Even if they run into the Astounding Thunder Fleet or other fleets of the expedition army, those hardcore nobles would never betray their families or back off.

"If the two parties have the most intense collision in a head-on clash, it remains to be seen who will win in the end!"

"Therefore—"

Jin Yuyan smiled. "You said that the whole thing was Wuying Qi's arrangement. Then, what is his ultimate purpose? Abandon all the defense so that the best fleets of the four families will be attracted to stab his heart?

"Tactically, it is true that the arrival of the Astounding Thunder Fleet may very likely change the outcome of the battle of Seven Seas. However, strategically, such a decision is most idiotic. Unless caught in desperation and it is absolutely necessary, not a single highest commander would have made such a decision!"

"That's right…"

The swirls of mists grew larger and larger still in Li Yao's head. He seemed to have been lost in a hazy fog by himself, and he could not find a way out however hard he struggled. He could only mumble, "I need to think. I need to think about it carefully."

"You can take your time to think about it, Black Wind King. After all, we have plenty of time."

Jin Yuyan said with a bitter smile, "The Astounding Thunder Fleet has successfully jumped over and joined the space battlefield in an organized formation. However strategically idiotic the decision is, and no matter how many schemes and traps it entails, our army has earned more advantages at least tactically, and we will not be killed for the time being!"

Hardly had Jin Yuyan concluded his sentence when explosive cheers burst out in the command center.

Li Yao's head was dizzy because of the overwhelming cheers. He did not know whether he should burst into fury or frustration. "Director Jin, how could you?"

"Like I said, I don't have a choice."

With helplessness popping on his face, Jin Yuyan said in a low voice, "The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors does not belong to me alone. The scandals about me that were exposed to the internal network significantly diminished my control. Many senior managers and great shareholders are already very dissatisfied with me.
