Under the navigation of three space gates, more and more fleets that belonged to the four families jumped over in an organized way, blocking and suppressing the reformists like an iron wall. Tremendous ultra-huge engineering warships that carried infrastructural resources had jumped over too. Seven days after the battle began, there was even a whole space fortress that appeared at the Empyreal Terminus Sectors after six hours!

Receiving the support of sufficient resources, the fleets were gathered into solid battle formations, and the army of nobles pressed forward unstoppably one footstep after another.

The Empyreal Terminus Sector was the heart of the Imperium after all. Countless defense lines in space had been constructed and strengthened in the past thousand years. Almost every solid planet, satellite, and stable asteroid had been set up with abundant bases and cubic defense bases. Even though the four families had the absolute advantage in combat ability, they were not in a hurry to break the core of the Empyreal Terminus Sector—the capital.

They pressed forward step by step, clearing every obstacle on the way to the capital planet and conquering all the fortresses and bases in space. After every defense base was conquered, they would immediately dispatch their own fleets to garrison it. A lot of materials were also shipped over to start heated construction works.

It was a war of a hundred Sectors versus one. The four families boasted enough time and resources to play a crushing game without taking the slightest risk.

On the twentieth day of the battle in the capital, the No. 5 planet closest to the capital planet was occupied by the four families, turning into the best platform for them to attack the capital planet.

It meant that the hands of death that grabbed the throat of the reformists had been completely closed.

The final attack was about to begin.

Faced with the aggressive, dominating performance of the four families, the reformists were naturally unwilling to wait to be killed.

The Deep Sea Fleet and the Imperial Guards organized a few counterattacks, only to be blocked by the four families' dense battle formation, solid defense, and overwhelming troops.

Even though the warriors of the reformists had the courage and combat ability to defeat multiple times more enemies, it was useless when they were faced with the enemies that significantly outnumbered them.

There were simply too many enemies, and they were too formidable!

One month after the battle started, the greatest problem that the reformists were faced with was in fact not the competition on the battlefield but the full blockage of communication. It was barely possible for them to reach out to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the Astounding Thunder Fleet, the other two main forces in the Seven Seas Space Zone at the edge of the cosmos.

The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors represented the strength of countless warlords and the capitals of the Imperium.

The Astounding Thunder Fleet, on the other hand, could be used to win the support of the expedition army that was still keeping neutral.

The two main forces were the only hope for the reformists, however dim.

However, it was very difficult for two worlds tens of thousands of lightyears away from each other to communicate. Their real-time communication depended on the "Hyperspace Communication Bases" established at the edge of the space zones, which allowed information and data to also jump through the four-dimensional before they were delivered to the recipient.

If the messages were sent in regular ways, they could only be received after ten thousand years even if they were kept at the speed of light, which would've been pointless.

However, the dozens of "Hyperspace Communication Bases" that the reformists set up at the edge of the Empyreal Terminus Sector had all been discovered, occupied, or destroyed by the ace fleets of the four families.

In the meantime, the four families had projected powerful interferences to the whole space zone. They unleashed twisting radiations and spiritual waves incessantly to disrupt the secret communication bases that very likely existed.

As a result, the capital was enshrouded in a heavy fog and blocked from the outside world—mainly the Seven Seas Space Zone. It was barely possible to learn the updates from the Seven Seas Space Zone or to command Lei Chenghu or Jin Yuyan to take appropriate reactions.

With the weak strength of the Deep Sea Fleet and the Imperial Guards, it was an impossible mission to block the information blockade of the ace fleets of the four families.

It was true that they had hidden a few mobile secret communication bases at a certain point in the vast space. There were also quite a few powerful communication ships in the complicated stone belts that had yet to be discovered by the enemy.

However, both the secret communication bases and the communication ships would release tremendous information waves once activated, which would immediately be detected by the ace fleets of the four families. They would be destroyed very soon.

According to the evaluation of the leaders of the Imperial Guards, every mobile secret communication base would be discovered and destroyed by the enemy half an hour after it was activated at most.

The communication ships were faster and had better survival abilities. Starting from the moment they released the first information wave, they could persist about an hour before they were destroyed.

However, the super-remote communication units accommodated on the communication ships were not as powerful, and the information to be transmitted was highly limited.

In other words, the real-time communication between the capital and the Seven Seas Space Zone could last half an hour to one hour at best. Also, each communication session would cost one mobile secret communication base or a valuable communication ship.

Therefore, as long as there were no particularly significant military affairs, the capital and the Seven Seas Space Zone would not turn on the real-time communication channel every day. Instead, they communicated in a more primitive way, which was the communication boats.

The communication boats were a small rapid starship no more than fifty meters long. They did not accommodate any attack magical equipment but were loaded with storage chips.

Every day, the highest commanders in the capital and the Seven Seas Space Zone would save the latest data in the command chain as well as the orders and requests into the storage chips in the boats. Then, the most reliable officers would be sent out to protect them. Through the supermassive teleportation arrays similar to the "cosmic slingshot" and the four-dimensional jump devices on board, they could be delivered to the target Sector directly.

Up until so far, several space gates were still controlled by the reformists on the two ends, allowing him to maintain the communication of personnel and resources to a minimal degree. The landing points of the boats were relatively accurate and would not be too far away from the space gates.

Also, every boat was loaded with the most advanced stealth device. Their size and mass were very small. In the vast space, they were but grains of dust and could barely be detected by the enemy.

Even if they were detected and captured by the enemy, every boat had been set with triple self-destruction facilities. The officer in charge needed to input a security code every five minutes, or all the data would be wiped out in ten seconds. It was definitely safe and secure.

While the efficiency of interaction with the boats was terribly low, the amount of information that each boat could carry was more than what could be transmitted wirelessly for three days and three nights. There was no need to worry that the network might be compromised by the enemy either. The reliability and security of communication were greatly improved.

Therefore, boats became the main means of communication between the capital and the Seven Seas Space Zone. Dozens of boats traveled between the two places every day.

The information was gradually grasped by the ace fleets of the four families.

They were unwilling to be stabbed in the back by the Astounding Thunder Fleet or the garrison fleets under the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors when they launched an attack on the capital planet.

Therefore, after checking the capital, it became their main focus to scan and destroy the boats.

The four families mobilized countless cyberwarfare ships and scout ships to perform the most thorough blockade and scan in the area near the capital planet, not leaving out the slightest space ripple. The moment they sensed space ripples, they would immediately deploy the best rapid response troops to stop the stranger from entering the defense circle of the capital planet.

The Imperial Guards and the Deep Sea Fleet, who had fallen back into the orbit of the capital planet, showed astonishing courage at this moment. They marched out without a care in order to protect the boats that had just jumped to the Empyreal Terminus Sector. They were determined to get the updates from the Seven Seas Space Zone despite the heavy casualties.

The fierce battle today happened exactly because of that.

Three boats from the Seven Seas Space Zone appeared in the space not far away from the capital planet without a sound like inconspicuous loaches. However, they immediately raised the attention of the predators that were lurking nearby.

The starships of the predators unleashed the most dazzling brilliance. Like sharks baring their shining teeth, they lunged at the three tiny loaches aggressively.

The three loaches fled toward the capital planet in panic, but they were caught by a second team of predators, which had arrived from another direction, before they entered the orbit defense circle of the capital planet. Right when the three loaches were about to be torn into shreds by the two groups of sharks, the daring teams of the Deep Sea Fleet and the Imperial Guards marched out and pierced into the sharks like a hundred fast swordfish.

The three loaches took the opportunity to rush toward the capital planet, only to be hit by the cannons of the predators. One of the boats disintegrated on the spot into brilliant fireworks, and the other two boats were riddled with holes and no better than remains. Unable to maintain their flight, they were caught by the gravity of the capital planet and fell to the surface of the capital planet crookedly.

The sharks finally got rid of the swordfish and bit the tails of the other two loaches, raining their firepower on them. The two boats were shaking and sparkling so hard that they seemed to be falling apart in the next second.

Right then, thousands of dazzling lines of fire interweaved into an impenetrable net, covering the sharks overwhelmingly, because they had pressed too forward and approached the orbit defense ring of the capital planet without them realizing it.

No matter how magnificent the ace fleets of the four families were, they were still not enough to compete with the defense arrays of a whole planet that was the heart of the Imperium in terms of firepower.

The few sharks in the lead were immediately ripped into shreds, and the rest of the sharks dispersed, retreating to a safe distance at full speed, before they finally turned around and looked at the two loaches diving into the atmosphere of the capital planet, turning into two clusters of furiously-burning fireballs in the air, and landing on the vast ocean of the capital planet.

Chapter 2595: The Secret Command Center Inside the Royal Palace

On the capital planet, below the atmosphere and above the earth, it was the iron nest where almost a hundred billion people lived.

Faced with the overwhelming fleet that occupied the entire sky, naturally, most of the residents of the capital felt that it was the end of the world.

However, in the competitions of interstellar warships, Colossi, and Immortal Cultivators, the ordinary people who made a living on the surface on the planet did not have any say about the matter. Thanks to the suppression of the reformists and the Imperial Guards who had gone mad, basic order was still maintained on the capital planet after a month of fierce battles.

The central politics district near the royal palace was the place where the most extreme, unwavering, and zealous reformists, as well as a tiny proportion of insiders who had faith in Blackstar the Great, were gathered.

As they were faced with the suppression of the four families' ace fleets, their courage to die together with the enemy was actually triggered. The whole royal palace was enshrouded in gloominess that felt like a concrete black fog.

"Your Grace—"

Deep inside the royal palace, two Imperial Guards supported a lackluster, heavily wounded officer of the Astounding Thunder Fleet to walk to Li Linghai and reported, "The three boats that jumped to the capital today suffered the blockade of the hunting fleets of the four families. One of the boats was blown up on the spot, and the other two lost momentum and defense shields and crashed on the planet.

"They were seriously torn and scourged because they entered the atmosphere from too steep an angle and too fast. It is estimated that one of the boats fell apart directly in the atmosphere into countless pieces that landed on the Silver Moon Ocean. We found part of the remains and confirmed the severity of the damage. It is barely possible that the crew inside survived.

"The other boat, while riddled with holes and terribly torn, managed to keep the basic struggle. The 'Red Box' where the storage chips were kept has been found, and the captain has been saved too.

"The data stored inside the chips are being parsed and read. This is the captain of the boat. At the behest of General Lei Chenghu, he wants to meet Your Grace!"

Deeply seated on the throne that was in the shape of a starship, Li Linghai was completely soaked in darkness although the light was flashing. The only things that could be seen were a few strands of white hair and her eyes that were almost transparent.

It was not until this moment that she finally craned her head out of the dark. She scanned the dreary captain with her eyes that could almost see through his soul before she said in a hollow and unpredictable voice, "What's the situation in the Seven Seas Space Zone? What does Marquis Liaohai want to say to me?"

"To answer your question, Your Grace, we have made significant breakthroughs in the battle of Seven Seas. Our army has crushed Yun Xuefeng's battle formation and even captured 'Landslide', his flagship-in-general. As for the person himself, he has disappeared from space. It is not confirmed yet whether he has died or fled."

The weary captain coughed lightly and said, "However, the four families' coalition fleet hasn't given up resistance yet. Their remaining forces have been gathered under Song Yushi, a seasoned general of the Song family, and they are still resisting our army.

"The details and data of the battle are all stored in the chips. After they are parsed, Your Grace will know everything.

"As for Commander Lei's secret order, he wished to speak to Your Grace in person. Therefore, he was hoping that Your Grace could activate the 'Heaven'-level communication line of the highest secrecy."

"He wants to use a 'Heaven'-level line?"

Li Linghai raised her eyebrow that seemed to be made of iron needles. "So to speak, Marquis Liaohai does have important military affairs to discuss with me?"

The real-time communication between the capital and the Seven Seas Space Zone was solely dependent on the communication ships and the mobile secret communication bases. Such a wireless way of transmitting information and data could be compromised and monitored by the enemy easily.

Chances were that every word that Li Linghai and Lei Chenghu said after their real-time communication began would be heard by the commanders of the four families' ace fleets. How could they fight the war if so?

Therefore, the communication ships and the secret communication bases had all been installed with the state-of-the-art anti-interference and anti-invasion units. According to their levels and performances, they were classified into four levels, namely "Heaven", "Earth", "Dark", and "Yellow". Naturally, the higher their level was, the less likely they would be hacked, and the more valuable they would be.

Among them, there was only one last secret communication base whose security level was "Heaven". Once it was activated and started transmitting abundant information between two Sectors, it would definitely be discovered by the hunting fleets of the four families and destroyed in an hour.

Lei Chenghu wanted to communicate with her in real time even though the cost was the last "Heaven"-level communication line. What was he trying to say?

Li Linghai sank into darkness again and rubbed her temples with her fingers softly. Pondering for a long time, she still decided to grant Lei Chenghu his request.

After all, Lei Chenghu was still the reformists' best warrior at this moment. There were plenty of his admirers even in the Imperial Guards. His prestige had already infiltrated the bones of all the soldiers of the Imperium.

The battle of Seven Seas had also come to the most critical moment. Lei Chenghu must have his own reasons to reach out to the capital in real time at such a great cost.

Besides, in another few days, everything would be settled. Nothing could possibly stop him from achieving an everlasting enterprise!

…

An hour later, deep inside the royal palace, in a secret command center that was independent of the joint command center of the Imperial Guards and the Astounding Thunder Fleet on the ground, Li Linghai stared at the array of super crystal processors that were as magnificent as mountains before her eyes coldly while floating in the midair.

The super crystal processors spurted out the glittering fog that condensed into a vivid light beam that was even more delicate than the Grand Illusionary Land.

She seemed to have crossed the bridge of time and space and directly appeared on the bridge of "Iron Torrent", the flagship of the Astounding Thunder Fleet, communicating with Lei Chenghu face to face.

No, it was not her. Her long hair and her eyes had turned black like abysses, and gloomy dominance could be found on her face. She seemed to be surrounded by a few raging purple dragons in black scales. It was Blackstar the Great who was controlling the body.

In the vast space at the periphery of the Empyreal Terminus Sector, a secret communication base that was as cold as an inactive volcano was woken up all of a sudden. Unleashing waves of a special frequency and slashing the barrier of the three-dimensional universe, it connected the data of two worlds that were as abundant as an ocean.

In front of Wuying Qi, colorful swirls were wriggling and gathering into a gate of rainbows. Lei Chenghu's image that looked like a tough iron statue appeared from the rainbow gate clearly.

"Your Majesty."

Lei Chenghu bowed at Wuying Qi deeply, with admiration and zealousness all over his face.

"Marquis Liaohai, courtesies are unnecessary. Time is precious. Let's be straightforward!"

Wuying Qi said gloomily, "We have only one last 'Heaven'-level line left. The secret communication bases that supported such real-time lines usually survived less than forty minutes in the past, which means that our communication may be cut off by the enemy any time after twenty to thirty minutes.

"I've read the battlefield data that you sent through boats over the past few days. Yun Xuefeng's defeat and death is only a matter of time, and it was within our expectation that Song Yushi, the old cunning fox, still kept part of the combat ability since he had been dawdling at the edge of the space zone. I don't think you are activating the extremely precious line just because of Song Yushi and Yue Xuefeng.

"Therefore, did something unexpected happen in the Seven Seas Space Zone?"

"Yes, Your Majesty."

Lei Chenghu said, "To be more precise, it was not something unexpected, but someone expected—this guy."

Lei Chenghu waved his hands. A tiny holographic window was immediately opened next to them, displaying the picture where three Colossi, countless crystal suits, and crystal tanks barraged and blew up a space shuttle.

However, right when the space shuttle exploded, a vigorous shadow jumped out from it and shouted in the most high-pitched voice, "Commander Lei, what are you doing? We are great heroes, and we should cherish each other! We are the same kind of people! We can agree to disagree and create a beautiful future together!

"Don't be so stubborn! At least run some investigation! There's a scheme in the capital! A great scheme!

"You… You stupid moron! My mouth is getting dry, and you still do not believe me? You will regret it! You will definitely regret it! Ahhhhhhhhhhhhh!"

Like a burning turkey, the guy jumped up and down in the storm of bullets and fled in panic.

"Huh…"

Seeing that, Wuying Qi narrowed his eyes and rubbed his nose. He said in a sigh, "Li Yao, the Cultivator who always appears in a wrong place at a wrong time and who brings nothing but trouble, has indeed emerged again. However, how did you manage to surround him? I can tell that the place of your battle is a garage on Iron Torrent. Is he alive or dead? What did he say to you?"

"It's indeed the Cultivator Li Yao that Your Majesty reminded me of earlier, and the place was truly a garage on Iron Torrent. However, I did not surround him; it was he who voluntarily fed himself to me."

Emotionlessly, Lei Chenghu said, "He came to my flagship like an idiot, with nobody else other than Li Wuji as a disguise, and tried to sow discord between me and Your Majesty. He was even trying to talk me into joining his side and cooperating with him to deal with Your Majesty.

"I was not a three-year-old, and I was certainly not tricked by him, but I pretended to agree with him to ease his wariness. Then, I concentrated on the firepower of three Colossi and a whole marine team, hoping to annihilate him once and for all.

"It's a shame that while the guy's brain was missing, he did have remarkable brawns. Despite the unfavorable circumstances, and even though he was faced with the destructive scourge of firepower, he was still able to take out a Cosmos Ring from somewhere unbelievable, summoning his Colossus. Eventually, he still got away and escaped."

Chapter 2596: The Sun Shooting Plan!

While talking, Lei Chenghu displayed dozens of holograms, which were the battle pictures captured by dozens of crystal suits and crystal tanks from different perspectives.

It could be seen that Li Yao was flying freely in the midst of fire like a cockroach that had grown wings on the picture. Very soon, he found a gap and unleashed thousands of rays. The Colossus "Arsonist" arrived magnificently!

In the next moment, all the holograms shook hard, and the picture was disrupted by the waves released by the Arsonist.

Very soon, smoke and fire covered all the cameras, and the whole garage fell into chaos. As it turned out, the Arsonist had blown a hole in the airtight door of the garage. All the materials inside were absorbed into the vacuum, including most of the crystal suits and the crystal tanks.

The three Colossi, on the other hand, were rather restrained in the battle because they were fighting on "Iron Torrent", the Astounding Thunder Fleet's flagship.

Li Yao took the opportunity to flee. Turning into a glittering ray of light, he ran into space.

"Not only was the combat ability of this 'Vulture Li Yao' astonishingly high, but he was also using a very advanced Colossus. It was more advanced than any Colossus in the Astounding Thunder Fleet."

Lei Chenghu gnashed his teeth. "I deployed seven Colossi and thousands of crystal suits and armed shuttles to surround him. However, the space was too vast and it was too unexpected. We were unable to block a Divinity Transformation Stage expert equipped with a state-of-the-art Colossus. Please forgive us, Your Majesty!"

"That was not your fault."

Wuying Qi said leniently, "This Li Yao is like an unbeatable and uncatchable cockroach. He is unbelievably shameless and cunning. Even I failed to catch him on the capital planet when I employed abundant troops. It is understandable that the scoundrel escaped from Marquis Liaohai's hands when you were in a fierce battle against Yun Xuefeng and Song Yushi's fleets.

"Thankfully, just like a cockroach, the man is upsetting but cannot affect the big picture. Marquis Liaohai, if such trivia are all that you want to say, you could've reported it to me via boats. There was no need for you to use the precious 'Heaven'-level communication line to ask for my forgiveness.

"I'm afraid that you misunderstood, Your Majesty. While it was regretful that Li Yao ran loose, asking for forgiveness is not my main purpose."

Emotionlessly and solemnly, Lei Chenghu went on, "In order to convince me, Li Yao told me a lot of things in his space shuttle, including plenty of slanders about Your Majesty…"

Amused, Lei Chenghu interrupted him, "Marquis Liaohai, You did not buy into his words, did you? Think about the destruction of 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky' or the so-called 'arrival of the Black Wind King'. Li Yao is best known for his mouthfuls of lies and his psychological tricks. Even dead people may come to life again under his persuasion.

"It is in fact very easy to deal with such silver tongues. As long as you turn a deaf ear to him and do not believe anything he says, there will be nothing he can do.

"Marquis Liaohai, you have been the most loyal and determined Immortal Cultivator for two hundred years. You are not changing your beliefs just because of a few words spoken by a Cultivator, are you?"

"That's absolutely right, Your Majesty. Naturally, I would never believe this Vulture Li Yao's bulls*it."

Lei Chenghu said, "However, something he said was indeed what had been worrying me. Even though I attacked him decisively at that time, the concerns have been haunting me and corrupting my beliefs since he escaped. I was almost mentally deranged.

"As Your Majesty knows, I am burning my soul to the maximum every second for the battle of Seven Seas. My body and my brain are already seriously exhausted and may collapse easily in the first place. If I'm mentally deranged at this moment, the outcome of the battle of Seven Seas may be unpredictable.

"Therefore, I boldly asked Your Majesty to activate the most precious secret line, hoping that Your Majesty could answer the questions for me and stabilize my mind to ensure the victory of the battle of Seven Seas!"

"Oh?"

Wuying Qi squinted. Gloomily, he opened his mouth slowly and asked, "What are your concerns, Marquis Liaohai?"

"It's Your Majesty's plan of victory in the capital."

Lei Chenghu went on, "Li Yao claimed that Your Majesty had dug a gigantic trap in the capital and prepared an enormous scheme that would affect the future of the Imperium and even mankind.

"I absolutely did not believe a word he said. However, when the Astounding Thunder Fleet left the heartland of the four families, we were both very clear that the unshackled ace fleets of the four families would attack the capital immediately.

"At that time, you asked me to fight the battle of Seven Seas in full confidence and said that you were definitely capable of resisting the four families' ace fleets until I won the battle in the Seven Seas Space Zone and united all the forces of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors as well as the warlords at the edge of the Imperium.

"However, I have calculated the combat ability of the Imperial Guards and the Deep Sea Fleet. Even though their combat abilities were multiplied, it would still be impossible for them to defeat the four families' ace fleets. Besides, the four families had not just the few ace fleets but also the fleets from a hundred Sectors, which could all jump to the Empyreal Terminus Sector during emergencies. How are you going to address them, Your Majesty?

"I asked Your Majesty the question at that time, but Your Majesty told me that you had a secret weapon that you could not reveal yet for confidentiality purposes. You asked me to focus on the battle in the Seven Seas Space Zone, assuring me that you would tell me everything when the timing was ripe.

"I believe that the timing is already very ripe right now. I have collapsed Yun Xuefeng's main forces in the Seven Seas Space Zone. Even though Song Yushi is resisting, he cannot hold on for long. In the capital, the four families' ace fleets have occupied the planets and the stone belts near the capital planet, and they are about to launch a final attack when the two planets are closest to each other on their orbits. It seems that no miracles could stop the capital from falling again.

"Your Majesty, whatever trump cards you have, you can tell them to me now, can't you? I still remember my ecstasy when you revealed your identity to me. I'm ready to die for you any second, Your Majesty. Can't you even trust me, Your Majesty?"

As he approached the end, Lei Chenghu was blushing and shouting hoarsely, as if he truly felt that he was terribly wronged.

"About that…"

His hands on his back, Wuying Qi pondered for a long time. "Marquis Liaohai, you've misunderstood me. Ever since I was reborn, everybody who got attached to me did so for certain interests. Li Linghai was so, Yue Wushuang was so, and Dongfang Sheng was so too! Everybody is fighting for themself, but Marquis Liaohai, you are the only one who is fighting for the Imperium, for the true path of immortality, and for the civilization of mankind!

"If I can't trust you, who else can I trust? It was only because my plan was too dangerous and could only work with absolute confidentiality. Should anything be leaked out, we might be doomed forever!"

Lei Chenghu said, "That's the reason why I asked Your Majesty to activate the 'Heaven'-level communication line. Only in this line that will never be hacked can Your Majesty reveal the whole plan without concerns."

Wuying Qi asked, "Marquis Liaohai, do you really want to know it?"

Lei Chenghu replied, "Your Majesty, I have to know it. If it is kept a secret from me, my beliefs may collapse any moment, which will result in the failure of the battle of Seven Seas."

"Fine…"

Taking a deep breath, Wuying Qi stared at Lei Chenghu in the eyes and said most sincerely, "Like I said, there are no secrets between Marquis Liaohai and I. What can't I tell Marquis Liaohai when I have already told you the secret that I hid in the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum for a thousand years and returned to the Imperium in Li Linghai's body? Now, I will inform you of the 'Sun Shooting Plan' exactly as it is."

"The Sun Shooting Plan?"

Lei Chenghu was slightly stunned. "… Houyi shooting the suns?"

"Exactly. The tale of Houyi shooting the suns is a legend that has passed on among human beings for hundreds of thousands of years. Eliminating the ridiculous, mysterious parts, we now know that there was indeed a 'Houyi Clan' in the thirteen carbon-based lives that made up the Pangu civilizations' alliance. As natural born 'star specialists', they were adept at the studies on most types of stars in the universe. They were also good at absorbing energy from all kinds of stars, or even… altering the statuses of the stars!"

Wuying Qi opened his hands. The Grand Illusionary Land behind him immediately turned into a vast cosmos where ten stars of different types showed up. Some were exploding intensely like supernovas, some were as huge as red giants, and some were unleashing colorful, dazzling brilliance while waving tentacles that looked like fiery rains. It almost seemed that ten suns were in the sky at the same time.

Right under the radiations of the ten suns, Wuying Qi explained slowly, "Stars are the sources of life and the most important celestial bodies in the universe that entail infinite energy. All the civilizations in the past and in the future need the illumination and nurturing of stars in order to live and prosper.

"Stars seem to have a stable structure and may have a longevity of hundreds of billions of years, living a life almost as long as the universe itself. Most of the time, they live in the prime years where they are extremely steady as they unleash light, heat, and all kinds of radiations to the entire system. Even if they do decay into white dwarfs or even black holes, it will take so much time that the brief civilizations originating from planets like ours can never feel.

"However, from another point of view, it is rather… easy, or at least theoretically possible, to slightly alter the status of a star so that it can unleash the furious energy that will sweep across the whole system.

"Because stars are too enormous and powerful, even if only one billionth of its force is triggered, it will be enough for the insignificant creatures like us to sense the destructive tide.

"It's almost like a sneeze from the sun is enough to blow away all the warships scattered in space."

Chapter 2597: Houyi Shooting the Sun!

Behind Wuying Qi, nine out of the ten suns had all dimmed and died out, and only the last one, which seemed to be steady and unattractive in the prime years, was magnified nonstop, revealing the crazily-dancing fire and the swirls that were enough to swallow planets on the surface. Lei Chenghu couldn't help but feel that it could burn everything.

As a contrast, some insignificant glass pebbles were scattered around the glittering sun. They were all the planets that were moving around the sun.

Around those planets, on the other hand, were clusters of dust. Even though Lei Chenghu had fully activated his spiritual energy in the Divinity Transformation Stage, he could only vaguely see the black spots that made up the dust.

The black spots that were no larger than dust were exactly the super warships that human beings were proud of, thinking that they could conquer the sea of stars with them.

Suddenly, the sun sneezed.

Or rather, a certain scorching swirl that only took up less than one percent of the surface of the sun had a minor sneeze.

Immediately, countless flames millions of kilometers long spurted out, snatching at the cosmos like the arms of gods that had been extended out of the sun.

Although the arms did not grasp any specific matter, they were like hands churning a lake and soon raised overwhelming, destructive waves.

The arms split, spread, and exploded. The crimson waves swept across all the planets like a cosmic storm.

Even the planets protected by the atmosphere sensed the intense shock. It was needless to say the fleets that were no more than dust. They were all blown apart or even shattered into smithereens.

"I am not a man who underestimates myself. However, how small and weak is the civilization of mankind compared to the formidable universe? The starships we are proud of, the Colossi that we consider as 'ultimate weapons', and our marvelous spiritual energy and techniques… All of these are nothing when faced with the wrath of the universe. They can't stop anything."

Wuying Qi heaved a deep sigh. "Since ancient times, the classic battle cases where the weak defeated the strong couldn't have been achieved without favorable circumstances. In some cases, the magnificence power of nature was the key to victory. Too many overwhelming armies were lost in the boundless deserts or destroyed by the surging tides in an ocean.

"It is not going to be an exception this time.

"I have never considered winning the war with only the Imperial Guards, the Deep Sea Fleet, or Marquis Liaohai's Astounding Thunder Fleet. Faced with enemies who are a hundred times more than us, any courage, determination, or schemes would be useless.

"However, even if the four Kurfürst families were ten times, a hundred times, or a thousand times stronger, they would still be dead for sure in front of the roars of the sun!"

Lei Chenghu's eyes were so deeply attracted by the exasperated sun that a minor volcano eruption seemed to have taken place inside his pupils. Staring at the virtual picture for a long time, he couldn't help but ask, "Does Your Majesty have a way… to make the sun sneeze?"

"Yes."

Wuying Qi smiled. "Like I said just now, I would never underestimate myself. However insignificant human beings and however vast and formidable the universe is, human beings will completely conquer and utilize the universe someday!

"While stars are among the most important celestial bodies in the universe, their structures and state of operation are relatively simple, even simpler than many planets. The stars in the prime years can run stably mainly because of the subtle balance when the power of fusion offsets gravity. You can imagine them to be extremely sensitive scales. As a matter of fact, as long as a tiny force is imposed on them, it will be possible to break the balance and make them plummet toward chaos and destruction.

"However, the scales are too enormous for the knowledge and technology of mankind. Precise calculations must be run, and all the features of the scales have to be grasped, in order to find the point to impose force upon.

"As long as the point is found, it will be possible to trigger a series of chain reactions. We can make the sun sneeze without the least trouble.

"There's no need to doubt our abilities. In fact, the whole thing is like a naïve child playing on a dry, windy hill. He accidentally caused a sparkle, which led to a forest fire that burnt down dozens of mountains and killed countless creatures. With the child's own heart, wisdom, and strength, was he capable of burning dozens of mountains and killing countless people? Of course not! However, when all the conditions were miraculously met at the right moment in the right place, one child and one sparkle would be enough for that.

"It's the same for us right now. All we need to do is to project a tiny sparkle toward the sun."

Lei Chenghu was silent for a long time, as if he were assessing the veracity of the matter. He asked cautiously, "How did Your Majesty learn the specific features of the star in the Empyreal Terminus Sector, how would you grasp the subtle balance point, and how are you going to send the 'sparkle' to the sun, Your Majesty?"

"This is exactly why I have named the plan 'Sun Shooting', from the tale 'Houyi shooting the sun'. Marquis Liaohai is not wrong at all. It hasn't been long since I woke up from a thousand years of dormancy. I do not control any study institutions or specialists in the Imperium. Naturally, I don't have enough time to understand the features of the sun. However, the Houyi Clan, part of the Pangu civilizations' alliance in the past, was capable of it."

Wuying Qi said, "I don't know how much you know about the Houyi Clan, Marquis Liaohai. They were a carbon-based life born on the planets very close to the sun. The planets suitable for the survival of mankind were usually the third or the fourth planet in a stellar system. However, the Houyi Clan mainly lived on the second planet of a stellar system.

"The Houyi Clan at the beginning were insignificant bugs that were covered in black shells that looked like solar panels. They were strongly resistant to heat. However, their living environment was too rough. The star granted them too much light, heat, and destructive radiations. They had to observe the star all the time and adjust the elements and internal structures of their black shells according to the radiations to absorb energy as much as possible and to block radiations so that they could survive in such an extreme environment.

"In the Pangu civilizations' alliance, there was also the Kuafu Clan, whose people were also star specialists born in similar ways. They pursued the sun to obtain energy. However, the Kuafu Clan's specialty was the stable utilization of the energy from the stars. They established abundant energy-absorbing panels on the orbit of the star, trying to enshroud the whole star with a piece of magical equipment named 'Kuafu's Sphere', thereby achieving a 100% utilization rate of energy.

"The Houyi Clan differed from the Kuafu Clan who were relatively mild and peace-loving in that they were tougher and better at breaking the subtle balance in stars to unleash the most formidable power instantly for war and destruction purposes.

"It is said that millions of years ago, before the Pangu civilizations' alliance was established, the Kuafu Clan and the Houyi Clan, two interstellar civilizations both good at making use of stars, once had a protracted, magnificent war.

"At that time, the Kuafu Clan was developed earlier. They stepped out of their mother planet almost ten thousand years earlier than the Houyi Clan did, colonizing the dozens of neighboring Sectors. They found ten stars in those Sectors that were very stable and suitable for energy collection.

"Around the ten stars, the Kuafu Clan set up 'Kuafu's Spheres' and established a glorious and powerful interstellar civilization with the energy of the ten suns. They suppressed all the developing civilizations within their range of exploration, not giving the latecomers a chance to surpass themselves.

"The Houyi Clan that was born in similar ways and equally good at making use of stars was their main target.

"At first, the Houyi Clan was absolutely no match for the Kuafu Clan. They suffered a losing streak because of the energy that the enemy collected from the ten suns. The whole civilization was almost obliterated.

"Everybody in the Houyi Clan said that the enemy was about to roast them with the ten suns.

"In the end, the Houyi Clan gathered all their elites and ambushed the stars with dozens of daring teams, finally detonating nine out of the ten stable stars under the Kuafu Clan's control.

"The nine stars had drastic changes and unleashed destructive flames and radiations, almost wiping out all the Kuafu's Spheres and the Kuafu Clan's fleets.

"The Kuafu Clan was covered in the infinite darkness. The fleets wailed at the dim, messy cosmos after the solar storms. They jumped from one Sector to another, but the tragedy was the same. Most fleets died of insufficient resources before they reached the last sun that was still steady and peaceful.

"The battle completely reversed the comparison of the Kuafu Clan and the Houyi Clan. Having achieved a strategic advantage, the Houyi Clan was determined to pursue further and shoot down the Kuafu Clan's last sun. Such an action would not bring them much good. After all, they were a species that depended on stars too. However, their strong hatred and their fear that the Kuafu Clan might rise again prompted them to obliterate the Kuafu Clan even though they would also suffer heavy losses in the process.

"At this moment, the Pangu Clan showed up.

"Or rather, the intense transformations of the nine suns made the Pangu Clan discover the Kuafu Clan and the Houyi Clan. They learned that the Pangu Clan was not the only intelligent carbon-based life in this vast universe.

"At that time, the Pangu Clan already boasted a civilization and war ability that was far beyond the Kuafu Clan and the Houyi Clan. However, they were still amazed at how the two carbon-based lives utilized stars—either in the peaceful way or in the violent way.

"The Pangu Clan realized that the carbon-based intelligent lives had been developed to such an appalling level that their war was very likely to destroy the whole universe or even jeopardize the civilizations that were not related to the war. The war among the carbon-based intelligent lives had to be stopped in order to maintain the peace of the universe. Therefore, the Pangu Clan intermediated in the war between the Kuafu Clan and the Houyi Clan, secretly supporting the Kuafu Clan and preventing the Houyi Clan from destroying the Kuafu Clan's last sun. Then, an agreement between the three parties was reached. That was perhaps the prototype of the Pangu civilizations' alliance later.

"I'm telling this not to waste your time but to let Marquess Liaohai know that the Houyi Clan mastered the ability to 'make the sun sneeze' a long time ago, and it almost resulted in the complete destruction of an interstellar civilization after it was devoted to a real battle!"

Chapter 2598: The Gold Crystal Pyramid!

Attracted, Lei Chenghu finally took a long breath in relief and asked suspiciously, "Have you mastered the Houyi Clan's technology to 'activate the sun', Your Majesty?"

Wuying went on, "After the establishment of the Pangu civilizations' alliance, the Houyi Clan and the Kuafu Clan continued their studies on stars. Spending tens of thousands of years, they measured the parameters and qualities of countless stars in the three thousand Sectors. They also ran experiments and constructions on the stars that were at strategic locations. There were both the 'Kuafu spheres' in peaceful periods and 'star bombs' and 'stellar storm generators' that were designed for wars.

"When the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan ended on bad terms and started the primeval war, most of the Houyi Clan were on the Pangu Clan. When the Nuwa Clan was gradually winning the war, the Houyi Clan was insane enough to propose a series of technologies to boost the sun and die together with the enemy. They established a glorious 'solar defense line' with countless stars, trying to stop the march of the Nuwa Clan.

"Imagine it. On the routes of the Nuwa Clan's army when they were jumping forward, thousands of suns were infuriated at the same time. The infinite fire walls that they spurted out divided the whole universe into two halves, one dazzling and the other dark. What a magnificent and soul-stirring scene it must've been!

"The Empyreal Terminus Sector is at a special astrological location where thousands of wormholes intersect. Naturally, it was the main focus of the 'solar defense line'. The Houyi Clan set up a very powerful device to activate the sun on the main planet of the Empyreal Terminus Sector, or the capital as we know it. It was called 'Gold Crystal Pyramid'."

"Gold Crystal Pyramid?"

Lei Chenghu slightly frowned. "Since it is a 'pyramid', it should be a massive, eye-catching building. Why have I never heard that there's such a wonder in the capital?"

Wuying Qi smiled and said, "The Gold Crystal Pyramid is a piece of 'stellar magical equipment' that the Houyi Clan produced after tens of thousands of years of studies on the stars. They were established inside every stellar system that had a steady and powerful star. Most of the 'Gold Crystal Pyramids' have a square pedestal and a sharp tip. Such a structure could focus the energy as much as possible, shooting it at the star billions of kilometers away as a signal to activate the sun.

"However, while the Houyi Clan made full preparations during the primeval war, the situation on the battlefield was not worsened to the point that they needed to infuriate thousands of suns. Or rather, the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, the Houyi Clan, and the other carbon-based species were all drained up by the war. Even if thousands of suns were sacrificed, their fate of being destroyed couldn't have been changed.

"Therefore, the Houyi Clan only activated part of the 'Gold Crystal Pyramids'. The majority of them stood after the destruction of the primeval civilizations and returned to the universe of ignorance, including the one on the capital planet.

"In the hundreds of thousands of years to come, due to the tectonic changes, the Gold Crystal Pyramids on the surface of the planet dropped nonstop. By the time mankind rose, most of them had already collapsed deep into the planet, melted into rocks, or were completely broken. Naturally, they were never known."

Thinking for a moment, Lei Chenghu asked, "Since the Houyi Clan are already extinct, and most of the Gold Crystal Pyramids have been buried and ruined during the hundreds of thousands of years, how did Your Majesty learn the existence of the 'stellar magical equipment'?"

"I learned the relevant knowledge in the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum. To be more exact, it was an incomplete volume of the Book of Revelations, in which the mysteries about the utilization of stellar energy were recorded. Of course, there were also ways to activate the 'Gold Crystal Pyramids' to turn the sun into the most powerful weapon!"

Wuying Qi said, "Do you still recall the myth of the rise of mankind ten thousand years ago, where the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God, his strongest clone, fought so hard on the capital planet that the capital planet was almost blown into halves?

"In fact, the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God both commanded billions of soldiers from a thousand worlds. How could they have dueled with each other? No matter how strong they were in person, it could not decide the outcome of a cosmic war or the face of the universe!

"The truth was that the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God did run into each other below the capital planet with the elites of their own. However, it was not an idiotic 'duel', but a mutual search for the 'Gold Crystal Pyramid' that sank into the depths of the capital planet.

"During his rise, the Supreme Emperor got three volumes of the Book of Revelations in total. One of them recorded the ways to utilize and boost the energy of stars. The coordinates of some of the Gold Crystal Pyramids and the ways to control them seemed to have been marked too.

"The Blood God, as the Supreme Emperor's clone, shared part of his memories. Naturally, he also grasped the secrets of the 'Gold Crystal Pyramids'.

"Both the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God believed that the 'Gold Crystal Pyramid' deep inside the capital planet was the most important one on the 'solar defense line' that the Houyi Clan set up in the old days. Therefore, it must've boasted the best defense and maintenance facilities. Even if all the other Gold Crystal Pyramids had turned rusty, the one here must be intact.

"As long as they discovered the Gold Crystal Pyramid, they would be able to control the sun of the Empyreal Terminus Sector to some extent.

"At that time, the fleets loyal to the Supreme Emperor and the rebels loyal to the Blood God were all gathered in the Empyreal Terminus Sector for an ultimate war that would decide the future of mankind. The situation was almost exactly like it is today. Whoever raised a minor, controllable solar storm would likely be the final winner!

"Therefore, while thousands of starships were engaged in space, the two of them led elite teams to excavate the Gold Crystal Pyramid at great risks.

"It was a shame that the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God were the same person and thought the same. The elite teams of the two parties had an unexpected encounter below the ground. After a lose-lose battle, not only were the two teams wiped out, but their leaders were also heavily wounded. It also led to the great tectonic movements in the shell of the capital planet. Indescribable energy blew out of the core of the planet and almost kept the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God below the ground for good. That's why legends have it that the capital planet was almost blown apart by the two experts.

"When the two commanders escaped from the bottom of the planet in frustration, they both lost the abilities to command the battle or to excavate the Gold Crystal Pyramid again.

"The Gold Crystal Pyramid, the sun controller hidden deep inside the capital planet, was never discovered when the Star Ocean Imperium crashed.

"As for me… Marquis Liaohai, you must know that I spent hundreds of years in the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum. During the time, I accidentally got an incomplete volume of the Book of Revelations, on which the Supreme Emperor's regretful soul signature was lingering. In it, he specifically described the underground environment and the geological parameters he met during the exploration of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. He was very certain that his team and the Blood God's were in fact very close to the Gold Crystal Pyramid when the battle began. It was safe to say that either of them could've touched the key to controlling the sun if they had excavated for a few more days without interference. What a jest of fate to have arranged for them to encounter in such an environment.

"The rest is history. After I returned to the Imperium, I began to try to accumulate my strength and plan for the great cause to cleanse the corruption and renew the Imperium. At first, I was forced to take action under the identity of 'queen of the Imperium', and I was hamstrung in many aspects. Naturally, it was impossible for me to excavate in the depths of the capital planet on a large scale. However, since we occupied the whole capital planet, I was finally able to take my chances. Perhaps the Supreme Emperor was watching over us from places we cannot see because I did find the 'Gold Crystal Pyramid' successfully!"

Lei Chenghu still exclaimed although he had known the result. He said, "No wonder Li Yao told me that he saw Your Majesty's Imperial Guards digging an enormous hole mysteriously below the capital planet. So, that was the 'Gold Crystal Pyramid'."

"What?"

This time, Wuying Qi, who wouldn't have changed his expression even if a planet fell apart right before him, was shocked. His eyelids and lips quickly shivered as he blurted out, "That scoundrel knows that, too?"

Realizing his loss of calmness, Wuying Qi took a deep breath and resumed tranquility. He said casually, "Yes, that's exactly the Gold Crystal Pyramid. Now, Marquis Liaohai, you should know that I did not lie to you and have shared all the secrets with you, right? Hehe, Li Yao thought everybody else was as sordid as him. Did he really think he could sow discord between us in such a way? How ridiculous!"

While saying, Wuying Qi suddenly thought of something else. His face slightly changed again, and hints of anxiety beamed out of his eyes.

Lei Chenghu coughed somewhat in embarrassment and said, "I'm certainly grateful for Your Majesty's trust, and I cannot repay one billionth of Your Majesty's appreciation even with my life. However, this 'Gold Crystal Pyramid' was a piece of stellar magical equipment from hundreds of thousands of years ago, designed and developed by the Houyi Clan. It must be very different from what human beings are used to. Can it really unleash infinite power in your hands, Your Majesty?"

"The Gold Crystal Pyramid is more or less damaged after hundreds of thousands of years. The volume of the Book of Revelation to maneuver the Gold Crystal Pyramid is incomplete and obscure too. Even the Supreme Emperor failed to completely understand it in the old days. It is even less possible for me to grasp it in only several hundred years."

Wuying Qi smiled and said, "Thankfully, our enemy is not the tricky opponents like the Nuwa Clan or the Kuafu Clan, but only people who are as unused to radiation as us.

"As long as we can activate one tenth of the power of the 'Gold Crystal Pyramid', it will be enough to raise a solar storm that will affect the whole Empyreal Terminus Sector. All the starships in space will be blown apart. The enemy's Spiritual Nexus will suffer destructive interference, their magical equipment units completely paralyzed. Even the vitality field of most soldiers would be temporarily disrupted, giving the enemy a huge headache and depriving them of their combat ability!"

Chapter 2599: Your Majesty, You Must Be Wary of Li Yao!

Deep in thought, Lei Chenghu asked, "Is it so powerful?"

"You can doubt anything, but you should never doubt the power of a star."

Wuying Qi said, "A star in the prime years can be compared to a gigantic living creature that has periodic activities like breath. In the Empyreal Terminus Sector, the cycle of every 'breath' of the sun is fourteen years. After every fourteen years, the sun will reach the most intense phase of stellar activities.

"During the phase, all kinds of radiations, magnetic fields, particles, and even the protons inside the sun that are involved in the spiritual energy will be spurted out at a high speed, sweeping across the whole system like countless burning tentacles.

"Even on the capital planet, the power of solar activities can still be felt. Volcano eruptions, tsunamis, earthquakes, spiritual energy disruption, and even the mental derangement of the Immortal Cultivators all have a lot to do with the solar activities.

"The universe is a giant magnetic field, and the human body is a small one. The training of the Immortal Cultivators is exactly to integrate the two magnetic fields into a new, powerful one.

"Just like pigeons that have to tell the direction by using the magnetically sensitive areas inside their heads, the Immortal Cultivators are unusually sensitive to all kinds of magnetic fields.

"When the solar activities are in the period of outbreaks, pigeons will be lost due to the disruption of the magnetic field. How could the Immortal Cultivators remain unaffected? Even the experts in the Nascent Soul Stage, the Divinity Transformation Stage, or even the Divinity Branching Stage are too insignificant when faced with the might of a star!

"That's still a pure natural environment without any human intervention under the protection of the atmosphere. Still, the consequences are severe enough.

"This year is exactly the period of solar outbreaks in the fourteen-year cycle in the Empyreal Terminus Sector. If we could add oil to the fire and make the solar activities on the sun more intense, it will definitely cause a destructive blow to the ace fleets of the four families!

"The supermassive magical equipment like starships are developing toward precision and automation today. The more precise, the more vulnerable. The more automatic, the more they will depend on the system. When particles penetrate through all the main warships in the ace fleets like meteor showers, paralyzing their mainframe crystal processors and power units and casting most of the crew members into a comatose state, those warships as big as mountains, the greatest reliance for the four families to dominate the universe, will become iron coffins flowing along the current, completely at our mercy!"

Profound sparks glittered in Lei Chenghu's eyes as he said, "So to speak, the solar storm will kill all the soldiers in the four families' ace fleets?"

"How is it possible?"

Wuying Qi burst into laughter. "I understand what you are saying, Marquis Liaohai. You are highly reluctant to see a civil war in the Imperium. To be honest, I'm unwilling to see the bloodstained scene too. Both Marquis Liaohai and most of the soldiers under the four families' command are my subjects and the sabers which I count on to conquer the universe. Now, our great enemy, the Covenant Alliance, hasn't shown up yet. How could I break my sabers for no good reason?

"Rest assured. The effect of the solar storm will only be temporary. The starships and the soldiers will only be paralyzed for a few days, which should be enough for our army to capture all the soldiers in all the ace fleets. I believe that they will certainly realize their mistake and walk on the correct path to serve their country after they are escorted to the capital planet and educated by me and Marquis Liaohai. Is such a result acceptable for you, Marquis Liaohai?"

Lei Chenghu thought for a moment and asked again, "Shouldn't the solar storm be indiscriminate? If the enemy is fully paralyzed, wouldn't our army be in too much pain to move anymore too? How are we going to capture the enemy?"

"Please look at this map, Marquis Liaohai."

Wuying Qi made a gesture. The enormous star behind him immediately turned into a map of the universe with dense spots and rays. "According to my observation of the sun with the Gold Crystal Pyramid and the data left by the Houyi Clan, there will be a sunspot outbreak of a minor scale in this area of the sun a day later.

"By then, the capital planet and the No. 5 planet will be relatively close to each other on their orbits. At the same time, the No. 5 planet will be at its perihelion.

"You know that the four families have conquered the No. 5 planet and regarded it as an advanced base to attack the capital. I have received solid intelligence that tomorrow will be the time of their general attack.

"By then, 80% of the main warships of the four families' ace fleets will have gathered around the perihelion, and they will have to face the sun defenselessly. The mainframe crystal processors and the power units on board will be running at the highest efficiency. All the magical equipment and crystal chips will shoulder the greatest pressure. A lot of crystal suits and Colossi would even be projected outside of the starships and float in space in their ignorance.

"When the solar storm sweeps over, they will naturally suffer the heaviest blow.

"Our army, on the other hand, is the defender. I have ordered most of the fleets to stay back within the atmosphere. The thick and dense atmosphere of the capital planet is the hardest 'shield' to resist the magnetic fields, the radiations, and the high-energy particles. They are enough to offset more than 90% of the power of the solar storm.

"On the other hand, I will order all the starships to turn off their mainframe crystal processors and power units when the solar storm takes place and ask all the soldiers to crawl into the protective gel and the starships to raise the spiritual shield of a special frequency.

"The so-called 'solar storm', as a matter of fact, is a mass of high-intensity waves. As long as we learn the frequency in advance and set the spiritual shield to the corresponding frequency, it will be enough to offset 90% of the damage.

"In such a way, at most, the blow of the solar storm that our army suffers will be 5% that of the enemy's. When the solar storm is over, it will naturally be easy for us to move forward and capture the enemy in bulk."

"Simple as that?"

Lei Chenghu asked, "There are no side effects at all? No damage will be caused to the surface of the capital planet by the orbits that pierce through the atmosphere or to the space towns connected to the orbits?"

"Marquis Liaohai, I don't want to lie to you."

Wuying Qi sighed and said, "The stellar magical equipment is too horrifying when it is on a rampage. How can there be no side effects or sequela?

"I can order the Imperial Guards and the soldiers of the Deep Sea Fleet to crawl into the protective gels in advance as a precaution. However, for confidentiality, it is certainly impossible to evacuate or warn the residents on the capital planet, much less the space residents that are not under the protection of the atmosphere. The outbreak of the solar storm will indeed affect them. Some of them whose body and soul are weak may even… die.

"Neither can I guarantee that all the soldiers of the ace fleets of the four families will only pass out but not die. Who knows whether or not their brains had any conditions before, or if their starships were seriously damaged in the previous battles, or if they would do anything unreasonable when they are panicking?

"This is a war. How can there be no victims in a war? If we do not resort to a secret weapon like the Gold Crystal Pyramid but fight in a regular way, will nobody die? Considering the style of the four families, will they not massacre the people after they occupy the capital? Does your Astounding Thunder Fleet only injure the enemy but do not kill them in the battles in the Seven Seas Space Zone?

"I can only guarantee you that, according to my accurate calculation, the casualty rate of the solar storm is 5% at most. Also, the victims will be the weakest and the least determined people. By the law of the Immortal Cultivators, such people are not qualified to survive in the first place, are they?"

"No…"

Lei Chenghu pondered for a moment and said, "A death rate of 5% is indeed an acceptable figure. Our army will be the sure loser in a regular battle. In the massacre after the four families occupy the capital, as well as the plagues and famines that will come with the massacre, the victims will definitely be more than 5%.

"In order to settle the civil war, build a new Imperium, and deal with the Covenant Alliance, the price of several billion people is not unacceptable. Did Your Majesty keep it a secret from me because you suspect that I would hesitate?"

"Absolutely not. I have never doubted Marquis Liaohai's loyalty and determination. However, our plan depends on the outbreak of solar activities to achieve a 100% effect."

Wuying Qi said, "The cycle of the solar activities in the Empyreal Terminus Sector is fourteen years. If we miss this year, we will have to wait for fourteen years for the next best opportunity.

"Once my plan is leaked, all the four families need to do is to wait patiently for a year and a half and attack the capital from the aphelion. Then, the Gold Crystal Pyramid will be useless.

"Do you understand my concerns, Marquis Liaohai?"

"I…"

Lei Chenghu took a deep breath; his eyes slightly red. "I fully understand it!"

Right then, the Grand Illusionary Land was twisted weirdly. The vivid holograms gradually disintegrated into greenish data that flew away in the air.

"Our mobile secret communication base has been detected by the enemy. It is suffering the blanket bombardment from the four families' ace fleets."

Slightly frowning, Wuying Qi said, "It seems that the real-time communication cannot last any longer. Thankfully, I have already told everything to Marquis Liaohai frankly. Do you still have any questions, Marquis Liaohai?"

"Not at all. I hope that Your Majesty can annihilate the four ace fleets of the four families smoothly!"

Lei Chenghu's neck and his face were both flushing. Suddenly, he recalled something else and changed his expression. He said worriedly, "Right now, you must be wary of the cunning thief Li Yao. Since he knows the underground hole, he may know a thing or two about the Gold Crystal Tower.

"Considering the guy's behavior in the past, it is very likely that he will sneak into the capital and crawl below the ground to sabotage the Gold Crystal Tower.

"I have sent a lot of ships to the capital over the past few days. Considering Li Yao's combat ability in the Divinity Transformation Stage, I really cannot guarantee that he hasn't hidden on one of the starships and snuck into the capital!

"The greatest dam may be compromised by a tiny ant's nest. You must be careful, Your Majesty, in case the scoundrel ruins your cause!"

Chapter 2600: Torn Planet!

Not far away from the capital planet, in the chaotic stone belt, a mobile secret communication base moved here and there like a clumsy tortoise, trying to dodge the predators behind with the help of the flying meteorites.

However, the predators from the ace fleets of the four families were apparently much faster and more agile than the secret communication base. Very soon, they bit its tail and gnawed at its spiritual shield, swallowing the spiritual shield completely very soon.

The highly-explosive bombs that carried abundant marrow crystals fell on the secret communication base like raindrops, blowing the whole base into smithereens. Everything was knocked over and split apart in the wreath of scorching fireballs.

"Your Majesty, Your Majesty—"

Inside the secret command center in the royal palace on the capital planet, Lei Chenghu's hologram seemed to have something more to say to Wuying Qi. However, as the communication base in space collapsed, the Grand Illusionary Land that connected the two Sectors also turned into highly unstable rays of light. He extended his hands at Wuying Qi, only to become a frozen statue. Very soon, the statue turned into digital sand and fell to the ground into nothingness.

Holding his forehead while facing the empty darkness, Wuying Qi thought for a long time.

Then, his hands danced agilely, drawing hundreds of black threads, while he retrieved abundant data from the mainframe crystal processor.

There was one thing that Lei Chenghu couldn't have known.

Wuying Qi had more sources of intelligence in the Seven Seas Space Zone than Lei Chenghu.

In the Seven Seas Space Zone, the Astounding Thunder Fleet, or even "Iron Torrent", Lei Chenghu's flagship, Wuying Qi had deployed plenty of spies.

As the battles grew more and more intense in the past few days, the Spiritual Nexus in both the capital and the Seven Seas Space Zone was under severe interference. Almost all the communication of information had been blocked.

However, a few days back, when the interferences were not as strong and he still controlled a few remote communication bases, he had obtained abundant information and raw data of the command chain from the Seven Seas Space Zone.

He also had a head-on clash with Li Yao in the internal district of the Seven Seas Grand Market earlier. Therefore, his understanding of Li Yao was a hundred times more profound than Lei Chenghu.

Wuying Qi browsed through the waterfall of data. Based on the raw data a few days ago, he checked the battlefield information that Lei Chenghu sent to him today.

After checking carefully for a long time, Wuying Qi reached the conclusion that Lei Chenghu was not lying to him. The data he provided should be true.

Unless Lei Chenghu boasted super crystal processors above those in the capital, he couldn't have the massive computational ability to fabricate the massive data of the entire battle of Seven Seas.

Besides, considering the strength of the Astounding Thunder Fleet and the garrison fleets of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, it was already their best to take down Yun Xuefeng's fleet neatly when they joined hands. It was impossible for them to finish the fleet of the old cunning fox Song Yushi so soon.

Compared to the proud, tough generals like Yun Xuefeng, Song Yushi, as an experienced general, might not have a high combat ability, but he had intuitional abilities to preserve his strength.

Therefore, Lei Chenghu was still confronting Song Yushi. Without ten to fifteen days or even longer, there would not be an outcome in the battle of the two seasoned generals.

Lei Chenghu's intelligence about Li Yao should be good too. If Lei Chenghu were not completely loyal to him, it would've been unnecessary for him to reveal the incident or to remind him that Li Yao had very likely snuck to the capital or even into the underground world.

Lei Chenghu did not betray him. The only problem was Cultivator Li Yao.

"Li Yao! Li Yao! Li Yao!"

Wuying Qi turned off all the data and closed his eyes, before he rubbed his nose heavily. "Why can such a cockroach never be killed? I detonated the brain of a super Meditation Healer. Did it not wound you at all? What kind of human being are you exactly?"

He landed on the ground and paced for a while. He suddenly clapped his hands.

Two officers in the most original uniform of the Imperium's army immediately showed up in the darkness. Their temples and their foreheads were protruding high, as if they had grown horns. Their eyes were as dark as ink, and the whites were barely visible. Not many feelings of a human being could be found in them, except for their admiration and reverence for Wuying Qi.

"Double the defense of the Gold Crystal Pyramid. Move the Blood Swallow Legion and the Abyss Wolf Legion over and monitor every inch of space around the Gold Crystal Pyramid. There's still the last day. The future of the Imperium—no, the future of mankind is about to be turned to a new page. I will not allow any trouble at such a moment!"

Wuying Qi said coldly, "Pay special attention to the annoying flies and cockroaches. Do not let them crawl into the Gold Crystal Pyramid in any case!"

"Yes, Your Majesty!"

The two officers replied rigidly and turned around like a machine.

"Wait—"

When they were about to step out of the command center, Wuying Qi stopped them rather uneasily. "I'll go in person. I'm really hoping to see the look on the cockroach's face when he sees me!"

…

"Hey, what do you think the look on Wuying Qi's face will be when he sees us?"

At the same time, in the narrow gaps close to the underground hole below the capital planet, Li Yao dug forward like a hybrid of a cockroach and a marmot while he asked the two people behind him with a smile, "I believe that he will look more awful than being forced to eat s*it, right?"

Behind Li Yao, Li Jialing silently carried out his bone-shrinking techniques, almost squeezing himself into a thin piece of paper, before he managed through the gaps.

Long Yangjun, however, bulged her eyes and said, "Yes, he will definitely look very awful. However, after that, he will burst into fury and knock each of us out in one punch! What's wrong with you that makes you think you can break into Wuying Qi's most important weapon base with only the three of us? And what's wrong with me that makes me agree to go on such an adventure with you?"

"Don't put it that way. While Wuying Qi is terrifying, he is stalled by the ace fleets of the four families after all. Didn't we see the overwhelming troops of the four families when we jumped over stealthily? Wow. What a fabulous lineup it was! I'm drooling whenever I think about it. Even I couldn't hold back the urge to surrender to them."

Li Yao swallowed and said, "Judging from the mobilization of the four families' ace fleets, they should be launching a general attack at the capital planet in a few days, shouldn't they? We are taking advantage of Wuying Qi's most pressured moment to sneak in. How can we be so unlucky as to run into him in person? Chances are that our mission this time will be very simple, and we will reveal his scheme without any accident.

"Is any mission with a cursed man like you ever 'simple'?"

Long Yangjun mumbled. Her breasts squeezed by the sharp rocks, she demanded angrily, "How are you leading the way? Only earthworms and cockroaches are fit for such narrow tunnels. What kind of shortcut is this?"

"That's not my fault. The underground world of the capital planet is your turf. The map was drawn by you last time!"

Li Yao said innocently, "This tunnel was exactly the route through which we retreated after we scouted the underground hole last time. It was very spacious at that time and could allow two people to move forward shoulder by shoulder. Who knows that it will become like this in only a couple of months? This is very close to the underground hole. I fear that Wuying Qi has set up some alarm magical equipment that can detect feeble quakes, so I cannot shatter the rocks with drills. As a result, we can only squeeze forward slowly."

"That's strange."

Long Yangjun said, "By logic, there shouldn't be such drastic tectonic changes in only a few months. What happened?"

Hardly had she finished her question when intense quakes echoed around. The rocks above and below them further pressed toward them, as if they were going to close and squash the invaders.

Li Yao, Long Yangjun, and Li Jialing were all greatly shocked. They were about to summon their crystal suits and drills of mystic rays to dig a way out when the movement of rocks came to an abrupt end.

The three of them looked at each other in surprise.

"Somebody seems to have planted abundant crystal bombs in the faults of the shell and is detonating them in a pattern."

As a bomb expert, Li Yao thought for a moment and said, "They are trying to change the tectonic structure or even to tear apart the land, thereby creating a huge artificial canyon.

"It must be Wuying Qi's doing, but what does he want?"

Long Yangjun pondered for a moment and said, "Wait. Changing the tectonic structure with crystal bombs, unleashing massive energy, tearing apart the land, and creating a huge canyon… It sounds exactly like what the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God did ten thousand years ago. Does he have any nefarious purposes?"

"About that…"

Li Yao scratched his chin hard, unable to think it through yet.

"I… have an idea."

Li Jialing suddenly said, "Brother Yao and Sister Long both said that Wuying Qi has a certain powerful secret weapon deep inside the capital. However, the enemy he needs to deal with is the four families' ace fleets in space.

"In my opinion, however powerful the secret weapon is, it cannot directly annihilate the enemy in space when it is buried deep below the ground, right?

"Therefore, the first thing Wuying Qi is going to do, and what the Supreme Emperor or the Blood God did first ten thousand years ago, is to find a way to tear apart the planet so that the secret weapon may rise to the surface of the planet."

"That's right!"

Li Yao slapped his forehead and said, "That should be it! Jialing, I didn't expect you to be growing so fast. After only one year, you're almost as smart as me!"

"Change the tectonic structure, tear apart the planet, and make the secret weapon rise to the surface?"

Long Yangjun, however, shuddered in coldness. "When the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God did it ten thousand years ago, it resulted in the permanent changes of the environment on the surface of the capital planet. There's no telling how many people got killed. Today, ten thousand years later, the population of the capital planet has expanded tenfold. If the tectonic structure is changed again, leading to a global earthquake or volcano eruption, there will be no telling what kind of living hell the capital planet is going to be turned into!"
